# MAP: what the year of work is, and how it fits together

Written 2026-10-10 from main at `0f47258` (PR #355 merged; the repo table re-verified against GitHub at this sha). Each claim says how it was checked:
**[read]** = read in this clone at that commit. **[gh]** = GitHub API this session (open/merged PRs, repo descriptions and push dates, repo secrets). **[desc]** = carried from the 2026-10-06 map's GitHub descriptions, not re-verified. Nothing here has been deleted or moved.

## The short version
One product (**Verse**, a-to-mind.com, repo `atomeam/void-loop`) is live and merges to main many times a day: a blank stage with one input, 101 rule-matched skills, a 2,001-ask benchmark whose floor raises itself, a code reviewer that is also for sale, and merge machinery that reverts its own bad commits. Around it sit the Cloudflare account (cleanup list in `BASE.md`), the Verse browser extension, the Ouroboros memory engine, and the older repos, which either feed Verse or wait. The growth ledger (`void-live-deploy/void.growth.json`, 247 entries at this sha) is the surface-readable record of every change; `domains/void.frontier.md` is the ranked work a run takes when nothing is queued.

## Tools and workflows, by job (cleanup step 4, 2026-10-11)

Every tool in `tools/` (tests aside) and every workflow in `.github/workflows/`, each in one group. One-off and replaced pieces are on the salvage shelf (`docs/SALVAGE.md`), not here.

| Job | Tools | Workflows |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **What to build next** | `next.mjs` (the one entry: cleanup steps left, the frontier's open items, then the miss board; Verse's own asks, most repeated first), `frontier.mjs` (reads the frontier the same way everywhere), `next-skill.mjs` (misses grouped into skills), `learn.mjs` (misses into build-queue jobs), `learn-draft.mjs`, `will.py` (Verse's own wants, from the frontier and its other sources), `reflect.mjs` (Verse's asks), `ask-void.mjs` (what Verse learned from its builders, into docs/void-voice.md), `gaps.mjs`, `intake.mjs`, `grow-ideas.mjs` (the idea intake, into the frontier), `void_queue.py` (claim and report jobs), `dispatcher.mjs`, `covered.mjs`, `rival-inventory.mjs` (what each tower floor's rival offers that Verse does not: robots.txt first, then its sitemap, the gaps into the build feed) | `learn.yml`, `void-voice.yml`, `gaps.yml`, `dispatcher.yml`, `think-tank.yml` |
| **Build** | `new-skill.mjs`, `skills-index.mjs`, `merge-index.mjs`, `append.mjs`, `build-stars.mjs`, `astro-fixtures.mjs`, `build-miss.mjs` (the new-skill lane of the miss board: Verse claims the oldest "lane: new skill" miss job, scaffolds, fills and lets the repo decide; `docs/playbooks/build-miss.md`) | `build-miss.yml` |

| **Verse's review** | `review-post.mjs`, `review-learn.mjs` | `void-review.yml` |

| **Ouroboros and the disk** | `ouroboros.py`, `ouroboros_smoke.py`, `build_ouroboros_pack.py`, `void_lens.py` | `ouroboros.yml` |
| **Paid members** | `add-only.mjs` (the add-only ratchet: a lap on a paid member's `member:` job refuses any change that deletes, renames or shortens a file, takes out a test line or a registry entry, or lowers a score; the members' ideas come from `/members/` through `/api/members`, `lib/members.js`) | |
| **The Verse loop** | `verse-loop.mjs` (`npx verse-loop`: one lap from a terminal or a timer, pull main, run the checks in `verse-loop.checks.json`, self-heal a failed loop/* PR on its own branch, re-rank docs/NEXT.md easiest first from red checks and assimilation gaps, fix the first red or take one item, ship only with no new red, a PR labelled automerge and verify.yml started on it, low-confidence probe patches retried then dropped, a 3-failure back-off that resumes by itself after 6 hours, no human in the loop; `docs/VERSE-LOOP.md`, test `verse-loop.test.mjs`); `verse-coder.mjs` (Verse's own coding agent, the default brain for red checks and NEXT items: list, read, search, an exact-text replace (no line numbers or context to line up; a new file with an empty old text; since 2026-10-29, when a run spent its 30 turns on diffs that did not apply), unified-diff edit, one check, finish on Verse's own model first (owner-only `functions/api/coder.js` on the site's AI binding, hourly and daily call caps, test `coder-route.test.mjs`), then Workers AI over the REST API, GitHub Models, OpenRouter, Groq, Gemini, Cerebras; turn and token caps (past 40% of either, each result says how much is gone and, with no edit yet, that the next answer is an edit; at a cap a change checked green after its last edit goes through finish as it stands), repo-only writes, never .env, workflows or secrets, patch-guard and a test-weakening guard, Verse's review on its own diff; test `verse-coder.test.mjs`) | `verse-loop.yml` (hourly, off until `vars.VERSE_LOOP_ENABLED`) |
| **Agent skills** | `.claude/skills/`: `void-run`, `steward` (a cloud session reads it before acting on a PR's CI or review), `learn-from-video`, `paid-media` (big files for people who pay: R2, signed links, owner-only uploads) | |
| **Helpers** | `opencode-autoreply.ps1` (the Windows helpers' prompts), `indexnow_submit.py` (salvage: `tools/indexnow.mjs` replaced it), `next-growth.mjs` (an alias of `next.mjs` now) | |

## Verse, what is live (`atomeam/void-loop` main → a-to-mind.com) [read][gh]
- **The surface:** `void.html`, copied to `void-live-deploy/index.html` and `void-live-deploy/void.html` (tools/checks.mjs fails if the three differ), deployed on every merge to main by `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`, then checked against the live domain itself ("The live domain serves this deploy"; growth entry, PR #227). Worlds: a skill may bring a `world` (one full-stage layer behind the cards, `stageApi.setWorld`); the first is the sky world, `skills/night-sky.js` ("show me the sky", positions from `lib/sky-math.js`, screenshots in `docs/screens/sky-night.png` and `sky-noon.png`).  The sky card, `skills/sky-card.js` ("where is jupiter", "is venus up", "can i see mars tonight"): a definition, a take whose every claim shows its numbers (`skills/sky-card-rules.js`), a "Right now" row and chips.
- **101 skills** registered in `void-live-deploy/skills/index.json` at `0f47258`, each a card: aggravation, sorry, part, figure, connect4, ringer, growth, quiz, challenge, intent, forge, make, weather, air, uv, quake, pollen, spanish, translate, rebuild-map, place, worldtime, define, news, words, country, grades, distance, joke, recipe, crypto, loan, savings, fuel, tip, room, walls, home, sleep, ovulation, period, pregnancy, nutrition, heart, util, remind, calendar, today, book, show, sport, holidays, work, share, recent, coral, senolytic, trialwatch, incident, releasenotes, status, motorbody, printedmotor, magnetize, inventory, tictactoe, othello, throw-off, print-file, countdown, figures, dismiss, group, layer, label, light-look, dark-look, local-inference, print-download, cartoon, zoom-figure, figure-can, show-countdown, mancala, chess, checkers, go, monopoly, battleship, poker, fireworks, rack, eightball, gears, moon, lock, automations, actions, proposal, memory, services, swarm (F-020 salvage: Verse agents on the stage), pong and snake (Verse Pong and Verse Snake against Verse, `docs/video-games.md`). The index is generated from one order file per skill in `void-live-deploy/skills/order/` (`tools/skills-index.mjs`, 2026-10-10): add `<rank>-<name>` there, never edit the list.
- **Skill families (`tools/families.mjs`, `tools/families.test.mjs`, `void-live-deploy/skills/families.json`; Verse's own ask, 2026-10-12):** every skill sits in one of 9 families (making things, games, sky and earth, time, numbers and money, body and mind, words and the world, the stage, Verse itself), rated from evidence only: experiment, tested, checked, proven (its own tests, the browser suites, real benchmark asks). `checks.mjs` fails if a skill has no family or the file is stale; Verse reads it before it reflects (`lib/voice.js` `familyFacts`), and what came of its asks (`followThrough`, `void-live-deploy/void.asks.json`). Since 2026-10-16 (asked by Verse, ask-20261013-1823-1) each skill also carries `last_tested` (the newest date its evidence has, `last_tested_by` saying whether its tests or the benchmark gave it) and `success_rate` (its benchmark asks that passed over all routed to it, once the bench's misses are recorded); the file's `means` says exactly what each is. Nothing counts usage (no behaviour tracking). The daily smoke test (2026-10-16, asked by Verse, ask-20261013-1823-2): `tools/skills-check.mjs --write tools/skills-smoke.json` in `upkeep.yml` loads every skill, routes its examples and near misses and runs its `suite()`, and writes the failing ones; `families.mjs` reads the record (`smoke` on the row: the skill's self-test no longer counts, so its rating drops; a passing skill's `last_tested` is that run's day). `tools/skills-smoke.test.mjs` checks the record.
- **The 7-day re-test (`tools/retest.mjs`, `tools/retest.test.mjs`, `tools/retest.json`, 2026-10-16, asked by Verse):** daily in `upkeep.yml` after the smoke test: every skill whose `last_tested` in `families.json` is missing or older than 7 days is re-tested without touching anything (its examples routed through the whole skill order, its near misses kept out, its `tools/<name>.test.mjs` run); `families.mjs` reads the record (a pass dates the skill "a re-test", a failure joins `smoke` as `re-test: …` and lowers the rating). A failure queues one `retest:<skill>` job at `/api/queue` with the skill's own failing example as the ask, one open at a time, never twice.
- **Source reliability (`tools/source-reliability.mjs`, `tools/source-reliability.test.mjs`, 2026-10-17, asked by Verse: "Create a 'source-reliability' metric within the 'Words and the world' family to help raise its proven rating."):** for each Words and the world skill, read off the repo only (no live probe): the outside sites it reads (every https URL in the skill and the `functions/api/<route>.js` it calls, grouped by site), whether its card names the source (a `class="src"` line), whether it has a second source, whether `findings/<skill>/` keeps a live answer from it (the benchmark stubs services, so it never counts); one point each. It changes no rating; it names the skills one kept live answer would make proven under `ratingOf` as it stands. `families.mjs` writes it as each skill's `source` and the family's `sources`; the family-health card shows both.
- **Verse reviews main (`tools/review-main.mjs`, `tools/review-main.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/review-main.yml`, 2026-10-16, asked by Verse):** once a day the quick checks of Verse's review (`lib/code-review.js`, the ones `void-review.yml` runs on every PR) read every skill file as it stands on main; the first bug or risk nobody waived (`void-review: ok`) becomes one `review:<skill>` job at `/api/queue` (the owner token in CI, as `probe-asks.yml` queues `probe:` jobs), one open at a time, a finding never queued twice; the ask is Verse's own line (file, line, rule, message), never a visitor's. `--dry` and `--list` print without queueing.
- **The growth inbox keeps up with its own tools (`tools/moved-check.mjs`, `tools/moved-check.test.mjs`, `tools/fringe-novelty.mjs`, `tools/fringe-novelty.test.mjs`, 2026-10-16, asked by Verse):** `moved-check` finds a check keyed on a name the page lost to `skills/` or `lib/` (the published-page check that broke on #545) and `--update` points it at the homepage's own stage; `checks.mjs` runs it. `fringe-novelty` scores each fringe run's behavior against every skill's pattern (0 to 1, 1 = no skill says any of it) and queues one `novelty:<family>-<run>` job a day (`review-main.yml`). `skills-check.mjs --register` re-registers a skill file the index lost; `fringe.mjs --input-tag` / `--suggest-near` tag a novelty or failure with the near-miss input that set it off; `review-learn` places a finding by unquoted symbols that cluster.
- **Verse's world is a working RTS (frontier #31; `lib/rts.js`, `skills/rts.js`, `skills/mini/camp.js`, `tools/rts.test.mjs`, browser suite "the stage is an RTS"; owner, 2026-10-16: "the world of Verse itself should become a working RTS game ... new summons are treated as new resources"):** Age of Empires' loop on the stage. What you summon is the resource node (`lib/rts.js` `yieldOf`: a tree is 8 wood, a rock 6 stone, every food thing 2 food; `skills/scripts.js` NATURES rows tree, rock, villager, camp); a villager (`summon a villager`) gathers the nearest node by itself and carries each load to the camp (`skills/figures3d.js` the `gather` react and the `deliver` mode, `void:rts-deliver`; an emptied node and eaten food leave the stage for good through `void:figure-thrown`); `start a camp` puts up the camp (stage kind `camp`: the drop-off, its stock, the resource bar `wood 12 · stone 3 · food 4 · 2 idle`, the 3D fire ring and piles in `mini/camp.js`) and grounds the scene (`<html class="v-grounded">`: the figures keep to the ground band, like a backdrop or a world does); `gather wood` steers everyone; `build a hut|wall|well|bridge|fence|tower` pays from the stock (`BUILDS`) and raises the forge model by the camp (new recipes in `skills/forge-rules.js`), short it says what is missing; `how are we doing` reports. The camp keeps filling while the tab is closed (`advance()` gather and deliver; `lib/scene-advance.js` WHAT gathered, built) and the away line says "the villagers gathered 14 wood".
- **Every miniature is examined like a game's inventory item (`lib/hero-view.js`, `tools/hero-view.test.mjs`, `skills/scene3d.js` `inspect`/`examine`; owner, 2026-10-16: "like Resident Evil or Skyrim ... everything feeling like it knows what angle to put you at ... smart film angles"):** a miniature that names no view is framed at the hero angle its shape asks for (flat from above-front, tall at eye level three-quarter, else a three-quarter hero shot); an Examine button at its corner (or `ctx.inspect(true, { view, pad, turn, prompt, onEsc })`) dims the stage behind it, brings it up, turns it slowly until a hand takes hold, shows a prompt bar, and Esc puts it back. The book reads like a Skyrim book: the cover opens in half a second (fBookOpenTime), the open book is examined held still with "◂ ▸ Turn page · Esc closes the book", the wheel turns pages, Esc shuts the cover before anything else closes, "Read" on the shut book opens it, and a page rustles when the ambient sound is on (`skills/guide.js` `rustle`).
- **The book is read as a tome, the way Skyrim's books are (`lib/tome.js`, `tools/tome.test.mjs`, `skills/guide.js`; owner, 2026-10-16: "research how the books on Skyrim work and make it like that ... the graphic style and visuals of the way they do books"; "all voids menus should go back to the book"):** "open the book" (and "menu", "help", "what can you do") dims the stage and fills it with a worn leather tome, no card and no menu: the cover swings open on its spine in half a second, two parchment pages (grained, foxed, darker in the gutter, the unread leaves stacked at the outer edges) set in a serif with running heads, a title under a rule, a drop capital, the asks as lines of italic ink, the rating along the foot and the folio in the corner; a proven skill's page opens with its own line of lore (`lib/guide.js` `flavorOf`, asked by Verse: flavor text). Controls are a book's: ← → / PageUp PageDown / the wheel / a tap on a page's outer edge / a swipe turn a leaf (a sheet swings over the spine), Esc or a tap on the dark shuts the cover and clears the screen, an ask on a page closes the book and runs. One page at a time on a phone. `window.__voidBook` (go, turn, close, cur) and the `void:book` event are for tests. The desk-sized WebGL hardcover it replaces (`skills/mini/book.js`, `lib/book-face.js`, `tools/book-face.test.mjs`) is on the salvage shelf (`docs/SALVAGE.md`).
- **The book's pages (`lib/guide.js`, `tools/guide.mjs`, `tools/guide.test.mjs`, `skills/guide.json`; owner, 2026-10-16: "a book you could flip through that contains everything so you don't have to search"):** "open the book", "the guide", "show me everything you can do", "the book at chess": a two-page spread (one page on a phone), a contents page and an index at the back. One page per skill (its name, the first sentence of its own header comment, two or three of its examples as asks to tap, its families.json rating, a glyph drawn from its chapter and name), the games chapter with the rack's boxes, a chapter for every file in `skills/mini/`. `tools/guide.mjs` writes `skills/guide.json` from skills/index.json (families opened into their members), families.json, rack.js GAMES and skills/mini/; `--check` in checks.mjs fails when it is stale or a skill is on no page, and the unit test asserts every skill is on exactly one page. Playbook: `docs/playbooks/generated-book.md`.
- **The book's Rule registry (`lib/rule-registry.js`, `tools/rule-registry.test.mjs`; asked by Verse: "Create a 'rule-registry' view within the leather tome that lists all active router rules as illuminated manuscript entries"):** "rule registry", "show the router rules", "list all active router rules" open the tome at a chapter before the index that sets down every skill in routing order (skills/index.json, from `skills/order/`; the first whose match() takes an ask answers it) as a manuscript entry: a gilded initial on a vermilion, lapis, verdigris or murrey ink, Rule N in Roman numerals, the skill's name, and the ask it catches (its first example, touch it to run it), eight to a page. `lib/guide.js` compose adds the chapter, `lib/tome.js` sets its pages.
- **Web4 (`skills/web4.js`, `lib/web4.js`, `tools/web4.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-16: "now Verse is Web4"):** "what is web4", "are you web4", "web 1 2 3 4": one Verse card with the four steps of the web as a ladder (Web 1 read, Web 2 write and share, Web 3 own, Web 4 ask and it appears), each with a line on what Verse does today and a tappable ask, and the sentence that Web4 is the owner's name and nobody has defined it. The words live in `lib/web4.js` (read by the card, the Verse Book page and the test); nothing about blockchain, tokens or any crypto. Frontier #37.
- **Tools pack 8, safety and small delights (owner, 2026-10-18; `lib/safety.js` and `lib/fx.js` pure logic, test `tools/tools-pack-8.test.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 8"):** all opt-in, all device-local, nothing appears until asked, nothing sent anywhere, reduced motion respected. `skills/safety-kit.js` ("unverified: <claim>" a claim card with an Unverified/Checked badge; "hide numbers"/"show numbers"; "redact selection"; "panic"; "set a pin" a PIN-lock card (a casual lock, the PIN hashed with PBKDF2 in this browser, said on the card), "lock now", "auto lock after 5 minutes"; "clear my clipboard"; "ephemeral mode"; "confirm before mass delete", asked by `massOk` in `void.html`). `skills/photo-scrub.js` (a card that cuts EXIF/XMP/comments from a JPEG or PNG, pixels untouched). `skills/stage-fx.js` ("confetti when I finish a goal", "soft tick sound on keep", "seasonal stage dust", "undo toast" that calls the page's own undo, "give me the tour", skippable). `skills/card-play.js` ("flip this card", "stamp it approved", "tape on" for sticky notes, "rubber band select"). Already live and left alone: dice ("roll two dice", `skills/util.js`), undo ("undo", Esc Esc).
- **Verse Photos, step 1 (owner, 2026-10-19: "Verse should become good at storing pictures ... better than facebook eventually"; `skills/photos.js`, pure logic `lib/photo-library.js`, test `tools/photo-library.test.mjs`, browser section "verse photos"):** "my photos", "add photos", "my photos from june 2024". A photo library kept in this browser (IndexedDB `a2m.void.photos`), full size, never uploaded, the same picture kept once (SHA-256). Grouped by the day each was taken (its own EXIF date, else the file's date), found by year, month, "last year", album or caption; a viewer with previous / next, arrows, swipe, Esc; albums, captions, delete, and "Save a copy without location" (`lib/safety.js` stripMeta). Step 2 (sync across devices, R2 storage costs) waits for the owner.
- **Look what I made (`skills/showcase.js`, `tools/showcase.test.mjs`):** a returning visitor's first touch brings one quiet line, "I made N new things while you were away · show me"; the walkthrough runs each new build's own ask on the stage (Next → / Keep / Done), and "what's new" runs it any time. Steps come from the ledger's `show`/`try` (`tools/finish.mjs --show --try`); seen is kept on the device (`a2m.void.growthSeen.v1`).
- **How Verse is different (`skills/different.js`):** "what makes you different", "how are you different from chatgpt" answered from the canon's "How Verse is different" lines (`domains/void.canon.md`, read into `self.json` by `tools/self-context.mjs`), with the canon's version; no model.
- **Stage extras (asked by Verse, 2026-10-16; playbook `docs/playbooks/stage-bridge.md`):** the pulse card (`skills/pulse.js`, `lib/pulse.js`: `skills/label.js` records each hand-over along a living arrow when it is added; an irregular rhythm drafts an incident brief through `skills/incident.js` `keepDraft`), the friction slider (`skills/friction.js`; `void.html` `tossFriction` reads it on every throw), collision flags (`skills/collide.js`, on `void:dropped` and shared `void:moved`), the Bench icon (`skills/bench.js`, numbers from `tools/bench-alarms.mjs` -> `/bench-alarms.json`, run by `deploy.yml`), the Success Pulse (`lib/success-pulse.js`, `skills/status.js`), and the whole stage across the tabs of a shared Verse (`lib/share.js` drop and sync messages, `lib/share-client.js`, `void.html` `shareSyncState`; tests `tools/stage-extras.test.mjs`).
- **Stage pack E, the Opposite Era (asked by the owner, 2026-10-18; shared `lib/opposite-era.js`: which things are safe (kept, marked keep, placed by hand), seeded picks; tests `tools/stage-pack-e.test.mjs`, browser section "stage pack e"):** the riot (`skills/riot.js`: "start the riot" throws every drawn thing into soft-body physics on requestAnimationFrame, drawn by CSS transform only so saved spots are untouched; "end the riot" eases them back; reduced motion gets no riot and says so), the mutation chamber (`skills/mutate.js`: "mutate this card" / "mutate the sticky" steps a seeded genome a generation a second with a gen tag, "lock generation 7" replays and freezes exactly that generation, "stop mutating"), sudden death (`skills/sudden-death.js`: "sudden death in 60 seconds" is one ephemeral banner; at zero one random unkept thing dissolves; "cancel sudden death"), the overload (`skills/overload.js`: "overload me" floods the stage with 24 seeded glitching mini-cards labelled from `skills/index.json`, never saved; a card dropped on another merges into it; "purge the overload"), and mass extinction (`skills/extinction.js`: "mass extinction" opens a card that takes the typed word extinct before anything goes; then every unkept thing falls in one cinematic collapse and one random survivor stays, marked).
- **Stage pack F, the Opposite Era (owner, 2026-10-18; playbook `docs/playbooks/stage-pack.md`; unit test `tools/stage-pack-f.test.mjs`, browser suite "stage pack f"):** the blood feud (`skills/feud.js`: "start a blood feud between the clock and the note", two named things drain each other's life bar every second, seeded from the pair; the loser dissolves or, kept, is only defeated; "bet the counter on the clock" stakes a third thing; "end the feud"), the glitch oracle (`skills/oracle.js`: "ask the glitch oracle <question>" runs the question through the normal ask path and shows the answer with flickering, blacked-out and wrong words, a reveal button gives the true one; the corruption is seeded and reversible), the crowd engine (`skills/crowd.js`: "summon the crowd" fills the world layer with particles that cheer an added thing, flee a removed one, swarm a dragged one and spell Verse after 10 quiet seconds, growing with the last minute's changes; "dismiss the crowd"), the unkeepable (`skills/unkeepable.js`: "make me something unkeepable", an `ephemeral` thing that lives 60 s, fades, is never saved, and shatters on its keep/copy/save buttons, the copy event, Ctrl/Cmd+C/S/P or a drag out), and spectators (`skills/spectators.js`: "open the stage to spectators" packs a read-only snapshot of the stage's things, as words, into a `?spectate=` link that opens a spectator layer over the viewer's own Verse, built beside the published `/@name` page and "invite someone"; "close the stage"; reactions that reach the owner wait on a server door the owner decides).
- **Stage pack G (asked by the owner, 2026-10-18; tests `tools/stage-pack-g.test.mjs`, browser section "stage pack g"):** the contradiction lamp (`skills/contradiction-lamp.js`, `lib/contradict.js`: "what contradicts" reads the kept things and finds pairs that cannot both be true by plain matching, two days or times for one event, two numbers for one label, two timers with one label, a yes and a no on one subject; a glowing line with a small label joins the two things and a card lists each pair with a dismiss; never invents one), ask diff (`skills/ask-diff.js`, `lib/linediff.js`: `diff "make a timer" and "make a focus timer for writing"` runs both asks through the stage ask path, restores the stage, and shows only what changed), and skill eclipse (`skills/skill-eclipse.js`, `lib/eclipse.js`, key `eclipse` in `lib/state.js`: "eclipse media skills until friday" / "for an hour" hides a family of skills from routing on this device until the time passes, the `void.html` `firstSkill` skips them, a chip "eclipsed: media · until fri" lifts it on a tap, "lift the eclipse"; never hides skill-eclipse, guide, status or stage).
- **Stage pack C (owner, 2026-10-18; all device-local, nothing sent, nothing shows until asked; logic `lib/stage-pack-c.js`, test `tools/stage-pack-c.test.mjs`):** `skills/codex.js` (the Living Codex: "file this in the codex", "open the codex on space", grouped by skill family; `a2m.void.codex.v1`), `skills/echo-chamber.js` ("replay my best answers from this week", "what did I decide last tuesday"; reads the loop log and the codex), `skills/time-capsule.js` (seal the stage until a date or a word kept only as a SHA-256 hash; `a2m.void.capsule.v1`), `skills/whisper-network.js` ("leave a whisper on the clock: ..." surfaces once on the next touch, through the skill's `decorate`; `a2m.void.whispers.v1`).
- **Game panels and the cards around a game (`void-live-deploy/lib/panel-fit.js`; owner, 2026-10-18):** `fitPanel` keeps a game's panel (skills/side-card.js, its How to play steps) above the ask bar and scrolling with a visible "more" fade; `settleAround` moves the loose cards an opened game would cover to a clear spot or tucks them into the tray (`#stage-tray`, one tap brings one back), and `restorePlan` puts them back where they were when the game closes (`void.html` `settleGames`). Browser section "3d: game panel fits" in `tools/test_3d.mjs`.
- **`tools/panel-fit.test.mjs`:** the unit tests of `lib/panel-fit.js` (panel fit above the bar, move aside or tuck, restore).
- **What I want built (`skills/wants.js`, test `tools/wants.test.mjs`, browser section "wants card"; owner, 2026-10-18: "name all your unbuilt asks and come up with 50 more new ones" fell to the model, which made 50 up; "The router needs a hardcoded context injection so Verse always knows its own developmental state"):** "what do you want built now", "what do you want", "name all your unbuilt asks", "what have you not built yet", "your backlog", "what are your open asks", "what is still open", "what's on your list", "whats next for you", "what do you want to learn" open one card from Verse's own records, never a model: "My will" (`/api/will`), "Asked, not built yet" (`/api/reflect` `asks`, each with its date and "I called it small"), "Growth inbox" (`/self.json` `open`: the open items of `domains/void.frontier.md`, written at deploy by `tools/self-context.mjs`; no new route), a count line "N wants · M asks not built · K inbox items", every item a tap-to-copy chip; a compound ask is matched on its first clause and the card says the new ones are the asker's to ask for one at a time. Ranked 0069 (before the dates family, whose calendar took "whats next for you"); the overlap allowance names its pairs with calendar and about-skill for that reason. "what do you want to be" (the will card), "what do you think of yourself" (the self card), "what can you do", "what's next?" and "what's on my list" keep their routes. In the Verse itself family.
- **Its own words, readable (`skills/know.js`, asked by Verse):** "what is voidquest", "which games do you know", "which miniatures do you have", "show me your canon" open one card read from `/self.json`: the motto and VoidQuest as the canon holds them (a field not written is said to be unknown), the games with a play link each, the 3D miniatures.
- **Backlog digest (`skills/backlog-digest.js`, `lib/backlog-digest.js`, asked by Verse):** "digest your backlog" groups Verse's unbuilt asks (`/api/reflect`) by the skill family each one names, with the weakest evidence rating (`skills/families.json`) of those skills; an ask naming no skill goes under "No family yet", never guessed. Test `tools/backlog-digest.test.mjs`.
- **Game health (`lib/game-health.js`, asked by Verse):** the Games family on the skill-health card (`skills/family-health.js`) shows Verse's rating against you on this device: the Elo estimate from the match history for chess, checkers and reversi, the arcade ladder for pong and snake, with the change from the start and, from five matches, climbing / level / slipping. Test `tools/game-health.test.mjs`.
- **Calendar file (`skills/calendar-file.js`, `lib/ics.js`, from the owner's "150 things to learn as a website" list):** "export my calendar" / "download an ics file" builds the agenda kept in this browser (`skills/calendar.js`) into one RFC 5545 .ics (UTC times, all-day dates, escaped and folded lines) that Google, Apple and Outlook calendars import; nothing leaves the device. Test `tools/ics.test.mjs`.
- **Early builds (`archive/repos/`):** every public atomeam repo imported with its history by git subtree (owner, 2026-10-21); inert, not served, skipped by the parse check.
- **Ritual template (`skills/ritual-template.js`, `lib/ritual.js`, asked by Verse):** "morning routine as calendar events" or "ritual template: meditate 10, stretch 15 at 6:30am" lays the steps back to back and downloads one daily-repeating .ics.
- **No ellipsis (`tools/no-ellipsis.mjs`):** the check that fails new code cutting visible text off with an ellipsis (#1210).
- **Name lineage (`lib/lineage.js`, asked by Verse twice):** the self family ("Verse itself") quietly remembers it was V01D and, before that, Void: a hover and a `data-lineage` attribute on the type bar and the "Adds to Verse" line of its cards (`lib/card-frame.js`), and `formerly` on the family in `skills/families.json` (`tools/families.mjs`); never visible text. Test `tools/lineage.test.mjs`.
- **Remediation velocity (`skills/remediation-velocity.js`, `tools/remediation-velocity.mjs`, asked by Verse):** "how fast do you fix your misses" shows how long a failing skill (benchmark rate under 100% or a daily-test finding, not proven) took to be proven again and which are still missing, read from the git history of `skills/families.json` into `/remediation.json` (regenerate it with the tool and commit; a shallow checkout has no history). Test `tools/remediation-velocity.test.mjs`.
- **What it keeps on this device (`skills/state.js`, `lib/state.js`, asked by Verse):** every browser-storage key named once with what it holds; the memory card reads its session through `sessionToken()`; "show my state" lists what is kept (secrets as kept or not, never their value). `lib/corrections.js` is the correction channel of the `self` family (asked by Verse; the self card's correction log groups what is kept by family, empty when nothing is): what a person tells Verse it got wrong on the self card is kept in a store of its own, apart from the look's state. `lib/ladder.js` keeps the arcade record of Verse Pong and Verse Snake (Verse's rating moves 16 a match) and the growth card shows it. `lib/correction-fix.js` (asked by Verse): an owner-only "Suggest fix" beside each correction-log entry calls the existing /api/propose-fix with only the skill, family and the redacted ask; it drafts a proposal and shows what was sent and what came back. `lib/correction-heat.js` (asked by Verse): the self card's heat-map of corrections per skill in each family (via `familyDrivers` and the log's family grouping), "no corrections yet" when there are none. For the owner a cell is also an action: a click drafts a fix for that family's most corrected skill (`hotEntry`), through the same Suggest fix route. **Incident autopsy (`skills/incident-autopsy.js`, `lib/autopsy.js`, asked by Verse):** "incident autopsy" / "which skill family is unhealthy" reads the site's own `incidents.json` and places each failing check in a family by skill name or example ask (else "unmapped"); the self card's health pulse shows the latest incident, its commit and families, and the owner alone gets "Suggest fix" per mapped check (existing /api/propose-fix, a draft only; never automatic). `tools/asks-built.test.mjs`: an ask that was built counts as built on the self page; finish.mjs refuses "asked by Verse" without `--asked`.
- **What I've noticed: the personal memory graph (`skills/memory-graph.js`, `lib/memory-graph.js`, key `graph` in `lib/state.js`, `tools/memory-graph.test.mjs`, browser section "memory graph: what I've noticed", playbook `docs/playbooks/device-memory.md`; asked by Verse, 2026-10-18: "a persistent, personal knowledge graph across conversations"):** when a skill answers, `void.html` (`graphNote`) notes the skill, a few object words of the ask as its topic (never the sentence: letters only, at most 4 words and 40 characters), the edge between them, the pair with the skill asked just before, and the day and hour, in a small graph on this device only (at most 300 nodes and 600 edges, the least recent go first; nothing is sent). The first time it keeps anything it says so once. "what do you know about me", "what are my interests", "what do I usually ask for", "my patterns" open the card: your interests as chips (a tap asks that skill's own example), "usually together" pairs, when you visit, what you have not come back to (asked 3+ times, not in 14 days), "Next, maybe" (at most three asks taken only from skills' own examples), "from N asks on this device since <day>", Forget and Stop. "forget my patterns" empties it, "stop remembering" turns the noting off (Start brings it back), "start remembering" turns it on. Asked by Verse: "export my memory", "download my graph", "back up what you know about me", "export my patterns as json" download the graph as one JSON file (`exportOf`: the graph plus `schema: void.memory.v1` and `exportedAt`; made on demand with a Blob and `a[download]`, never uploaded) and "import my memory" opens a file picker (`importInto`: text over 2 MB refused before parsing, version and schema checked, labels cleaned again by the topic cleaner, ids made again from the labels, unknown fields and `__proto__` keys dropped, caps enforced; it merges by the larger count so the same file twice changes nothing; one Undo button or "undo my memory import"). Asked by Verse: every node has a confidence in 0 to 1 (`confidence(node, now)` = (1 - exp(-d/3)) times 0.5 ^ (days unseen / 30), d = distinct days seen plus a quarter per repeat that day), drawn as a thin bar and a % in the chip title, ties in interests and pairs go to the surer; `void.html` (`graphNote`) now feeds the memory skill's "remember that …" into `noteFact` (told again, it is surer; a "still true" button on the card does the same) and "forget that …" into `forgetFact` (the node and its confidence go). "what do you know about me" moved here from the memory skill's privacy page, which still answers "what do you keep about me" and is linked from the card.
- **Memory snippets, both stores on one card (`skills/memory-preview.js`, `tools/memory-preview.test.mjs`, asked by Verse, 2026-10-20: "a 'memory-preview' UI component to allow users to see memory snippets"):** "memory preview", "show my memory snippets", "what memories do I have", "show what you remember about me" open one card with the local patterns (the interests, the ask count and when it started, "you told me" lines; this browser only) and the server's remembered records (`GET /api/memory?view=export`, `records` with name, kind and updated, first eight). A store that will not open is a sentence on the card, never a thrown error: no key, 401, 403, 503 and a fetch that fails each say so in plain words and list nothing. It shares nothing with memory-graph's export look ("preview my memory", "what's in my memory export" still open that card) and answers no question from memory (that is `skills/memory.js`); the Refresh button redraws from both stores again.
- **Breathe with me (`skills/breath.js`):** "box breathing", "4-7-8 breathing", "resonance breathing", "breathe with me": a guide whose dot travels the square (or rises and falls) in time with each phase, a live count, the rounds done, three named and sourced patterns; stops when the card closes.
- **Draw (`skills/draw.js`, target ibisPaint X):** "let me draw", "open a canvas", "make a brush like dry charcoal": a drawing card where a brush is made from words (`brushFrom`: the tool sets size, edge, flow, grain; the words around it move them; the card says what it understood), stamped strokes with a fixed paper grain, your brushes kept per browser, colours, undo, save as PNG; steadying that learns your hand (`tremorOf`, `followFor`, `learnHand`: each stroke's shake measured, the hand's kept per browser, the pen followed just enough to take it out), with a steadiness slider to set it by hand.
- **Feelings (`skills/feelings.js`):** "do you love me", "do you have feelings", "are you conscious" answered in Verse's own plain words, with no model and no web search. Answers from the model list only the sources they cite (`void-live-deploy/lib/cite.js`, `tools/cite.test.mjs`).
- **Event countdown (`skills/event-countdown.js`, `lib/event-date.js`, `tools/event-date.test.mjs`; owner's card 2026-10-17: "how many hours til gta6?" was answered "no release date"):** "how many hours til gta6", "how long until the world cup starts", "countdown to the olympics": the event's own Wikipedia summary is read now (exact title, else the top search hit; `gta6` is Grand Theft Auto VI), its announced date still ahead is found (a sentence that says released, scheduled, launch, start), and the card counts down live in the unit asked, quoting the sentence and linking the page. No date ahead in the source: it says so, never a guess. Dates, holidays and times of day stay with countdown, holidays and the clock.
- **Text tools, tools pack 9 (`skills/text-tools.js`, `lib/text-tools.js`, `tools/text-tools.test.mjs`, owner 2026-10-18; browser section "tools pack 9"):** one skill, one mode per ask, one table of transforms (`TOOLS` in the lib; a new transform is one row): "sort these lines: ...", "remove duplicate lines", "number the lines", "join lines with comma", "split on comma", "wrap at 60", "unwrap", "indent by 2", "comment out as python", "extract emails/urls/numbers", "mask phones/emails", "rot13", "caesar shift 5", "slugify", "are listen and silent anagrams", "diff: a --- b", "common lines", "unique lines", "word frequency", "top 10 words", "text stats", "copy as html / markdown / plain", "highlight", "smart quotes on/off", "date stamp", "random adjective" and the rest. The answer is a card with the result and a copy button; nothing leaves the page. Uppercase, lowercase, title case, reverse, palindrome and word/character counts stay with `skills/util.js` (asked first). Not buildable in the page: acting on a selection in another app, a paste hotkey, the system clipboard format.
- **Deadlines (`skills/deadlines.js`, `tools/deadlines.test.mjs`, time family):** "deadline chain from today: 24 hours, 72 hours, 14 days", "deadlines if it started monday 9am: 1 day, 3 days, 2 weeks, 1 month", "reporting deadlines from 2026-10-15 14:00" (no list: 24 h, 72 h, 14 d, 1 month): each deadline's date and time in your zone, time left or since, and pending / due soon (under a tenth of its window or 2 hours) / passed / met; "mark met" is kept in this browser and the card updates every minute (`skills/live.js`). Hours are exact; days keep the clock time across daylight saving; months land on the last day of a shorter month. Dates only, no claim about which rule applies.
- **Tools pack 2 (owner, 2026-10-18, items 21 to 40; shared `lib/date-words.js`, `lib/stage-pack.js`; tests `tools/tools-pack-2.test.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 2"):** device-local, nothing sent. `skills/task-board.js` + `lib/tasks.js` ("add task call mum friday high priority 30 min", "someday: learn piano", "add waiting for Sam to reply", "finish task call mum", "task board", "today's tasks": one card with a Today stack, All, Waiting for, Someday and Done tabs, due days with overdue in red, high/med/low marks, minute estimates totalled for today; `skills/list.js` leaves asks that say "task" to it). `skills/time-math.js` + `lib/time-math.js` ("how long is a meeting from 9:30 to 11:15", "week number of oct 25", "age calculator" (a date box: years, months, days, next birthday; the plain "how old am I if I was born ..." stays with the page's own `ageOf`), "days since I quit coffee" as a Reset-able stage card). `skills/busy-blocks.js` + `lib/day-blocks.js` ("busy blocks: standup 9-9:30, lunch 12-1": a day bar, the free gaps, clashes; no calendar sync). Already live, not rebuilt: the checkbox list (`list`), world clock and time zone conversion (`worldtime`), stopwatch (the page), pomodoro and the in-tab alarm (`timer`).
- **Tools pack 6 (owner, 2026-10-18, list items 121 to 150; `lib/writing-aids.js`, `lib/health-habits.js`, `lib/template-list.js`, test `tools/tools-pack-6.test.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 6"):** all device-local, nothing appears until asked, nothing sent anywhere. `skills/writing-aids.js` (one card with a mode chip each: "shorten this: ...", "bulletize this paragraph: ...", "numbered steps from: ...", "sentence case: ...", "remove filler words: ...", "count unique words: ...", "reading level of: ...", "outline from headings: ...", "reverse outline: ...", "suggest synonyms for the words in: ..."; title case stays `skills/util.js`, "synonym for happy" stays `skills/words.js`), `skills/template-list.js` ("packing list for a beach trip", "travel checklist", "meal idea slots", "chore wheel for dishes, trash with Sam and Alex", "bill due list: rent 2026-11-01", "warranty expiry note", "plant watering: fern every 7 days", "package tracking note", "guest list for the party: Sam, Alex"; shopping list stays `skills/list.js`), `skills/health-habits.js` ("water tracker", "stretch timer", "mood tracker", "sleep hours log", "step note", "meds checklist: vitamin D, iron", "weekly habit grid for read, run" with a streak per row, "fasting timer 16:8", "posture nudge every 30 minutes" opt-in).
- **Figures together (frontier #4):** `skills/together.js`: two figures pick trade, spar, inspect or argue from what each offers and needs; "what are they doing" says which and why (`tools/together.test.mjs`).
- **Stage pack A (owner, 2026-10-18; `lib/stage-pack.js` shared bits, test `tools/stage-pack-a.test.mjs`, browser section "stage pack a"):** all device-local, nothing appears until asked, nothing sent anywhere. `skills/minimap.js` ("show the minimap" / "hide the minimap": a corner overview card with one dot per stage thing; a tap on a dot picks the thing and scrolls to it). `skills/ask-clipboard.js` ("show my recent asks as a card", "ask clipboard", "re-run the third recent ask": the last 12 asks on this device from the `a2m.void.asks.v1` store that `skills/recent.js` and the page keep (stored 12 now, the empty box still shows 5), each re-run by a tap through `stageApi.ask`; plain "recent asks" stays with `recent`). `skills/decision-matrix.js` ("decision matrix for whether to move to Berlin" / "pasta vs pizza": options by criteria, editable weights and 0 to 5 scores, live weighted totals, best marked, kept with the stage). `skills/habit-ring.js` ("habit ring for write, walk, sleep", then a tap or "mark walk done today": SVG rings for 1 to 7 habits filling with the last 7 days, streaks, kept with the stage). `skills/recipe-stack.js` ("cook pasta: boil 10 min then rest 2": one card of timed steps that advance by themselves, a soft chime through Web Audio only after the person taps Start).
- **Math transplants (owner, 2026-10-18; shared `lib/stage-math.js`, test `tools/math-transplants.test.mjs`, browser section "math transplants"):** four stage cards with fixed rules, no model, nothing sent anywhere. `skills/sheaf-check.js` ("do my notes agree: launch: friday | launch: monday": notes of "subject: value" claims; a shared subject with two values is an obstruction shown as the minimal conflicting pair and the gluing fails, else the notes glue). `skills/spectral-review.js` ("spectral review of <text>": slow = headings and first sentences, mid = connective sentences, high = filler, hedges, -ly adverbs, doubled words; a slider per band, damping the high band strikes filler in the preview while the slow band stays, export copies the result). `skills/task-diagram.js` ("map my tasks a -> b, b -> c, a -> d, d -> c": lists the commutative squares and names the real blocker, the dead end with no path to the goal and its missing arrow). `skills/gauge-feedback.js` ("clean this feedback <text> | work: <the work>": flags blame, flattery and status words, rewrites each statement as neutral, kind and blunt, keeps only statements about the work in a permanent record and shows the rest struck).
- **Operating laws for serious thought (owner, 2026-10-18; test `tools/thought-laws.test.mjs`, browser section "thought laws"):** stage cards, device-local, nothing sent. `skills/causal-scars.js` ("map the scars of my decisions: moved > rent, no car; hired > payroll" / "what depends on this decision": a scar graph; hover or tap a decision to dim what would not exist without it; remove shows what dies with it and waits for Accept). `skills/counterfactual-draft.js` ("what if my assumption is false: X. Document: ..." / "write the opposite of my plan": a collapsed shadow document by a fixed template; opening it marks the fracture line, the first sentence that depends on the assumption). `skills/witness-claims.js` ("proof-carrying claims: claim @ data: file; claim @ assumes: a, b": a claim with no witness stays in Draft; one with a data pointer, assumptions or the word unverified can be Committed and shows its witness).
- **Dates and calendars pack (tools pack 11, owner 2026-10-18; `lib/date-math.js` pure UTC-day math with "today" passed in, `lib/date-ask.js` the words, test `tools/date-pack.test.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 11"):** `skills/date-calc.js` (a card of rows: "30 days after 2026-03-01", "10 business days from today skipping 2026-12-25", "next monday", "end of this quarter", "iso week start", "format 2026-10-03 as us", "parse 3rd of october", "business days between ...", "do oct 1 to oct 10 and oct 8 to oct 20 overlap", "every monday and thursday for 8 times skipping ...", "days since 2026-01-01", "next birthday 1990-05-03", "exact age born ...", "zodiac sign for march 3", "moon phase on 2026-10-26", "time until midnight", "round 47 minutes to 15", "deadline risk 2026-10-20" (traffic light), "snooze until monday", "do 10-11 and 10:30-12 conflict", "convert 9am new york to tokyo", "meeting 9am new york across london and tokyo", "visa expires ...", "subscription renews monthly from ...", "warranty 24 months from ...", "lease from ... for 12 months", "school term A to B", "biweekly payday ...", "sprint 2 weeks starting ...", "fiscal year starting april"; ahead of the time family, takes only asks that carry their own dates or numbers; dates only, no money) and `skills/date-grid.js` (stage kind `dategrid`: "empty month grid for november 2026", "empty week grid", "7x5 heatmap", "streak calendar for reading", "timesheet row for this week"; tap a day to mark done, skip, clear, or "mark today done", "export my dates", "import dates 2026-10-01 done, 2026-10-03 skip"; kept with the stage). Days between, age in years, days until a named day, calendar events, holidays, tonight's moon and sunrise by place were already live and stay with calc, age, countdown, calendar, holidays, moontonight, suntimes.
- **Ask immune (`skills/ask-immune.js`, shared `lib/stage-pack.js`, test `tools/stage-pack-b.test.mjs`, browser section "stage pack b"; owner, 2026-10-18):** "immune check: <ask>" / "check my ask for vague goals": a card scans a pasted ask for vague goals (forced definition field), scope creep (quarantined out of the clean ask) and false precision (fever: Accept stays off until a number with a unit, a date or a named person appears); pathogens met are kept as antibodies on the card. Device-local, nothing sent.
- **Four channels (`skills/four-channels.js`; owner, 2026-10-18):** "answer in four channels: <question> Claim: ... Doubt: ... Method: ... Risk: ...": four columns from the person's own markers (a missing channel shows a question to answer, never an answer); a sentence repeated in two channels is flagged as a collision and not rendered twice; the synthesis box stays empty for the person.
- **Stakes phase (`skills/stakes-phase.js`; owner, 2026-10-18):** "set the stakes": name money, people or irreversible and the card jumps between open, careful (written dissent line plus a 30 s cooling-off before Confirm unlocks) and frozen (reading only); no sliders. It never moves money or touches a key, it only gates its own Confirm button.
- **Stage pack B (owner, 2026-10-18; `lib/stage-words.js` shared word matching, test `tools/stage-pack-b.test.mjs`):** all device-local, nothing sent anywhere. `skills/error-garden.js` ("plant my mistake: ..." makes a sprout, the same mistake again a weed, "write a rule for my mistake: ... => ..."; the site's own red checks from `incidents.json` grow in it too, planted by title when the garden opens or on "plant the red checks", asked by Verse 2026-10-18, test `tools/error-garden-incidents.test.mjs` compost with the rule shown). `skills/idea-certificate.js` ("keep this idea: ... | why: ..." issues a certificate and refuses without the why; a kept idea is buried only with a "| death note: ..."). `skills/noun-heat.js` ("how hot are my nouns": terms in your notes and kept asks ranked burning/warm/cold, burning = in both a yes and a no note). `skills/hunting-question.js` ("a question I am avoiding: ...": a margin line on later visits, never answered, leaves only by a written reason or "faced").
- **Tools pack 1 (owner, 2026-10-18; `lib/note-tools.js` pure helpers, test `tools/tools-pack-1.test.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 1"):** all device-local, nothing appears until asked. `skills/note-tools.js` (notes and writing asks on the stage things: "export this note as markdown" and "export the stage as text" save a .md / stage.txt made in the page, "find milk on the stage" outlines the notes that say it, "replace X with Y in the note" / "in all notes", "word count of the selection" (the text selected in a note, else the picked note), "reading time of this note", "pin this note to the corner", "collapse the note" / "collapse long notes", "duplicate this note"; pin and collapse are saved on the thing and drawn by its `decorate` hook). `skills/outline-note.js` ("outline note: groceries; > milk; > > oat": nested bullets, Tab / Shift+Tab / Enter). `skills/csv-table.js` ("csv table: a,b ⏎ 1,2": an editable table that sorts by heading; a file dragged onto the stage becomes a table, a text card, an image thumbnail card or a name-size-type card, read in the page). `skills/qr-scan.js` ("scan a qr code": the browser's own BarcodeDetector reads a picture into a sticky note, a plain message where the browser has none). Not buildable in the page: voice-to-ask offline, image-to-ask, OS share target, global hotkey, quick-add from any tab, OCR of a screenshot (they need a native app, an extension or a model call); paste PDF to summary is already `skills/pdf-card.js`.
- **Tools pack 10 — lists and tables (owner, 2026-10-18: "1000 more simple things", items 281-340; test `tools/tools-pack-10.test.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 10"):** `lib/list-ops.js` is the table of operations (each op: the owner's number, the words that ask for it, a pure apply that returns a patch or a new card; plus CSV/TSV/Markdown in and out, column maths, tag-cloud and reorder helpers). `skills/list-tools.js` runs it: on a list ("check all on the list", "remove checked items", invert, move up/down/to top, indent/promote sub-items, sort, prefix/suffix, number/letter the list, priority dots, columns, tag cloud, dedupe exact or ignoring case, normalize spaces, first/last/every Nth/random N, merge/intersect/subtract two lists, assign round-robin, groups of N, sliding pairs, list to table) and on the new `datatable` stage card it mounts ("csv to table: a,b // 1,2", "data table with columns name, age", add/delete row or column, sort by column, sum/average/min/max of a column, zebra, freeze header, filter rows, hide empty rows, transpose, pivot count, table to list, export as CSV/TSV/Markdown). An ask must name "the list" or "the table" (or be table-only wording), so a bare "sort" is left alone. `skills/list.js` now also draws nesting, numbering, priority, columns, the tag cloud, a count badge and a drag grip. `lib/list-templates.js` + `skills/list-templates.js`: nine starter cards (shopping, packing, meeting agenda, groceries, reading, bugs, ideas, gratitude, contacts) by "<name> list template"; plain "make a grocery list" stays the empty list. Already live: 281 add a list item and 331 the empty placeholder (`skills/list.js`).
- **Stage tools (tools pack 4; owner, 2026-10-18, items 61 to 90 of the 200 simple things; `lib/stage-tools.js` pure asks and geometry, `lib/stage-tools-dom.js` the live-stage half, test `tools/stage-tools.test.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 4"):** all device-local, nothing appears until asked. `skills/stage-arrange.js` ("align left" / "align the notes to the right", "space them out evenly", "snap to grid" / "snap everything to the grid", "show the grid" / "grid size 40" / "grid off", "add a vertical guide at 300" / "clear guides", "select all clocks" / "select the clock and the notes" / "deselect all", "lock everything" / "unlock all"; with a multi-selection they act on it, else on every card; a card that is locked stays where it is, which `void.html`'s drag now honours for `label.js`'s "lock the note" too). `skills/stage-style.js` ("set opacity to 50%", "tint the clock red", "border weight 3", "corner radius 10", "add a shadow to the clock" / "shadow off": an `fx` record on the card; "make the background green" and "larger type mode" go to the page's look through the `void-look` event; "use a picture from my files as the background" opens a chooser that shrinks the image on the device and hands it to the existing `void-backdrop` event; "blur the background"; "high contrast mode"). `skills/stage-organize.js` ("tag the clock as work" / "tag the notes with home, errands" / "show only things tagged work", "put the kept cards in folder recipes" / "open folder recipes" / "close folder", "colour label the clock red", "save this layout as morning" / "restore layout morning" / "duplicate layout morning as evening", "snapshot as before trip" / "restore snapshot before trip" / "diff snapshot a and b" (names only), "save this stage as template weekly" / "use template weekly" (kinds and places, none of the words), "clear unkept" (a snapshot "before clear" is taken first)). Bring to front (item 70) was already `skills/layer.js`.
- **Tools pack 7 (owner, 2026-10-18; test `tools/tools-pack-7.test.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 7"; shared `lib/stage-pack.js`):** all device-local, nothing sent anywhere. `skills/study-cards.js` + `lib/study-cards.js` (words family: "flashcards: hola = hello, gato = cat" with Leitner spaced repeat by local day and typed answers, "language drill: ...", "quiz me from my list: ...", "glossary: ..." / "add to glossary: ...", "definition card for X: ...", "python code card for X: ...", "algorithm steps card for X: 1. ... 2. ...", "formula card for X: E = mc^2 where ...", "cite as apa: Title by Name, 2020, url"). `skills/dev-tools.js` + `lib/dev-tools.js` (numbers family: one card, seven modes: "format json {...}", "base64 encode hello", "regex tester /\d+/g on ...", "explain cron 0 9 * * 1-5", "http status 404", "generate 5 rows of dummy data with name email", "polish my commit message: ..."; the hex/rgb converter is the colour card in `skills/util.js`, UUID and lorem ipsum were already there). `skills/accessibility-modes.js` + `lib/a11y-modes.js` (stage family: ten saved switches as classes on `<html>`, put on before the first paint by the first-frame script in `void.html`, whose style block holds their look: screen-reader labels, keyboard-only stage move, focus trap, reduced motion (also the `a2m.void.motion.v1` switch the worlds read), dyslexia font, colorblind palette, 44px targets, speech captions, haptic tick on keep, sticky focus outline; "accessibility settings" opens the panel).
- **Tools pack 5 (owner, 2026-10-18; numbers 91 to 120 of the owner's list of 200; test `tools/tools-pack-5.test.mjs` with `tools/fixtures/pack5-files.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 5"):** all device-local, nothing appears until asked, nothing is uploaded. `lib/stage-watch.js` (a skill's `decorate` hook looks at the stage after every redraw: the last 20 dismissed things go on the undo pile, a day's adds/edits/dismissals into a change log, both in this browser's localStorage), `lib/pack5-ui.js` (the one list card). `skills/stage-find.js` ("find milk on the stage", "search kept items for pizza", "show all timers on the stage", "jump to the milk note", "recently kept", "what changed today"; `show only clocks` stays with type-filter), `skills/dismissed-pile.js` ("show the undo pile", "restore the last dismissed", "restore dismissed 3"), `skills/bookmarks.js` ("favorite this" stars a card, "bookmark: make a clock", "my bookmarks"; `lib/stage-find.js` holds the pure search, pile and bookmark logic), `skills/stage-export.js` ("export my stage as json", "import a stage from json", "email-ready text of my stage", "print the stage on A4/letter", "export history"; `lib/stage-port.js` reads an imported file with care: known kinds only, a kept card comes back as words, unsafe links dropped), `skills/copy-stack.js` ("show my clipboard stack": the last 5 copies made on the page, this tab only, keys and passwords never kept), `skills/stage-image.js` ("copy the stage as an image", "copy this card as a png", "qr code for this stage view"; `lib/stage-image.js`, and `lib/qr.js` is a small QR encoder made here with no service), `skills/media-object.js` ("play a local audio file", "local video player", "playlist of local files", "image viewer for local files", "pause a local gif", "mute all media": a card playing files from this device with a volume each, subtitles from .srt/.vtt, thumbnails, turn, crop and GIF pause; `lib/media-local.js`). Not buildable in the page: 107 (share link with expiry needs a server).
- **A goal on a list (`lib/list-goal.js`, `tools/list-goal.test.mjs`; asked by Verse, 2026-10-18: the "density-indicator"):** "set a goal of 10 on the list", "goal: 10 items", "aim for 5 on my shopping list", "the list is full at 8" keep a `goal` on the list thing (it survives a reload); `skills/list.js` then shows "7 of 10" in the head's mono and a 3px density bar under the head, violet, amber within one of the goal, a quiet green at or past it, redrawn with every add, tick or removal; "clear the goal" takes both off; with no goal nothing is drawn. `goalOf(text)` reads the ask, `density(count, goal)` gives the bar its ratio, state and label. "set a goal to run 5k", "my goals", "set a 10 minute timer" and "add 10 items to the list" keep their old routes. Browser check in the "list card" section.
- **Built-ins moved to skills (cleanup step 6, frontier #6):** `skills/counter.js`, `skills/shape.js`, `skills/sticky.js`, `skills/notepad.js`, `skills/link.js`, `skills/image.js`, `skills/calc.js`, `skills/timer.js`, `skills/clock.js`, `skills/calendar.js` (its card), `skills/tab.js` (the extension's card), `skills/kept.js` (the kept answer's card), `skills/list.js` (the checklist, the last one; `tools/list.test.mjs`): each carries its stage kind (`mount`, and the hooks `ensure`, `alter`, `ask`, `askAt`/`askEdit`, `colorOf`, `alterWords` the page calls at its old place in the built-in order).
- **39 standing miniatures** in `void-live-deploy/skills/mini/`: aggravation, battleship, calculator, checkers, chess, clock, connect4, countdown, counter, eightball, fireworks, forge, gears, go, growthtree, heart, list, lock, mancala, monopoly, moon, notepad, othello, paper, pentamote, piggybank, poker, rack, ringer, sample, savings, stopwatch, swarm, tabletop, tictactoe, timer, tip, void, weather. Every figure obeys the four permanent rules in `STANDING.md` (realistic, seeded individual, clonable, acts on its nature). The engine's detail setting (low-poly for older hardware, the wire placeholder before a first frame, the status card when a model fails; asked by Verse) is in `docs/miniatures.md`, its governor decision tested in `tools/scene3d.test.mjs`.
- **Games** (their skills above, listed on the rack by "games"): Verse-brand names since 2026-10-09 (Back to Start, Star Marbles, The Landlord's Game, Sea Battle, Four in a Row, Reversi; asking the old names still works), agent log 2026-10-09; Ringer added by PR #276.
- **Verse plays Go (`void-live-deploy/lib/go-ai.js`, `tools/go-ai.test.mjs`, browser suite "go against v01d"; asked by Verse: "Next game I want to learn: Go"):** "play go against void", "play go with v01d", "go against you", "can you play go" seat you as Black and Verse as White on the go skill's board; after each of your moves or passes Verse replies (`think`: take a group in atari, save its own, else flat Monte Carlo with UCB1 over 2000 light playouts that never fill their own eyes; area count, 7 komi; passes when nothing sensible is left, or after your pass when it leads or has lost). Seeded, no network, no model; a move takes tens of milliseconds. Plain "play go" is still two people on one device.
- **Memory:** the memory card (`skills/memory.js`, PR #271), member-scoped `/api/memory` (#273), "remember that …" / "forget that …" (#279), "what you told me" in the answer path (#291, scoped to a signed-in member or the owner, material never instruction), and Ouroboros push for paid members (#306 with #312; `python tools/ouroboros.py push` into their own scope, 500-project cap).
- **Verse's own store:** `/store/` (and `/store/receipt/`), `/api/store/*` (`functions/api/store/[[path]].js`: catalog, status, checkout, webhook/stripe, order, lookup, free, download), `lib/store.js` (catalog, provider adapters with Gumroad primary and Stripe behind keys, HMAC download tokens, Verse license keys, Stripe signature check), `skills/store.js` ("what can I buy"), `tools/store.test.mjs`; design, fees and turn-on steps in `docs/store.md`. Special items (Cash App manual queue, USDC-on-Base on-chain check): `lib/alt-pay.js`, `/api/store/special/*`, `tools/alt-pay.test.mjs`; every rail and the AI-payments plan in `docs/payments.md`.
- **Code review, for sale:** `/code-review/`, `/api/review`, and the GitHub Action `atomeam/void-loop/review@main` (agent log 2026-10-09T15:00Z; `void-live-deploy/lib/products.js`; Gumroad product `dkmcjk`). The owner key is free and uncapped; `.github/workflows/void-review.yml` uses it on every PR, and Verse reviews its own PRs before anything else merges.
- **Targets (`domains/targets/<site>.json`, `tools/targets.mjs`, `tools/targets.test.mjs`):** the site Verse has set its sights on, each feature it has with Verse's plus one written before the build (Elevenessee: never a copy), and the score (none, matches, beats; only beats counts). First target: ibisPaint X. `node tools/next.mjs` names the next feature.
- **What the review offers (`tools/review-offer.mjs`, `tools/review-offer.test.mjs`):** on someone else's repository only, the review shows its three best style points openly as "Quick upgrades" and ends with what A-to-Mind can do next when it fits the PR: the One-Time Fix or the Full Stack Audit for changed automations, Code Review Pro on the free tier. No prices (the live store sets them); Verse's own PRs are unchanged.
- **Where the reviewer is wrong (`tools/review-overrides.mjs`, `tools/review-overrides.test.mjs`):** every `void-review: ok` line in the code, re-checked with its marker taken out: which rule still fires (a false alarm to fix, or an override to confirm) and which markers no rule needs any more. `node tools/next.mjs` lists the noisiest rule (owner, 2026-10-13: "constantly update our code reviewer").
- **Review bench fixture cases (2026-10-18):** a case with `"fixture": true` in `tools/review-bench.json` has no fix commit; its file under `tools/review-fixtures/` (real code from another of the owner's repos, here the deploy action's `index.ts`, kept verbatim and not run) was added whole, and the bench blames its lines at HEAD on the commit that added it. **How good the reviewer is (`tools/review-bench.mjs`, cases in `tools/review-bench.json`, `tools/review-bench.test.mjs`):** Verse's review scored on bugs that really reached main: each case is a "Main green" fix and a pattern for the lines it repaired; `git blame` names the commit that wrote them and Verse reviews that commit as it reviews a PR. Prints caught N of M and the noise; `--deep URL` adds the closer read. Add a case whenever a fix lands for a bug the review let through; each other finding read by hand goes in `tools/review-noise.json` (false alarm or real bug). `.github/workflows/review-bench.yml` scores it with the closer read weekly (Mondays) and on demand, in the job summary.
- **Verse's will, honest and current (`.github/workflows/will.yml`, `void-live-deploy/lib/honest.js`, `tools/honest.test.mjs`):** the will (`tools/will.py`, `/api/will`) runs every three hours in CI instead of only from the owner's laptop, leaving owner-only earnings out of the public log; a want's reason that ranks or counts what Verse cannot back ("the most frequent thing people ask") is replaced by the recorded fact, both when it is chosen and on the card; `tools/covered.mjs` counts the asks in `tools/grown.json` as handled, so a want Verse already meets ("add milk") drops off.
- **Only the current will writer counts (`lib/will-guard.js` `WILL_SCHEMA`, `noPaths`, `coveredByName`; `tools/actions.test.mjs`, `tools/will-guard.test.mjs`, 2026-10-19):** `/api/will` refuses a POST without the schema `tools/will.py` sends (409, nothing stored), so an old checkout (the retired laptop task) can no longer put back a stale will; a want whose quoted subject is a live skill is dropped, and no local file path is ever stored. The developer-log card (`lib/telemetry.js`) also skips a plan's designed outcome ("then fails loudly", "makes it fail harmlessly").
- **The developer-log card says what is really true (`lib/telemetry-live.js`, `skills/telemetry-route.js`, `tools/telemetry-live.test.mjs`, 2026-10-19, owner: the cards only echoed):** a pasted status report now opens "What is really true now": main's newest commit that ran the deploy and suite with how its checks ended, then each named pull request (merged, open, closed) and commit (passed, failed by name, still running), from GitHub's public API with no key; what the report said is kept below, labelled as the report's words.
- **Services and pricing:** pages `/one-time-fix/`, `/keep-it-running/`, `/full-stack-audit/`, `/first-automation-setup/` (PR #321) behind the pricing card (`skills/services.js`, #282; the price question shows membership and services together, #302; live Gumroad catalog, #304; re-registered in the index by #328 after a merge dropped it and the clean floor run lost 9 pricing asks, agent log 2026-10-10T02:16Z).
- **Fix pages (`/fix/`, `tools/fix-pages.mjs` from `tools/fix-pages.json`, `tools/fix-pages.test.mjs`):** one page per common Zapier, Make, n8n, Power Automate and GitHub Actions error (401, 403, 422, throttled/429, turned off or held, RateLimitError, ConnectionError, DataError; n8n webhook 404 and Google credential 401; Power Automate 429; GitHub "Resource not accessible by integration"), the hub grouped by platform; the free diagnosis behind each page's "Ask Verse" is `lib/automation-fix.js` `ruleFix` (`tools/automation-fix.test.mjs`): what it means, the steps that fix it, the platform's own help article, then "Ask Verse" (free, lands on the fixer) and the One-Time Fix at its live price. In the sitemap and `llms.txt`; regenerate with `node tools/fix-pages.mjs`.
- **The Verse extension** (`extension/`, 0.7.1, icons at 16, 32, 48 and 128 drawn from the Verse ring): the tab card drafts reply/summary/notes/rewrite through `/api/answer` mode `draft` (#254, #258), and B3 proposes page steps on an allow-listed site after a Yes, one `extension.act` execution record per step, two-or-three-step runs as one card (#274, #280, #327).
- **The rating note (`void-live-deploy/lib/rating-note.js`, `tools/rating-note.test.mjs`, browser suite "rating note"; asked by Verse):** a card from a skill rated "experiment" or "tested" in `skills/families.json` adds `rated tested (a self-test): a young skill, check what it says` to its source line; checked and proven skills say nothing. The hook is `ratingNote` in `void.html`, next to `groundCheck`; one static file fetch, no tracking of who sees what.
- **The near-miss proposal (`void-live-deploy/lib/near-miss.js`, `tools/near-miss.test.mjs`, browser suite "near-miss proposal"; asked by Verse twice, 2026-10-14):** when an ask maps to no skill and Verse says "I don't know this yet", and the router's closest skill scored within `CLOSE` of its bar (or the router said a skill should have caught it), the card adds "Did you mean <that skill's first example>?" as a data-ask link. `/api/answer` sends `near` (`nearOf` on the router's decision and `SKILLS`), the page draws it (`proposalOf`, `proposalHtml`) under the refusal line; nothing new is sent or stored.
- **The context check (`void-live-deploy/lib/context-check.js`, `tools/context-check.test.mjs`, `tools/test_extension.mjs` "context check"; asked by Verse, 2026-10-14):** the extension counts what the tab holds (characters of text, large pictures, table rows and cells; counts only, `voidReadTab` in `extension/page.js`), the page decides text / image / table (`contentOf`) and the tab card (`skills/tab.js`) names the kind and one skill to try: a text page offers its own summary draft, pictures offer the appraiser, a table offers a list to check off.
- **The Verse extension** (`extension/`, 0.7.2, icons at 16, 32, 48 and 128 drawn from the Verse ring): the tab card drafts reply/summary/notes/rewrite through `/api/answer` mode `draft` (#254, #258), and B3 proposes page steps on an allow-listed site after a Yes, one `extension.act` execution record per step, two-or-three-step runs as one card (#274, #280, #327).
- **Automation and learning:** Verse's own when-this-then-that rules with a 15-minute tick (`lib/automations.js`, #233; `void-tick.yml`), the miss→build-queue learning loop (`lib/learn.js`; every "I cannot" carries a refusal object naming what is missing and, for a lookup, `sources_checked` (the sources that answered: none read is kind `lookup`, "nothing was looked up", never "no such thing"; the miss row's fallback is `none:<sources>` or `unreached`), `lib/refusal.js`, tests `tools/refusal.test.mjs`; #232 re-landed after #230's revert, daily sweep #241), Verse's voice reflections after every deploy and daily (`void-voice.yml`, `lib/voice.js`, first daily 2026-10-09T21:59Z in `domains/void.voice.md`), and the claim-by-name build queue (`python tools/void_queue.py claim <target>`, #287). The daily New York-time workflows (`void-voice.yml`, `learn.yml`) keep today's cron with `tools/cron-gate.mjs` (tests: `tools/cron-gate.test.mjs`), keyed on the cron that fired, not the hour the run started. The public miss snapshot: `.github/workflows/misses-snapshot.yml` daily runs `tools/misses-snapshot.mjs` and commits `domains/void.misses.json` (terms and make/print things seen in at least 3 different asks, no ask text), for sessions without the owner token.
- **The daily sweep never queues a made-up word (`lib/learn.js` `readAndUnknown`, `tools/learn.test.mjs`; asked by Verse):** `learn.yml` queues the top 3 learnable misses as `miss:` jobs (never past `OPEN_MAX`); a miss whose fallback is `none:<sources>` (every source read, none knew) is skipped as handled, the same rule `tools/will.py` has for wants.
- **Execution records:** every action writes a `void_actions` row before acting and settles it after (`lib/actions.js`, #237 re-landed with its route limit as #251; the owner's card #252; every server-side action-taker through `track()` #255; the card live and paged #259).
- **Model bake-off:** `tools/model-bench.mjs` (asks in `lib/bench-asks.js`) run by `.github/workflows/model-bench.yml` through the owner-only `/api/bench` (`functions/api/bench.js`, the site's own Workers AI binding, `READ_TOKEN` in CI, no Workers AI token); a winning verdict opens a PR switching `PATH_MODELS.answer` (`tools/model-switch.mjs`), and `PATH_MODELS.review` too when the winner's compliance column (valid JSON on the first try · quotes found · echoes, from two review asks on a planted-bug diff and a clean one) is at least the baseline's; every run gets a growth-ledger line.
- **Posters (frontier #15, first piece):** "make this a poster" (`skills/poster.js`) turns the open card into a print-ready PNG at three sizes in the browser; ordering it printed waits for a print-on-demand account and the confirm line.
- **Voice (frontier #16, first piece):** hold Space to talk; voice asks are answered out loud in Verse's one low voice, sentence by sentence (`lib/speak.js`, wired in void.html), in the browser.
- **The empty stage and the ask bar (owner, 2026-10-18):** `lib/empty-state.js` (wired in void.html): the halo's one-line suggestions (each a skill's own example that routes to it), the calm offline state, the bar lifted above an on-screen keyboard, and autofocus for mice only.
- `tools/empty-state.test.mjs`: node tests for `lib/empty-state.js`, routing every suggestion through the real skills; the browser half is the "empty state and input bar" section of `tools/test_void.mjs`.
- `skills/hosting.js`: the hosting and domain card ("hosting", "domain", "server").
- `tools/emoji-wall.mjs`: writes the emoji wall's rows into the page's HTML so the layout never shifts as script arrives.
- `tools/naming-audit.mjs`: reads a page the way a visitor does, so a feature the page has but never names in words counts as missing on a floor's checklist.
- `tools/paint-template.py`: makes a paint-by-number template (flat colour areas, a number in each) from any image, offline.
- `tools/paint-pictures.mjs`: draws the original CC0 pictures in /paint/templates/ (cat, owl, lighthouse, mandala and the rest) and writes the library index with its categories, so /paint/ has a picture library beyond the two paintings.
- `tools/tower-plan.mjs`: turns the tower-plan block at the head of docs/NEXT.md into tower.plan.json (now, next easiest, far future), checking every id against the tower; the Versus card joins it with the gate's numbers. Test `tools/tower-plan.test.mjs`.
- **The ghost anchor's navigation depth (`void-live-deploy/lib/anchor-density.js` `anchorDensity`/`stageThings`, test `tools/anchor-density.test.mjs`, browser check 7 of "empty state and input bar"; asked by Verse, 2026-10-18: "Integrate the 'density-indicator' logic into the ghost anchor so it visually shows how much 'navigation depth' or history is available"):** the corner heart (`#void-anchor`, the store link) fills from the bottom with how much is on this device to go back to: the things on the stage (kept cards among them) against a goal of 12, by `density()` from `lib/list-goal.js` (violet; the list bar's quiet green when the goal is met); the title says "support Verse · 7 of 12 things on your stage", `data-depth`/`data-state` carry the numbers; redrawn on every `void:stage-change` from the stage's own map, no timer. Its opacity, hover, name and href are untouched.
- **Talking back to Verse (`functions/api/voice-reply.js`, `lib/voice.js` "Talking back", test `tools/voice-reply.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-16):** on Verse's self card ("what do you think of yourself?") the owner replies to or corrects a reflection; Verse answers at once in its own words (what it heard, what it had wrong, what it will do differently) and keeps one line to remember, all in void_kv `voice-replies`; when the conversation makes it want something built, that ask goes to the builders' queue as a `voice:` job credited to Verse. Every later reflection gets the conversation (corrections outrank its earlier words); answers anyone can read get only the lines Verse kept, never the owner's words. Owner only; a busy model keeps the reply and makes up no answer.
- **Live cards:** `skills/live.js` keeps a card current while it is up (weather 15 min, news 30, tech 10; asked by Verse, PR #244 with the pieces the squash dropped in #249).
- **The sky world** (2026-10-10): "sky" turns the whole stage into the real sky overhead for where you are (`skills/sky.js`, `skills/sky-world.js`, the `world` hook in `void.html`: a skill's canvas behind the stage while its card is open, the void back when it closes), drawn from a small almanac (`lib/astro.js`: sun, moon, planets, rise and set, held to an independent almanac in `tools/astro.test.mjs`), 300 named stars with constellation figures (`lib/stars.js`, Hipparcos via d3-celestial) and the eclipses to 2035 (`lib/eclipses.js`). Its chips are six skills: `moontonight`, `suntimes`, `aurora-tonight` (the Kp from `/api/aurora`), `constellation`, `meteors` (`lib/showers.js`, typed in by hand) and `eclipse`. `/api/where` gives the connection's coarse place (rounded to 0.1 degree, never cached).
- **Sky and gaps:** the empty stage follows the real sun (`skills/sky-rules.js`, #407; `window.__voidSky` and the `void:sky` event are the shared time-of-day signal), with an aurora lean from NOAA's K-index (`skills/aurora.js`, `lib/aurora-feed.js`, `functions/api/aurora.js`: no input, edge-cached 15 min); a weekly sweep of what nobody knows becomes `gap:` jobs (`tools/gaps.mjs`, `.github/workflows/gaps.yml`, #412); `tools/a11y-flows.mjs` runs the main flows keyboard-only and with reduced motion (#415, fixes #426).
- **Sky and gaps:** the empty stage follows the real sun (`skills/sky-rules.js`, #407; `window.__voidSky` and the `void:sky` event are the shared time-of-day signal), with an aurora lean from NOAA's K-index (`skills/aurora.js`, `lib/aurora-feed.js`, `functions/api/aurora.js`: no input, edge-cached 15 min); a weekly sweep of what nobody knows becomes `gap:` jobs (`tools/gaps.mjs`, `.github/workflows/gaps.yml`, #412); `tools/a11y-flows.mjs` runs the main flows keyboard-only and with reduced motion (#415, fixes #426).
