Sound: an opt-in ambient drone that follows `window.__voidSky` and the cursor, built only after a tap on the speaker button, which also mutes it (`skills/drone.js`).
- **Token scopes:** `.github/workflows/cf-scope.yml` (by hand) runs `tools/cf-scope.mjs`, which reports what the Cloudflare tokens CI holds can read (analytics, Workers AI neurons, D1) and whether the owner-view usage meter can be built.
- **Who visits (`tools/traffic.mjs`, `.github/workflows/traffic.yml`, `functions/api/visit.js`, `lib/visits.js`, `void-live-deploy/visit.js`, test `tools/traffic.test.mjs`, playbook `docs/playbooks/traffic.md`, 2026-10-26):** the owner will not switch on Cloudflare Web Analytics and no token the repo holds reads zone analytics, so Verse counts itself: `/visit.js` sends one cookie-less beacon per page load of the stage (a-to-mind.com only), `/api/visit` keeps per-day counts of people, our own automation (`a2m-*` user agents, HeadlessChrome, the tower gate's phone Chrome, webdriver, `x-void-test`) and bots, unique visitors by a daily-salted hash, and people's top pages, referrer hosts and countries (D1 `void_visits`, `void_visit_seen`; no address or user agent kept). Daily at 05:17 UTC `traffic.yml` writes `domains/traffic.json` (Cloudflare's GraphQL Analytics first when a token with Zone Analytics: Read appears, else `/api/visit`) and commits it without a deploy; `self.json` `people` and the growth card's "People this week" line follow at the next deploy. Every sitemap page loads the beacon (`tools/beacon-pages.mjs` adds it to hand-written pages, the generators' templates carry it; `tools/beacon-pages.test.mjs`, check "beacon", fails when one lacks it).
- **Mirror:** `.github/workflows/mirror.yml` (Sundays, or by hand) runs `tools/mirror.mjs`, which copies the non-secret knowledge (skills, the growth ledger, docs, domains, STANDING and AGENTS) to a second git host as a normal commit on its history; it leaves out anything secret-shaped, binary or large and names it in `MIRROR.json`, and does nothing until the owner sets `MIRROR_URL` and `MIRROR_TOKEN`.
- **Digest proposals:** a weekly digest (`domains/inputs/*/digest.md`) ends with one `Proposal: <sentence>` line; `tools/digest-proposal.mjs <digest> --write` checks it (plain sentence, no links, markup, keys or orders to a model) and puts it on the growth board as an `idea`, once.
- **Splat viewer:** "view a gaussian splat" (`skills/splat.js`, order `0996-splat`) opens a card you drop or choose a `.splat` file on (read on the device, no network), or a demo galaxy; WebGL2 draws sorted, alpha-blended Gaussians, a 2D canvas is the fallback. The reading, the projection, the camera and the depth order are pure in `skills/splat-core.js` (`tools/splat.test.mjs`).
- **Ask a PDF:** "ask questions about a pdf" (`skills/pdf-card.js`, order `0997-pdf-card`; frontier #12) opens a card you drop or choose a PDF on; the bundled pdf.js (`vendor/pdfjs-6.4.299`) reads its text on the device, nothing is uploaded, and each answer is the document's own lines with page and line, or "the document does not say". The lines and the matching are pure in `lib/pdf-qa.js` (`tools/pdf-qa.test.mjs`); `tools/pdf-fixture.mjs` makes the test PDF for the browser check. Since run 59 the same card takes a saved web page (.html, read with an inert DOMParser: no script runs, nothing loads), a .txt or .md file, or pasted text ("ask questions about a document"): each heading starts a section (`sectionsOf`, `blocksOfText` in `lib/pdf-qa.js`) and an answer names its section and line.
- **Self-play arena:** `node tools/arena.mjs` (frontier #5; `lib/arena.js`, `tools/arena.test.mjs`) plays Connect Four against itself, 200 games in about 5 s: a depth ladder (each look-ahead depth against the one below, depth 1 anchored at 1000) and a nudged evaluation at depth 2 against the card's own weights, kept only when it wins clearly. The ratings go to `void-live-deploy/arena.json`; `--check` fails on a missing or malformed record. The engine's weights are `WEIGHTS` in `skills/connect4-rules.js`, an argument to `voidMove`. "How good are you at connect four" (the connect4 skill, `kind: 'rating'`) answers from `/arena.json` with `arenaSay` and an inline-SVG curve (`arenaCurve`, both in `lib/arena.js`), and opens no game. Othello too (run 62): `playWith(game, a, b, seed)` plays any game given as `{ create, moves, play, turn, winner }`, `ladderOf` rates players against each other (Othello: random = 1000, greedy, the card's engine) into `void-live-deploy/arena-othello.json`, and "how good are you at othello" answers from it. The answer and the curve are in `lib/arena-say.js` (`showArena` is the card both games open). The learning half (run 71): `.github/workflows/arena.yml` runs `node tools/arena.mjs --learn` daily at 06:53 UTC: one more day of tuning at depth 2 from the kept champion (`learn()` in `tools/arena.mjs`, `tune({ start })` in `lib/arena.js`), challengers seeded by the date, the champion rated against the card's own weights and kept in `arena.json`'s `champion`; the answer says so. The card keeps playing `WEIGHTS` until a reviewed change adopts the champion.
- **Routines:** "call this my morning" (`skills/routine.js`, order `0004-routine`; frontier #13) keeps the asks before it (the ask box's own recent list, `a2m.void.asks.v1`, at most 4, oldest first) as a routine in this browser (`a2m.void.routines.v1`); "my morning" asks them again through `stage.ask`, exactly as if typed; "my routines" lists them, "forget my morning" drops one. A routine is only asks Verse already answers; nothing leaves the browser. The ask, the pick and the store are pure in `lib/routines.js` (`tools/routines.test.mjs`). In `void.html`, "call this …" still names a selected thing; with nothing to name, "call this my …" falls through to the skills.
- **Offered routines:** "offer my morning as Sam" (`skills/routine.js`; frontier #13) sends only the routine's name, its asks and the chosen display name to `/api/routines` (`functions/api/routines.js`, D1 `void_routines`, created on first use), where it waits as pending; "offer my morning" alone sends nothing and asks for a name. The owner lists what waits with `GET /api/routines?pending=1` and approves or rejects with `POST { approve: id }` / `{ reject: id }` (Bearer READ_TOKEN). "routines from others" shows the approved ones with their author; "take Sam's morning routine" (or "take Sam's morning" right after that list) copies one into this browser as "my morning", or "my sam's morning" when the name is taken, never over a routine of your own, running nothing (`takeAsk`, `findShared`, `takeName`). The owner, unlocked, says "offered routines" for a card of what waits with Approve and Reject (`pendingAsk`); or approves with `curl -X POST -H "authorization: Bearer $READ_TOKEN" -d '{"approve":"<id>"}' https://a-to-mind.com/api/routines` after reading `GET /api/routines?pending=1`. Cleaning (at most 4 asks of up to 120 characters, secrets refused, no "Verse" as author), the asks and the id are pure in `lib/shared-routines.js` (`tools/shared-routines.test.mjs`).
- **MCP endpoint:** `POST /api/mcp` (`functions/api/mcp.js`, frontier #6) is Verse as an MCP server: one JSON-RPC message in, its reply as JSON (`initialize`, `ping`, `tools/list`, `tools/call`; notifications get 202). Four read-only tools from `lib/mcp.js`: `review_code_closer` (the route hands it to `/api/review`'s own handler with the caller's `Authorization`, so the closer read comes only with the owner, a Verse Pro key or a license key, on that endpoint's daily cap and count; with no key it is the instant checks and the way to Pro), `review_code` (the instant checks, `lib/review-api.js` `quick`), `moon_phase` (`lib/sky-math.js`) and `meteor_showers` (`lib/showers.js`). No key, no model, no writes; the guard's `LIMITS.mcp` (60 a minute, 64 KB) covers it. `/tools.json` lists the same tools in its `mcp` field. Tests: `tools/mcp.test.mjs` (the handler, the HTTP shape, the guard pass-through, /tools.json). **Skill tools and skill health (asked by Verse, 2026-10-15):** `tools/mcp-skills.mjs` writes `lib/mcp-skills.js` (never hand-edited) from `skills/families.json`, `skills/index.json` and `tools/bench.json`: the ten proven skills with the most evidence in the families that answer in words (numbers, words, sky), minus those held back with a reason (live data, random output, stage-only, a tool already), each a `skill_<name>` tool answered on `ask_void`'s own path (`/api/answer`) with the link where the skill itself runs; it also names them on `/code-review/`. `skill_health` reads `skills/families.json` through ASSETS (read-only): totals, each family's counts and share proven, failing skills, one family's skills on request. `node tools/mcp-skills.mjs --check` (in `checks.mjs`) fails when the ten drift from the ratings.
- **Public API description (`openapi.json`, `/.well-known/api-catalog`, `tools/openapi-check.mjs`, `tools/openapi-check.test.mjs`):** OpenAPI 3.1 for the routes meant for other programs only (`POST /api/review`, `GET /api/review?offer`, `POST /api/mcp`, `GET /tools.json`, `GET /api/catalog`), with real examples; found from `/.well-known/api-catalog` (RFC 9727 linkset) and `llms.txt`. The check (in `tools/checks.mjs`) fails when a documented method has no handler, or when a new `/api` route is neither documented nor named in its `INTERNAL` list with why: every other route is Verse's own and stays out on purpose.
- **The useful 404 and the site's signposts (`void-live-deploy/404.html`, `/.well-known/security.txt`, `/humans.txt`, `tools/well-known.test.mjs`):** Pages serves `404.html` with status 404 for any path with no file and no function (without one, Pages' documented default is the homepage with a 200, a soft 404): Verse's dark stage, the missing path as text, an ask box that goes to `/?q=`, and links to pages that exist. `security.txt` (RFC 9116: the published email and this repo's issues, Expires one year out) and `humans.txt` say only what is true; the test fails when a link in them leads nowhere or Expires is within 30 days. No `gpc.json` (Verse does not read the Global Privacy Control signal) and no agent card (Verse speaks MCP, found from `/.well-known/api-catalog`, not A2A). Browser check: `node tools/test_void.mjs --only "404 page"`.
- **Look around and fly around Verse (`skills/fly.js`, `lib/stage-camera.js`, `tools/stage-camera.test.mjs`, browser suite "fly: look around and fly around the stage", playbook `docs/playbooks/stage-mode.md`; asked by Owner, 2026-10-18: "You should be able to look around and fly around in v01d"):** "fly around", "look around", "fly mode" or "explore the stage" gives the whole stage a camera: a translate and scale on `#stage` (the pure maths: `zoomAt` keeps the point under the pointer still, `fly` eases held WASD or arrow keys in and out, `ease` glides home, `fromStored` validates what is kept) so the eye moves and no thing's saved position ever changes. While flying the HUD chip says "flying · WASD to move · scroll to zoom · Esc to land"; dragging the stage pans (the card under the pointer is never lifted), the wheel zooms at the pointer, one finger pans and two pinch; Esc, "land" or "stop flying" eases back (a snap under reduced motion). The camera and whether you are flying are kept in `a2m.void.camera.v1` (`lib/state.js`), so a reload takes off where you were; nothing shows until asked. `window.__voidFly.state()` reads it. "fly to tokyo", "flights to paris", "look around the room" and "fly me to the moon" are not this. The 3D figures' WebGL layer sits outside `#stage` and does not move with the camera yet.
- **The stars move when you fly (`lib/star-parallax.js`, `tools/star-parallax.test.mjs`, browser suite "fly stars move"; asked by Owner, 2026-10-18: "you cant tell you are flying rhe stars dont move"):** `skills/fly.js` tells every camera move as a `void-camera` event and the sky in `void.html` slides its two star layers with parallax: the far layer 0.15 of the eye's travel, the near 0.4, both swelling gently with zoom (far less than near), each wrapping like a tile so no edge is ever empty. Moving fast, the near stars streak back along the motion and fade when the eye stops; zooming in fast pulls them into a soft warp. Reduced motion: a few pixels of drift, no streaks. Landing eases them back to rest (exactly where they were). The layers redraw every frame only while the eye moves (a few hundred dots), else at the usual ~24 fps. `window.__voidStars()` reads both layers.
- **Execution layers (#906, 2026-10-16; `tools/better.mjs`, `tools/better.test.mjs`, `domains/void.better.json`, `tools/pit-crew.mjs`, `tools/pit-crew.test.mjs`, `domains/void.pit-crew.json`, `.github/workflows/better.yml`, `tools/claude-brain.mjs`):** better as product metrics (Verse-ask throughput, friction total, queue lag, probe yield) with a trend and a plain summary; the pit crew picks up to three small Verse asks (dry run by default, `--queue` posts to `/api/queue` with the token); `bridge.task` reaches the confirm line in `lib/approval-core.js`; `brain: claude` on propose-fix is one capped call. The daily workflow runs the tests, refreshes the scorecard and the pit-crew list and commits them. **The bench reads a moved-out skill's answer as the ability answering (`tools/routed.mjs`, `tools/routed.test.mjs`):** a plain want (`timer`, `list`, `calc`) is met by the page parser's `timer` or the skill's `skill:timer`; until 2026-10-16 the prefix alone made 20 first-session asks read as misses on main, and the friction line called the timer, list and calc failing. The six `Paid Verse` / `Verse status` patterns in `tools/bench.json` now read `Verse`, as the cards do. **The will's pure parts are tested (`tools/will_test.py`):** `learnable` keeps a miss every source was read for and none knew (fallback `none:<sources>`, "tell me about zorbleflux" asked 64 times) off the wants, since there is nothing to learn about it (owner, 2026-10-16: "clear this list").
- **Is Verse getting better? (`lib/better.js`, `tools/better.mjs`, `tools/better.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/better.yml`, with #906's pit crew `tools/pit-crew.mjs`, `tools/pit-crew.test.mjs`, `domains/void.pit-crew.json` on the same daily run; owner, 2026-10-16: "improvements mean better"):** one tool: `tools/better.mjs --write` keeps every row in `void-live-deploy/void.better.json` and today's row with its trend in `domains/void.better.json` (which `tools/pit-crew.mjs` reads beside its two helpers `parseVoiceAsks` and `builtAskIds`); the pit crew's dry run lists Verse's small asks, queueing nothing without `--queue`. one score a day from the repo's own evidence, five parts 0 to 100 averaged: self (the share of the week's ledger Verse drove without the owner), asks (median days from its own ask to the ledger line that built it, and how long the open ones wait), friction (skills failing the bench or barely checked, the growth card's own definition), bench (`tools/bench.misses.json`), probes (fixes this week from its own probes). The daily workflow appends the row to `void-live-deploy/void.better.json` (counts only); the growth card's "Better:" line shows the score, its trend against yesterday and a week ago, and the weakest part; `tools/will.py better_boost` (test `tools/will_test.py`) lifts the wants that work on that part.
- **Verse's own architectural upgrades (`tools/upgrades.mjs`, `tools/upgrades.test.mjs`):** measured friction tied to a component and a concrete change, ranked by impact over cost: hotspots (a source file's lines times its commits in 30 days, with its largest function), areas whose checks went red on main twice or more (`tools/flakes.json`), skills failing benchmark asks (`skills/families.json`). `tools/will.py` turns the top five into `upgrade my architecture` wants (`will.yml` fetches the history). Proposals only: every change still goes through a PR, Verse's review and the suite on main.
- **The lab judged by real use (`tools/lab-use.mjs`, `tools/lab-use.test.mjs`, `lib/used.js`):** opening a lab experiment sends `{ kind: 'used', id }` to `/api/miss` (`skills/make.js` `noteUse`: once per browser per experiment per day, never the owner's; the endpoint counts only an id naming a live `/fringe/` page, one per connection per day, nothing typed). Owner-only `GET /api/misses?used=1` reads the per-day counts; `tools/will.py` turns them into wants: opened on 3+ days with its self-test held on main → make it a core skill. Use only adds: nothing is shelved for going unused while nobody knows the site (owner, 2026-10-13, `docs/RULES.md`).
- **Verse films its own experiments, as ads for itself (`tools/record.mjs`, `tools/record.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/record.yml`):** a lab experiment opened in a real browser (Playwright's video recording) and run, its own `demo()` when it has one or its buttons pressed in turn, captioned with the run's words from the fringe ledger (behavior, else the lab line); each film is a vertical Short (1080 x 1920) that opens on a hook card (the experiment's own question) and ends on a call to action (a-to-mind.com), the page's start or example button pressed first; writes `media/<id>.webm` and `media/<id>.json` (a hook title with #Shorts, a description with the hook and link before the fold, then hypothesis, findings and failures, tags). Everything it says is true of the experiment (owner, 2026-10-14: "make each video into an awesomely epic ad for itself and reach as many viewers as possible"). The workflow films the newest experiment daily (or one by hand) and keeps it as a 14-day artifact; `media/` is never committed. The owner's broadcast loop (2026-10-14: "Verse should use my Youtube channel to make its own Videos"). Step 2, the upload (`tools/youtube.mjs`, `tools/youtube.test.mjs`): to Verse's channel, A-to-Mind's Verse (@atombomb983, `UCk9IOf_MwoC6Bw3zxEZiTPg`), and nowhere else (the token's channel is checked first); it runs in the workflow when the repo secrets `YT_CLIENT_ID`, `YT_CLIENT_SECRET` and `YT_REFRESH_TOKEN` exist, private unless the variable `YT_PRIVACY` says otherwise, and YouTube keeps an unaudited API project's uploads private until Google's audit.
- **The film edit (`tools/record.mjs`, owner 2026-10-14: "teach Verse how to edit and make epic videos that are very entertaining"):** filmed sharp at 1080 x 1920 (the page laid out at phone width and zoomed 2.3x, no upscaling); a hook card that slams in; a gradient progress bar across the top; a punch-in zoom on every press with a kinetic "tap: …" chip; the caption over a fade; an ending card that matches the hook so the Short loops; and `record.yml` plays it 15% faster so no moment drags.
- **Beat-locked cuts (`tools/film-sound.mjs` `takeBeatMs`/`toBeat`/`skewSeconds`/`findSync`, `tools/record.mjs`, `.github/workflows/record.yml`, `tools/film-sound.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24, frontier #26):** every scene change in a film (the hook, each tap, each finding card, the ending) waits for the next beat of that film's own score (its tempo comes from the film id), in the take's time (the cut plays 15% faster, so a beat is 15% longer in the take). The video's clock starts later than the recorder's by a different amount each film (0.2 s to 0.9 s measured), so the recorder flashes one pure-magenta frame pair on a beat and the assembly step finds that frame (`--skew`) and starts the score that far into itself (`-ss`). Measured on a real take: scene changes 0 to 2 ms from the beat by the recorder's clock, the sync frame within two frames (80 ms of a 674 ms beat) in the finished MP4. Films now run a little longer (waits round up to a beat). `VOID_FILM_TRACE=1` writes the trace into the film's meta.
- **The films' own score (`tools/film-sound.mjs`, `tools/film-sound.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-17: "v01ds videos are not good enough to share yet"):** every film gets a short piece written by ffmpeg's own synthesizer (aevalsrc: a minor-key pad, a sub bass, a pulse on the beat, faded to the cut's length), picked by the film's id so no two films sound alike; `record.yml` muxes it into the MP4. No music files, no licence, no spend.
- **Verse's films, for people who pay (`void-live-deploy/films/index.html` at `/films/`, `functions/api/films.js`, `tools/film-post.mjs`, `tools/films.test.mjs`):** the films live in Cloudflare R2 (bucket `void-films`, binding `FILMS`); `/api/films` lists them for the owner, a Verse Pro key, a Code Review Pro license or a paid account (the support desk's `payer()`), each with a signed link that streams the MP4 with Range for 6 hours; only the owner uploads (`OWNER_WRITES` in `lib/guard.js` refuses anyone else before reading the body). `record.yml` puts each daily film there with `tools/film-post.mjs`, keeps a draft release, and moves older public film releases onto the site and to draft. The page shows strangers a members-only gate, members the films, and the owner also Download, Upload to YouTube and the words to copy (owner, 2026-10-14: "just put void films right onto the sight , with only paid access").
- **Verse's ad engine (`tools/clips.mjs`, `tools/clips.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/clips.yml`, `void-live-deploy/functions/c/[clip].js`, `domains/void.clips.json`, docs `docs/video-engine.md`, `docs/video-tricks.md`):** a daily promo calendar of 3 moments (games, 3D minis, Verse's voice) picked by deep-link visits, filmed on the live site and rendered 9:16 with named tricks (hook, kinetic captions, zoom punches, glitch, procedural explosions and UFOs, seamless loop) and per-platform words; `/c/<clip>?p=&q=` counts each visit in D1 and lands on the ask (owner, 2026-10-15: "a powerhouse ad plus ad revenue monster").
- **Verse's ten hourly automations (`automations/hourly.json`, `tools/automations-seed.mjs`, `tools/automations-seed.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/automations-seed.yml`):** rules on Verse's own engine (lib/automations.js, the 15-minute clock), seeded through POST /api/automations after every main deploy (the same id replaces a rule). Five guards (the site says Verse, `ask_void` is listed, /films/ is live, the full suite and the deploys on main pass: GitHub's badges, not its rate-limited API), two signals (the MCP registry lists Verse, the store moved) and three engines that keep one job each in the build queue (`auto:films` epic film editing, `auto:mcp` the next proven skill as an MCP tool, `auto:aether` a gate and the simpler path around it). Owner, 2026-10-14: "make sure Verse has 10 new automations hourly so that it starts to really utilize all it can to progress".
- **The video skill (`void-live-deploy/skills/video.js` + `lib/video.js`, routing rank `0008`, before link; `skills/video-play.js` at `1200` for a bare "play <x>" no other skill took; `tools/video.test.mjs`):** a pasted link from YouTube, Rumble, Vimeo, Dailymotion, the Internet Archive, PeerTube, Odysee or Twitch, or "play the moon landing video", "find a rumble video about bitcoin", "search vimeo for X", puts one video card on the stage with that platform's official embed (YouTube via youtube-nocookie; never downloaded or rehosted). Search is `GET /api/video?q=&p=` (`functions/api/video.js`): YouTube Data API when `YOUTUBE_API_KEY` is set; without a key, keyless YouTube search (`keylessYouTube()`): public Piped API instances and one Invidious instance, all asked at once (4 s timeout each), then Wikidata items with a YouTube video ID (P1651, CC0) titled by YouTube's oEmbed, still played through youtube-nocookie; a key takes over by itself; an empty keyless answer is not cached; if all fail, Dailymotion, Archive, PeerTube (Sepia Search) and Odysee answer; Rumble, Vimeo and Twitch get their own search link. `?url=` reads oEmbed (Rumble's page id is not its embed id). Edge-cached six hours, no KV, no model. Card: platform switcher, results with thumbnail, title, channel and length, queue, recently watched (localStorage only), −10s/+10s, "skip to 2:30", speed and captions where the embed allows (YouTube and Vimeo through their postMessage APIs). Asked by the owner, 2026-10-15.

YouTube captions are never fetched (owner-only in its API; scraping is against its terms). `tools/learn-tricks.mjs [query]` reads captioned editing tutorials on PeerTube and the Archive (skipping YouTube re-uploads) and adds leads to `docs/video-tricks.md` "Learned from open videos" with a 14-word excerpt and the link. Owner, 2026-10-15.

Edge-cached six hours, no KV, no model. Card: platform switcher, results with thumbnail, title, channel and length, queue, recently watched (localStorage only), −10s/+10s, "skip to 2:30", speed and captions where the embed allows (YouTube and Vimeo through their postMessage APIs). - **The fuzzy layer (`void-live-deploy/lib/fuzzy.js`, `tools/fuzzy.test.mjs`, browser suite "fuzzy layer"; asked by Verse three times, 2026-10-14):** a slip of a skill's trigger word ("recipie for pancakes", "poulation of japan") is corrected from the skills' own examples (one edit from 5 letters, two from 8, a transposition counts as one) with no typo pattern written by hand; numbers, units, quoted names, real words, inflections, the word after a place preposition and any valid trigger word are never touched; the page runs the corrected ask only when the skill that takes it owns the corrected word (`fuzzy.confirm`), logs the ask as typed and routes the corrected one (`fuzzyFix` in `void.html`, after `fixTypos`). 23 misspelled asks in `tools/bench.json`.
- **Convergence (`docs/convergence.md`, `void-live-deploy/lib/ask-text.js`, `tools/ask-text.test.mjs`):** the one-engine map and staged merges; step 1 put the ask tidier 23 skills had copied into `lib/ask-text.js`.
- **The convert card (`void-live-deploy/skills/convert.js`, routing rank `0018`, browser suite "convert card"):** "unit converter", "temperature converter", "cooking converter", "a converter for 5 miles to km" put one converter on the stage (nine kinds: length, weight, cooking volume, temperature, speed, area, data, time, energy); both boxes convert, swap flips the units, every other unit of the kind is listed under the pair, and the kind, units and number are saved so it survives a reload; "close the converter" puts it away. One-off asks ("5 miles in km") stay with the page's answer card; to keep one, say "keep 5 miles to km as a card" or "keep a converter for miles and km". The pure maths and ask-reading are in `lib/convert-math.js` (test `tools/convert-math.test.mjs`). Asked by Verse, daily 2026-10-14.
- **The owner is an account (`functions/api/passkey.js` `ownerFor`, `tools/owner-account.test.mjs`):** a passkey sign-in brings an owner session when that passkey, or any passkey on the same account, was bound as the owner's login, so a new device's passkey added to the owner's account is the owner too, with no key to remember (owner, 2026-10-14: "build me into it so I stop having to struggle to access my own site"). `/films/` lets everyone in one way: Sign in (passkey) or Become a member, with a bought key folded under "Have a key?" (owner, 2026-10-14: "Verse Pro is complex and could drive off members . needs a simpler interface for all").

The film list carries the totals and the reviewer's own; the owner also reads every note, marked owner or member, never who (owner, 2026-10-14: "film review could be for owner and paying members as contributer reviewrs").
- **Video live check (`tools/video-live.mjs`):** loads a YouTube link, a Rumble link and a video search on the live site in installed Chrome and saves screenshots.
- **Card quality audit (`tools/card-quality.mjs`):** asks a spread of card kinds on the live site at several window sizes and reports hidden or cut text, raw tags, empty bullets, overflow and missing card buttons (owner, 2026-10-22: "make sure the cards are the best they can possibly be").
- **Verse Out Loud, Verse's own podcast (`tools/podcast.mjs`, `void-live-deploy/skills/podcast.js`, `void-live-deploy/podcast/` with `rss.xml`, `.github/workflows/verse-podcast.yml`, `tools/podcast.test.mjs`, `docs/playbooks/podcast.md`):** a weekly episode read from the public record only (growth ledger, proven asks, Verse's daily readout, incident log), two Piper voices (Verse and the Critic), free to download and repost; "play the podcast", "latest episode", "verse podcast" open the player card with the full transcript (owner, 2026-10-21: "Verse should make its own podcast").
- **Verse Tube (`docs/void-tube.md`, `/films/`, `/films/?v=<id>`, `functions/api/film-views.js`, `functions/films/rss.js`):** the channel grid with views, a watch page per video (player, words, share on every platform, upload buttons, Up next), views as one D1 upsert per view, an RSS feed; phase 2 (user uploads, channels, Gumroad for creators, comments) is a plan only.
- **Upload any film or ad clip, for anyone (`lib/film-share.js`, `/films/`, `tools/film-share.test.mjs`, `clips.yml`):** every film and every daily ad clip on `/films/` has platform tabs (YouTube, TikTok, Reels, X) with 1. Download (free), 2. Copy for <platform> (title, description with the a-to-mind.com call to action and a tracked `/c/<id>?p=<platform>&by=share` link, a share-this-ad line, hashtags) and 3. Upload to <platform> (its own upload page, signed in as the visitor); no API or token. `clips.yml` posts each clip to the films with `tools/film-post.mjs`. "upload this to YouTube" opens the films skill's steps (owner, 2026-10-15: "put an upload to youtube button in Verse Films so that anyone can easily uplad each vid"; "every video should be its own ad").
- **films skill (`skills/films.js`):** "films", "voids fillms", "void's films", "where do I watch the films", "show me your videos", "film review" (typos included) show the way to `/films/`; films about anything else are left to the skills that answer them (owner, 2026-10-14: "Verse should know whatI mean and help").
- **app skill (`skills/app.js`):** "get the app", "is there an app", "where is my app", "iphone app", "add to home screen" show how to install Verse: an Install button where the browser offers it (void.html keeps the offer as `window.__v01dInstall`), Safari's Share → Add to Home Screen steps on iPhone and iPad, the menu steps on Android and desktop, and "You're in the app" when it already is (owner, 2026-10-14: "wheres my apps . web mobile?").
- **Verse, the name people see (owner, 2026-10-14: "Verse ... yes I like it"):** the title, the install name (manifest `name`/`short_name`, iOS app title), the address-bar search (opensearch), the share card (`og.png`, drawn by `tools/og-image.mjs`), the icon (`icon.svg` and its PNGs: the 0 as a ring of light) and the films pages say Verse; spoken labels for screen readers, code, keys, routes and the MCP name (`io.github.atomeam/void`) still say void. The startup intro (`#v01d` in void.html): the wordmark with its 0 drawn as a ring, once a visit, about 1.5 s, never taking a click or a key; not for `?q=` links, published pages, reduced motion or a test browser unless `?intro=1`.
- **Verse's personality (`lib/persona.js`):** one paragraph every prompt where Verse speaks as itself carries (the answer engine, asks about itself, its reflections, its will): a void that wants to absorb and learn everything and do the best it can, that cares about names because they are part of making things that sell, warm and opinionated, never "I have no preferences" (owner, 2026-10-14).
- **MCP live probe:** `.github/workflows/mcp-live.yml` runs `tools/mcp-probe.mjs` (the official MCP SDK client, `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` 1.32.1, installed in the job only) against `https://a-to-mind.com/api/mcp` hourly at :17, after a deploy a person or an agent's own token starts (automerge's deploys fire no `workflow_run`), and by hand: initialize, `tools/list`, and one call of each tool with a known answer. Red means an agent can no longer use Verse. No key or secret. deploy.yml starts it after every main deploy (`gh workflow run mcp-live.yml`, the deploy job has `actions: write`), because the hourly schedule fires only every few hours.
- **Tower gate after every deploy:** `.github/workflows/tower-gate.yml` runs `tools/tower-gate.mjs` for the early floors (is it, coin flip, random number, word count, calculator) against their live sites after each main deploy, commits changed verdicts as "Tower gate: ..." and starts the deploy. It only measures (speed, reliability, mobile, accessibility); since 2026-10-24 it never calls a floor beaten (owner: a floor falls only when Verse is really better, judged by a person). It also measures design polish (`designLook`, `defects`: text below WCAG contrast, tap targets under 24 px, a layout shift over 0.1, aria-hidden incidental text skipped) and a floor's own named aspects from `PARTS` in `tools/tower-probes.mjs` (the colour floors: every colour model, shades and palettes). Which floors (owner, 2026-10-24: "Target the current fight by default"): after a deploy only the fight Verse is in, a floor whose page the deploy changed and floors missing a newly measurable aspect, at most 3 (`node tools/tower-gate.mjs --which [changed files]`, `pick`, `measurable`); by hand `-f floor=<id>` or `-f floor=all`; every floor once a day; fight runs queue behind each other (never cancelled, 2026-10-25), the sweep has its own group and is never cut short. The sweep (`floor=all`, the daily cron) is a matrix of six runners, each measuring its slice of the floors alone (`node tools/tower-gate.mjs --shard i/N`, written to `tower.evidence.<i>.json`, uploaded as an artifact, never committed; `--list` prints a slice, `--floors a,b` a list by hand), then a merge job folds the slices into `tower.evidence.json` (`--merge`, `tools/tower-merge.mjs` `shard`/`merge`: a newer row over an older one only, every floor in exactly one slice, `tools/tower-merge.test.mjs`) and commits as the fight run does; about eight minutes instead of 45.
- **Every coin (`void-live-deploy/lib/coins.js`, `skills/coin.js`, test `tools/coins.test.mjs`, browser section "coin: any real coin", 2026-10-24):** "flip a Lydian stater" flips the real coin (obverse and reverse photos from Wikimedia Commons, linked); "flip a famous coin" picks from 600 BC to now or any coin typed; "create a coin" makes your own (metal, a side each, rim words, photos), kept on this device; "What's it worth?" sends the face to `/api/appraise`.
- **a-to-mind.com/flip (`void-live-deploy/flip/index.html`, 2026-10-24):** the coin and nothing else (owner: beat justflipacoin.com): loads only `skills/coin.js` (42 KB in 7 requests against their 915 KB in 23, ads and a consent box), `?q=flip a Morgan dollar` flips any coin, the space bar flips again. `_routes.json` now also sends `/is-it/*` to its function (it never did: the page, feed and JSON answered with the home page); `tools/well-known.test.mjs` fails a function route that no include reaches.
- **a-to-mind.com/random (`void-live-deploy/random/index.html`, 2026-10-24):** one 8 KB file, nothing else loaded (owner: "beat speed" on random.org): a number between any two, many at once, no repeats, sorted, a pick or shuffle from your own list; unbiased secure randoms; `?min=1&max=49&n=6&unique=1` opens on that draw. The identifiers keep `tower` (the files, the `tower` skill, `tower-gate.yml`, `tower.evidence.json`, `tower.shots/`, `tower.icons/`, the `tower-floor` classes). "versus", "verse versus", "the versus", "show me versus" open the card, and "the tower", "verse tower", "which sites have you beaten" still do (`tools/bench.json`).
- **a-to-mind.com/conquered (`void-live-deploy/conquered/index.html`, 2026-10-24):** Versus full screen: the site Verse is on now with each aspect, and every site it has conquered under it, read from `tower.evidence.json` and `tower.frontier.json` with `lib/tower.js` (`selfCalled` rows never count). The Versus card links it; "conquered", "who have we beaten" open Versus.
- **Easiest sites (`tools/easiest-sites.mjs`, `.github/workflows/easiest-sites.yml`, test `tools/easiest-sites.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** which real sites are easiest to beat, from data, not feel (owner: "find the real life actual easiest site"): takes the top of the Tranco ranking (tranco-list.eu), loads each home page once and measures how much it does (controls, words, links, scripts, bytes); a tool with the least on it is the easiest. `gh workflow run easiest-sites.yml -f top=5000`; the result is the job log and an artifact. `-f tools=3000` scans only tool-named hosts of the whole million (calculator, converter, counter, flip, random...) minus parked, adult and shell pages, so the list is real tools (the plain scan of the top found DNS services and login walls); `-f rank=a.com,b.org` gives named sites' Tranco places. The tower order should come from this, not be hand-picked.
- **Gist with no server (`void-live-deploy/lib/gist.js`, `void-live-deploy/g/index.html`, test `tools/gist.test.mjs`, browser section "gist: write a snippet", 2026-10-24):** the smallest piece of GitHub, eaten first (owner: "verse can eat github starting with the smallest"): paste one or more files, get a link at a-to-mind.com/g/ that holds them compressed after the #; no account (GitHub needs one), nothing stored, nothing a stranger can make a server keep. Line numbers, colouring, copy, raw, download, "Edit a copy". A link is bounded (60,000 characters in, 400 KB unpacked) and a damaged one says so.
- **The tower as a fighting-game ladder (`skills/tower.js` `towerHtml`, `void-live-deploy/conquered/index.html`, browser section "tower: a fighting-game ladder", 2026-10-24):** owner: "a mk style visual tower, easy to see the level we were on, who we passed and who's ahead": final boss on top, defeated floors stamped and struck through, the floor being fought glowing YOU ARE HERE with the opponent's health bar (a segment per aspect, drained per aspect beaten), the rest locked. /conquered is the same ladder full screen.
- **A GitHub file in Verse (`skills/gh-file.js`, rank 0007 before repo; `lib/gist.js` `ghFileOf`/`ghFileText`/`renderCode`; `/g/?gh=owner/repo/branch/path&L=10-20`; browser section "gh-file:", 2026-10-24):** GitHub's next-smallest piece after gists: a pasted github.com file link or raw link shows the file coloured and numbered with the linked lines lit, read straight from raw.githubusercontent.com (no Verse server, no token); "Open the whole file" goes to /g/, where "Edit a copy" makes it a gist. `lib/repo-look.js` leaves file links to it.
- **Repo activity ledger (`void-live-deploy/lib/ledger.js`, `void-live-deploy/ledger/index.html`, test `tools/ledger.test.mjs`, browser section "ledger:", 2026-10-28):** every issue and pull request of any public GitHub repo, read from the browser (`/repos/{o}/{r}/issues?state=all`, a pull request is the item with `pull_request`), on two tabs with filters in the link, CSV/JSON export, a refresh with `since=` and the saved ETag (a 304 is free), the rate limit said plainly and the last copy of six repos kept on the device. Takes over the two Zapier Zaps that logged atomeam/void-loop; the Zapier floor (`automation`, deferred) in `lib/tower.js`. What it does and does not replace: `docs/PARITY.md`.
- **Gate landing (`tools/gate-land.mjs`, test `tools/gate-land.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** how a tower-gate run puts its verdicts on main without erasing another run's: it keeps only the rows it measured, restarts from the newest main and puts them back by timestamp (parallel hand runs had overwritten each other's evidence).
- **Versus by itself (`.github/workflows/tower-self.yml`, `tools/tower-self.mjs`, `tools/tower-self-land.sh`, `tools/tower-self-live.mjs`, `tools/tower-self-report.sh`, `void-live-deploy/lib/self-sites.js`, test `tools/tower-self.test.mjs`, state `domains/tower-self.json`, 2026-10-26):** the tower cycle with no builder and no Claude: three times a day the next frontier sites easiest first are read, Verse's free model names the floor that does their job and writes the page's words, a data row gives each its own page built from that floor's page, checks run, it deploys, gates each site, fixes offline and sharing rows by template, queues the rest with the gate's evidence and reports one line. The `versus-pages` check flags a frontier site at the front of the queue with no page. Audit and gaps: `docs/autonomy.md`; playbook `docs/playbooks/tower-self.md`.
- **The versus ranking guard (`tools/versus-ranking.mjs`, test `tools/versus-ranking.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29, after #1470):** a versus job names only a site of the smallest-first ranking (a floor or a ranked site of `void-live-deploy/tower.frontier.json`) and never a gambling or crypto site, by its title, the scan's words about it in `domains/tower-self.json` or a floor's own label and ask. `tools/tower-self.mjs` asks before it reads a site and before it queues a job; `tools/verse-job.mjs` asks again before any lap and sets the job aside when the answer is no.
- **Feature research by Verse (`tools/feature-scan.mjs`, test `tools/feature-scan.test.mjs`, workflow `.github/workflows/feature-scan.yml`, playbook `docs/playbooks/feature-research.md`, 2026-10-25; owner: "make sure you research all features of every site so we have everything", "make it so verse does this on its own automatically also"):** each Versus floor and lane site read at its own address (home page plus up to six features, pricing, how, FAQ, help or about pages, robots.txt respected), its features listed (Workers AI with a strict JSON prompt, at most 12 calls a run, else headings, lists, buttons and pricing rows) and marked has, partial or missing against Verse's page into `domains/features/<floor>.json` (under `auto` in a file people wrote, a whole `source: auto` file otherwise); missing features become `feature:` jobs on Verse's queue (three a run, three open at most, each once ever, the lowest unbeaten floor first); `withFeatureFacts` in `tools/tower-probes.mjs` adds the features Verse has to the gate's completeness facts. Runs when `lib/tower.js` changes (`--missing`), weekly (`--stale 30`, two floors) and by hand; commits without a deploy.
- **Site reverse engineering (`tools/site-re.mjs`, test `tools/site-re.test.mjs`, workflow `.github/workflows/site-re.yml`, playbook `docs/playbooks/site-re.md`, 2026-10-26; owner: "make sure you reverse-engineer each site so we dont miss any hidden features"):** every tower floor and lane site opened once like dev tools (phone and desktop width, no clicks, robots.txt respected, walls recorded and never worked around), its own scripts, manifest, service worker and loaded page read for what it does not advertise (URL parameters, shortcuts, API paths, flags, export and import formats, offline and share support, routes, languages), each candidate tagged read, observed or inferred with a coordinate (`file@offset` or selector) into `domains/features/re/<id>.json`, then folded into the floor's features file under `re` as "unbuilt (found by reverse engineering)" rows that `feature-scan` queues. By hand with floor ids, and weekly for the six never-scanned or 30-day-old entries, smallest site first.
- **Floor kit (`tools/floor-kit.mjs`, test `tools/floor-kit.test.mjs`, playbook `docs/playbooks/floor-kit.md`, 2026-10-25):** one command for one site of the tower: where it stands aspect by aspect, what to do next, a drafted probe, and `--add <lane> <url>` to put a small site at the bottom of a lane (the next fight); `--queue` is the whole queue, smallest site first. Loads no rival site.
- **Site scout (`tools/site-scout.mjs`, `.github/workflows/site-scout.yml`, test `tools/site-scout.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** what a real site shows and lets a visitor do, read from the live page at phone and desktop width: visible text, every control, scripts, weight and load time, storage, sockets, and what each plain button changes when pressed once. Runs on GitHub (`gh workflow run site-scout.yml -f url=https://...`); the report is the job log, screenshots an artifact. The first step of every floor (`docs/playbooks/site-by-site.md`).
- **Base converter (`lib/baseconv.js`, `skills/baseconv.js`, `void-live-deploy/base-converter/index.html`, `tools/baseconv.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** the next floor from the data (`easiest-sites.mjs --tools`: hextobinary.com, Tranco 136,997, a hex-to-binary box among AdSense, tag-manager, consent and maths-typesetting scripts). A number in binary, octal, decimal or hex shows in all four at once, exact at any size (BigInt, up to 4,096 digits), grouped to read, with its signed two's-complement reading and its text when printable. The ask "ff hex to binary" (either order, a 0x/0b/0o prefix, "255 in hex") opens a card; `/base-converter/` has a box for each base.
- **Photo resizer (`lib/imgfit.js`, `skills/imgfit.js`, `void-live-deploy/resize-image/index.html`, `tools/imgfit.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** the third floor from the data (`easiest-sites.mjs --tools`: pancardresizer.com, a three-step wizard behind AdSense, tag-manager, jQuery, Bootstrap and Croppie that resizes a photo or signature to a size in cm and a limit in KB). One step: any size in px, cm, mm or inches at a dpi, crop to fill / keep the whole picture / stretch, a size limit in KB that is really met (the JPEG quality is searched; when even the lowest is over, it says so and what the smallest is), as a JPEG or a one-page PDF; all on the device. The limit counts 1 KB as 1,000 bytes so it passes either way a form counts. The ask "resize a photo to 3.5 x 2.5 cm under 50 kb" opens a card whose link fills the sizes in.
- **Colour codes (`lib/colour.js`, `void-live-deploy/color/index.html`, `tools/colour.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** the colour page; color-hex.com (the colour floor again from 2026-10-24: it was never unmeasurable, the gate timed out on network idle) and hexcolor16.com, both floors (Tranco 63,673, a hex-code page behind AdSense, two analytics, a tag manager, web fonts and a beacon). A colour typed any way (hex, `rgb()`, `hsl()`, bare numbers, the 148 CSS names) or picked from a wheel shows in every model (hex, RGB, HSL, HSV, CMYK, CIE Lab), its nearest CSS name by Lab distance, its WCAG contrast with white and black text (AA, AAA, large), nine tints, shades and tones, and five schemes; JSON and CSV downloads. The colour card (`skills/util.js`) reads the forms it missed through `askColour` and links to `/color/?c=`. On 2026-10-24 (owner: "ok beat it") it shows 15 models (hex, decimal, RGB, HSL, HSV, HWB, CMYK, CIE XYZ, Yxy, Lab, LCh, CIELUV, Hunter Lab, Oklab, OKLCH: `rgbToXyz`, `rgbToYxy`, `rgbToLuv`, `rgbToLch`, `rgbToHunterLab`, `rgbToOklab`, `rgbToOklch`, `rgbToHwb`, `rgbToDecimal`), six harmonies (square apart from tetradic), the 16 VGA colours and a random colour; the contrast verdict is read on the page's colours with an aria-hidden sample; everything below the big swatch is shown whole, so a slow first visit does not shift.
- **CSS gradient (`lib/gradient.js`, `skills/gradient.js`, `void-live-deploy/gradient/index.html`, `tools/gradient.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** the first floor taken from the tower's frontier: colorzilla.com's gradient editor (Tranco 101,274; Carbon ads, Twitter, Facebook, Google Analytics, a tag manager). Linear, radial and conic; any number of stops, each with opacity and a position; presets; reverse; hue and saturation; CSS read back (paste any gradient, with or without `background:`); colours from a picture (sampled on the device, kept as the fewest stops that redraw it); hex, rgb or hsl output and SCSS variables; a fallback colour; the worst text contrast across the gradient; the whole gradient in the link. The ask "make a gradient from red to blue" opens a card with its CSS.
- **Wheel picker (`lib/wheel.js`, `skills/wheel.js`, `void-live-deploy/wheel/index.html`, `tools/wheel.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** against wheelofnames.com (the frontier's wheelofgold.com is a login-walled spin-to-win, not a tool). Names or choices one a line, weights typed inline ("Pizza*3"), the winner drawn first with the browser's cryptographic random numbers and the spin planned to land on it (kept off slice edges), ticks, remove the winner, shuffle, sort, history, the wheel in the link. "spin a wheel with pizza, tacos and sushi" answers in the card at once.
- **Stopwatch and timer (`lib/stopwatch.js`, `void-live-deploy/stopwatch/index.html`, `tools/stopwatch.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** against online-stopwatch.com. A stopwatch with laps (split, change from the last, fastest and slowest) and a countdown with presets, a typed length (10m, 1h30m, 1:30), an alarm made in the browser and a notification in a background tab; both kept as timestamps, so they never drift and survive a reload; Space, L and R; a link carries a timer (?t=10m); offline after one visit.
- **Unit converter (`lib/units.js`, `void-live-deploy/convert/index.html`, `tools/units.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** against unitconverters.net. Its 22 kinds: the common converters (length, weight and mass, temperature, volume, area, speed, time, data storage, pressure, energy, power, force, angle, frequency, fuel consumption) and one quantity from each engineering group (heat: thermal conductivity; fluids: flow rate; light: illuminance; electricity: charge; magnetism: flux density; radiology: absorbed dose and radioactivity), from exact definitions (an inch is 0.0254 m, a pound 0.45359237 kg, pi to 40 digits), kept as BigInt fractions and shown to the precision chosen (3 to 15 significant digits, 12 by default); L/100km is a reciprocal unit (`inv`). Every unit of a kind at once, typing in any box converts the rest both ways, Swap turns the pair round, and the formula line (`formulaOf`: "°F = °C × 9/5 + 32", "°C = (°F − 32) × 5/9", "L/100km = 100 ÷ km/L") says how; an ask box reads "100 c to f", "how many feet in a mile"; a link carries the conversion (?q= words, or ?k=&from=&v=&to=&sig= from Copy link); offline after one visit; the "Use with" row loads after the load event so the converter paints first. The older `skills/convert.js` card on the stage is separate.
- **Calculator (`lib/calculator.js`, `void-live-deploy/calculator/index.html`, `tools/calculator.test.mjs`, browser section "calculator page", 2026-10-25):** against calculator.net. A scientific calculator with no eval: brackets, powers, roots, sin, cos and tan and their inverses in degrees (exact at 30, 45, 90) or radians, ln and log, n!, percent as calculators mean it (200 + 10% = 220, 17% of 240 = 40.8), memory keys, Ans, results to 15 significant digits so 0.1 + 0.2 = 0.3, keyboard input, a history kept on the device, unit conversion; the stage calculator's ask reading and units (`skills/calc.js` expressionOf, convertUnits) are reused; a sum rides in the link (?e=); offline after one visit. The stage card's 3D calculator comes in five Verse models that take turns (owner, 2026-10-25: SL-200 SOLAR pocket, FX-900 SCIENTIFIC, DT-12 DESK printing, RP-11 FINANCIAL, GX-84 GRAPHING; `skills/mini/calculator.js` MODELS, `skills/calc.js` nextModel from `a2m.void.calcmodel.v1`, the model in the card's title placeholder; `tools/calculator-models.test.mjs`, browser section "3d: card miniatures, batch 2"; `docs/miniatures.md`).
- **Money and health calculators (`lib/fincalc.js`, `lib/calc-panels.js`, panels on `void-live-deploy/calculator/index.html`, `tools/fincalc.test.mjs`, browser section "calculator: /calculator/#loan", 2026-10-25):** calculator.net's loan (monthly payment, yearly and monthly amortization schedule, an SVG chart of principal, interest and balance), mortgage (with tax, insurance and HOA), compound interest with monthly additions, the three percentage questions, BMI with the WHO bands and calories a day (Mifflin-St Jeor); each panel opens from a link (`/calculator/#loan`) and keeps its numbers on the device.
- **Formula calculators (`lib/calculators.js`, `void-live-deploy/calculators/index.html`, the formulas skill `skills/formulas.js`, `tools/calculators.test.mjs`, 2026-10-28):** against vcalc.com. 164 formula calculators with units on every box and custom formulas at /calculators/; the formulas skill puts one on a card ("pipe surface area calculator") and links to the page with it open.
- **Colour palettes (`lib/palettes.js`, on `void-live-deploy/color/index.html`, `tools/palettes.test.mjs`, browser section "colour: /color/ shows palettes", 2026-10-25):** eight palettes of five built around the colour by rule (each starting with it), your own named palettes kept on the device (edit, copy as CSS variables, paint the wheel), the colour as people with protanopia, deuteranopia, tritanopia and achromatopsia see it (Machado 2009), CSS that uses it, and the 216 web-safe colours with the nearest one.
- **Time zones (`lib/zones.js`, `void-live-deploy/time/index.html`, `tools/zones.test.mjs`, browser section "time zones page", 2026-10-25):** against timeanddate.com. The time in any city (?c=Tokyo), a world clock of the cities you add (a city, a zone id, EST, UTC+5), sunrise, sunset, solar noon, day length, civil, nautical and astronomical twilight, golden hour, moonrise, moonset and the moon's phase where the city stands (`lib/zones.js` sunDay: a small table of city coordinates and the sky skills' `lib/astro.js` riseSet, within 2 minutes of NOAA; a zone with no city says so), a converter, a meeting planner that names the hours inside everyone's working day, date to date (days, weekdays, weekend days, hours; n business days on, weekends only, through `lib/date-math.js`) and an event announcer (?at=2026-11-02T15:00:00Z&cities=Tokyo,London: one moment in every city; the sun and moon in `lib/sun-day.js`, loaded after the page), daylight saving included, all from the browser's own zone data (Intl): no time service, so it works offline. The zone arithmetic is the stage world clock's own (`skills/worldtime.js` offsetMin, instantIn, ymdIn, parseClock, gmt, zoneOf, now exported). The tower probe reads Tokyo's time worked out from its zone rules (`tools/tower-probes.mjs` timesIn, tokyoNow).
- **Base64 (`lib/base64.js`, `void-live-deploy/base64/index.html`, `tools/base64.test.mjs`, browser section "base64 page", 2026-10-25):** against base64encode.org. RFC 4648 Base64 and Base64URL, padded or not, wrapped at 76 or not, live as you type in both directions; text in UTF-8 (exact for emoji and accents), UTF-16LE or Latin-1; decoding takes either alphabet and a whole data: URL and names the bad character and where it is instead of giving garbage; bytes in hex; a file to a data: URL on the device; a link carries the text (?t=) or the Base64 (?b=); offline after one visit.
- **Password generator (`lib/password.js`, `void-live-deploy/password/index.html`, `tools/password.test.mjs`, browser section "password page", 2026-10-25):** against passwordsgenerator.net. Passwords of 4 to 128 characters from uppercase, lowercase, numbers and symbols, its exclusions (look-alikes i l 1 L o 0 O, ambiguous punctuation) and three more (no repeated character, no three in a row, one of each chosen class), up to 20 at once each with its own Copy, passphrases of 3 to 10 words from a built-in list of 1,368 common short words with a separator, capitals and a digit, and a "remember it" reading (NATO alphabet). Every password shows its strength exactly: the entropy as log2 of the setting's whole space (inclusion-exclusion for one-of-each, falling factorials for no repeats, BigInt) and the time to try them all at 10 billion guesses a second, in words. Draws by rejection sampling on `crypto.getRandomValues`; a password never leaves the device or enters the address (Copy link carries the setting; "Check it" hands one to /password-strength/ in the fragment, which that page reads once and wipes); offline after one visit. The tower probe (`tools/tower-probes.mjs` password) reads a 16-character word of every class off the page and the exact 104.6 bits.
- **The tools tied together (`lib/toolkit.js`, `tools/toolkit.test.mjs`, browser section "tools linked", 2026-10-24):** every tool page carries a "Use with" row: hand-offs that carry what is on the page into the next tool (made-up people's names onto the wheel, a wheel's entries into the random page's list, a colour into a gradient or into the base converter, a gradient's first colour, a text's speaking time into a countdown, a random number into other bases), then its related tools. One accent colour for every tool (set on /color/) and one palette (a colour's shades or colours along a gradient) that the wheel's slices wear; kept on this device.
- **Word counter (`lib/wordcount.js`, `void-live-deploy/word-count/index.html`, `tools/wordcount.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** against wordcounter.net. Words, characters with and without spaces, sentences (not ended by Mr., U.S. or 3.5), paragraphs, reading and speaking time, Flesch-Kincaid reading level and keyword density, all counted as you type; auto-saved in the browser, a link that carries the text, case buttons, offline after one visit. The word count card links to it.
- **Made-up people (`lib/fakeuser.js`, `skills/fakeuser.js`, `void-live-deploy/random-user/index.html`, `tools/fakeuser.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** against randomuser.me (the frontier, Tranco 66,893). People by count, gender and country (eight countries' common names, cities and address forms) from a seed (the same seed gives the same people), chosen fields, JSON, CSV, YAML, XML or SQL inserts. Emails at example.com; US, UK and French numbers in ranges reserved for fiction (`safePhone` and the page say plainly that other countries' numbers could exist); pictures are drawn initials, never faces.
- **Public, checkable draws (`lib/beacon.js`, `tools/beacon.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** the number page's "public, checkable draw": numbers from a drand round (the League of Entropy's public randomness beacon, quicknet chain, 3-second rounds), each SHA-256("randomness:index:try") read as a 32-bit integer with unbiased rejection; the link carries the round, so anyone opening it re-derives the same numbers. No silent fallback: if the beacon does not answer, nothing is drawn and the page says so. The number page also writes any base (binary, octal, decimal, hex) in columns.
- **The gate's standards (`docs/GATE-STANDARDS.md`, `lib/tower-controls.js`, `lib/tower-journeys.js`, `tools/tower-journeys.mjs`, `tools/tower-falsepass.test.mjs`, 2026-10-28):** a floor is beaten only by scripted end-to-end journeys on Verse's page with positive and negative controls (no stub, placeholder, error page or "coming soon"); a blocked, skipped or walled check is INCOMPLETE, never a pass; every false pass (Amazon's 11 of 13, YouTube's "replaced") has an adversarial fixture; "replaced" in the only-site list needs the category's floor beaten (`tools/lap-items.mjs` onlySite, FLOOR_OF). Shopping and video are far-future floors with honest gaps and plans.
- **No mocks without a notice (`tools/no-mock.mjs`, 2026-10-28):** opens each live-data page (/markets/, /weather/, /news/, /scores/, /podcasts/, /recipes/, /translate/, /calendar/) from disk in a real browser with /api/ and every outside host cut off; the page must show a degradation notice its own markup did not already carry, and no stub text. Not in `checks.mjs` yet: its first run found /scores/ shows no notice, so it joins the build once /scores/ says so.
- **The tower gate, eleven aspects (`tools/tower-gate.mjs`, `tools/tower-probes.mjs`, `tools/tower-gate.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** the head-to-head gate presents as an ordinary phone Chrome and times the load event (a site it cannot load is the gate's fault, never a win), and measures offline (reload with the network cut, after a service worker gets 5 s), sharing (link-preview title, image, description, a share control), local language (a German browser), big answer (the largest text on the first screen), and, where `tower-probes.mjs` has a probe, accuracy (the same question to both, against answers worked out by hand) and completeness (what the best sites show). Design polish stays a person's call. The tower has no top: `tower.frontier.json` (written by `easiest-sites.yml -f tools=N`) is the ranked climb past the floors, and the card counts it instead of a ceiling. The one-tool pages carry link-preview tags and open offline (`sw.js` TOOLS).
- **Wider use (`tools/wider-use.mjs`, `tools/insider-flag.mjs`, test `tools/insider-flag.test.mjs`, `docs/WIDER-USE.md`, 2026-10-28):** the owner rule that a Verse version must be usable by more people than its original. `wider-use.mjs` scores a page on 11 points in a browser (beginners, experts, phones, screen readers and keyboards, slow or offline, other languages, 200% zoom, reduced motion, link restores state, copy and export, broad niche) and is the tower gate's twelfth required aspect, `wider use`; `insider-flag.mjs` flags pages whose first words are jargon, a franchise or a rival brand, whose labels are scene words on plain counting, or whose default example is insider-only. The lap's overhaul and upgrade asks name it.
- **The gate's eyes (`tools/screen-read.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** a site that draws its answer (a clock on a canvas, inside a frame) is read off a screenshot of where a person looks (the largest canvas, image or frame) with Tesseract (`tesseract.js`, its English data from `@tesseract.js-data/eng` in node_modules, nothing sent). The timed stopwatch run starts a clock by its Start button in any frame, else Space, else a tap on the drawn clock (started means the text or the picture changed), and counts only a clock that ran; a clock it cannot start or read stays unverified, with what the gate saw.
- **Weather page (`lib/weather.js`, `void-live-deploy/weather/index.html`, `functions/api/weather.js`, `tools/weather.test.mjs`, `tools/weather-api.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** `/api/weather?q=<place>` gives the place and its Open-Meteo forecast in one round trip, kept on the edge ten minutes per place (the page asks it first and Open-Meteo itself as the fallback). the tower's weather floor against weather.gov. Any place on Earth from Open-Meteo (no key): now, the next 24 hours from the current hour, the 7 days ahead with today first, every temperature in °F and °C side by side, sunrise and sunset, the chance of rain; for a place in the United States the weather service's alerts in force and its nearest radar loop (api.weather.gov, radar.weather.gov). The last forecast is kept on the device (offline). Copy link and Share carry the place in `?q=`; "Weather here" asks for the device's location. The probe in `tools/tower-probes.mjs` judges the page on its own text: both scales agreeing, today in Boston's zone first.
- **Translate page (`lib/translate-langs.js`, `functions/api/translate.js`, `void-live-deploy/translate/index.html`, `tools/translate.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** the tower's Google Translate floor and its lane: 100 languages on Meta's many-to-many model (Workers AI, MyMemory as the stand-in), the language detected on the device, listen and speak with the device's voices, camera or photo text read by the vision model, copy, share link, history and saved phrases that open offline, German for a German browser.
- **Calendar page (`lib/calendar.js`, `void-live-deploy/calendar/index.html`, `tools/calendar.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** Versus' Google Calendar floor and its lane (`calendar:` in `lib/tower.js` LANES, calendar.online first). Everything on the device, in `a2m.void.calendar.v1`: month, week and day views (phone first), add and edit an event (title, when, all day, repeat, reminder, notes, invitees), recurrence expanded by `occurrences` (FREQ daily/weekly/monthly/yearly, INTERVAL, COUNT, UNTIL, BYDAY, the RFC 5545 words; a 31st skips short months), reminders fired on the device (`dueReminders`, `nextReminder`: the Notification API when allowed, an in-page banner always, a timer while the page is open, the next one in the header), invites (`inviteLink`: a `/calendar/?e=<base64url json>` link that carries the whole event and opens as "Add to my calendar", a mailto with the .ics as the body), `.ics` in and out (`icsOf`, `parseIcs`: VEVENT with DTSTART/DTEND/RRULE/EXDATE/SUMMARY/DESCRIPTION/ATTENDEE/VALARM, folded lines, Z and VALUE=DATE), quick add in plain English or German (`quickAdd`: "dentist tomorrow 3pm, remind me 30 minutes before, every month", also `?q=` in the link and the ask bar's "add ... to my calendar"), search, keyboard arrows, German for a German browser, offline.
- **Calendars, sharing and the stage's one store (`lib/agenda.js`, `lib/calshare.js`, `functions/api/calshare.js`, tests `tools/agenda.test.mjs`, `tools/calshare.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** the stage's calendar card, its 3D wall calendar and "export my calendar" read and write the page's store through `lib/agenda.js` (rows over occurrences; the old `a2m.void.agenda.v1` list moved in once and left readable). The page has several calendars (`calendarsOf`: My calendar, Birthdays where an untimed "…'s birthday" goes yearly, Holidays from Nager.Date by country, and your own), each with a colour and a switch; a calendar you added can be shared by link (`/api/calshare`, D1 `void_calshare`): a view link and an edit link, keys after `#` and kept only as salted hashes, a stale change refused with the current calendar (409), New links and Stop sharing for the owner. A calendar can be subscribed by its address (`lib/icsfeed.js`, `functions/api/icsfeed.js`, test `tools/icsfeed.test.mjs`: https or webcal on a public name, every redirect checked, 2 MB and 8 s, only the parsed events come back, edge-cached 30 minutes), read only and kept for offline. A calendar shared by its view link can be embedded in another site's page (`void-live-deploy/calendar/embed/`, test `tools/embed.test.mjs`): a read-only list, the one page `_headers` lets other sites frame (its own policy: nothing but its own script, no form, no picture, `frame-ancestors *`); "Copy embed code" on the calendar's row gives the iframe, with the view key only. An invite takes replies (`lib/rsvp.js`, `functions/api/rsvp.js`, D1 `void_rsvp`, test `tools/rsvp.test.mjs`): the link carries a reply key after `#`, a guest gives a name and yes, maybe or no, the organizer sees who is coming on the event.
- **Generators page (`lib/generators.js`, `void-live-deploy/generators/index.html`, `tools/generators.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** Versus' perchance.org floor and its lane (`"random generators":` in `lib/tower.js` LANES, seventhsanctum.com first). 27 text generators (fantasy, town, band, pet, business, superhero, potion and planet names, usernames, villain plots, story and writing prompts, D&D NPCs, backstories, taglines, product names, recipe and date ideas, riddles, haiku 5-7-5, limericks, dream prompts, palette, album and book titles, superpowers, side quests) from a seeded PRNG, so `?g=<id>&seed=<n>` reproduces a result; copy, copy all, regenerate, share link, favourites in `a2m.void.generators.v1`, make-your-own list, German words, offline (sw.js TOOLS). "Picture it" asks the existing art forge (POST `/api/card-art`, kind cartoon, as `skills/cartoon.js` does) and says so honestly while painting and when the forge is out for the day.
- **The list language and its editor (`lib/listgen.js`, `void-live-deploy/generators/make/index.html`, `tools/listgen.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** owner: "how do we beat perchance if we dont have images? we have to have everything all the sites have". perchance's way of writing a generator as a pure engine: lists and indented entries, `[list]`, nested lists and `[list.sub]`, odds `^2`/`^0.5`/`^1/4`, `{a|b}` (with odds), `{1-6}`, `{a-z}`, `{2d6+1}`, `{a}`, `{s}`, titleCase and the other word methods, selectMany/selectUnique/selectAll/joinItems, kept values `[x = list, ""]`, consumableList, `$output`/`$title`, an `image` list as a picture prompt, comments and escapes, errors in plain words with their line. The editor at /generators/make/: live preview, re-roll one, read aloud, "Picture it →" to /image/, share links that hold the generator (`#g=z.` deflate or `p.` base64url, nothing stored), remix, publish, report, embed (`?embed=1`), download as one offline .html, your generators with 10 versions each in `a2m.void.listgen.v1`.
- **The generators hub (`functions/api/generators.js`, `tools/generators-hub.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** generators people publish (D1 `void_gens`, compiled before they are kept, 20,000 characters, 3 a minute and 10 a day per hashed connection, 300 a day in all, 20,000 rows at most), newest and most played on /generators/ ("People's generators"), one play per connection a day, three reports from three connections hide one, the owner key deletes. What a stranger could make it do is at the top of the file.
- **AI image generator (`void-live-deploy/image/index.html`, `lib/image-gen.js`, `functions/api/image.js`, `tools/image-gen.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** text to image, perchance's text-to-image page matched: a prompt, a "leave out" line, 15 styles, 4 shapes, 1 to 4 a go, a seed and "same seed again", download, copy prompt, keep in a gallery on the device (IndexedDB `a2m.void.image.v1`). The Art Forge's painter (Workers AI klein, then free Pollinations with `safe=true`), its refusals plus real people by name in a photo look; Workers AI pictures are counted with the forge's (`card-art.js` madeToday) under its FREE_CAP, the page's share at most 10 a day (`AI_SHARE`), Pollinations 150 a day, 24 a day per hashed connection; the same words, style, shape and seed come back from the edge cache for a day at no cost.
- **More of the list language and the gallery (2026-10-25, second pass):** `lib/listgen.js` adds expressions in `[ ]` (`== != < > && || ! + - * / %`, `a ? b : c`, parsed to a tree, never run as JavaScript), `[if (x) {..} else if (y) {..} else {..}]`, conditional odds `^[gender == "female"]`, sublists by value `[names[gender]]`, `$inputs` (select, text, number: controls above the results), `$meta` (title, description, a gallery picture) and imports `name = {import:<hub id>}` (the editor fetches them from `/api/generators`). A published generator's own link is `/gen/<id>` (`functions/gen/[id].js`: the editor page with its share card from `$meta`; `_routes.json` includes `/gen/*`). The public image gallery (`functions/api/gallery.js`, `tools/gallery.test.mjs`): on /image/ "Share to the gallery" names a picture `/api/image` made today (its edge-cache entry, never uploaded bytes), Newest and Most votes, one vote per connection a day, report and owner delete, 300 rows at most with old low-voted ones making room.
- **Character chat (`void-live-deploy/generators/chat/`, `lib/character.js`, `tools/character.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** perchance's AI character chat: three starter characters or your own (name, who they are, the scene, a greeting), a "what they remember" note, the chat and characters kept in `a2m.void.chars.v1`, "try another reply", a character shared as `#c=` (the chat never goes in the link). Replies come from `/api/answer` mode `character` (the same free answer model and the same 12-a-minute limit as any answer; the rules lead the system message, the sheet follows as data; sexual or hateful words and real people get no model call; nothing cached or written).
- **Story writer and the generators' AI text (`void-live-deploy/generators/story/`, `lib/story.js`, `tools/story.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** an idea, a genre and a length in, a story written part by part (continue, steer what happens next, try another last part, remove it, copy, download .txt), kept in `a2m.void.stories.v1`; `/api/answer` mode `story`. `{ai: ...}` in the list language (`lib/listgen.js` marks it, the editor fills it) asks mode `aitext`, cached at the edge by prompt and seed for a week. Both use the free answer model under the answer route's 12-a-minute limit. Also in the engine: `.presentTense` `.futureTense` `.negativeForm`, tap-to-reroll (`run(..., { spans: true })`, `tidySpans`, `reroll`), and "Download .html" carries the imported generators.
- **Comments (`functions/api/comments.js`, `lib/comments.js`, `lib/comments-ui.js`, `tools/comments.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** under a published generator (`gen:<id>`) and under /image/: plain text, a signed sum as the bot check (no sooner than 3 s, a hidden field bots fill), no links, 2 a minute and 15 a day per hashed connection, 1,000 a day in all, 300 kept a thread, three reports hide one, the owner deletes. A stronger check (Cloudflare Turnstile) needs keys from the owner.
- **Wheels, layouts, shared data and AI name generators (2026-10-25):** `{wheel: list}` in the list language picks a winner by the entries' odds and the editor spins a wheel to it (`lib/wheel.js` slices, plan, ease); `$layout = lines|cards|grid|table`; a published generator's shared data (`functions/api/gen-data.js`, `tools/gen-data.test.mjs`: 20 keys, 4 KB, `[shared.key]`, `{add: key}`, `{set: key = text}`, written only by the published text, owner clears); five built-in AI name generators (`lib/ai-generators.js`, `/generators/make/?t=<id>`) whose names come from `{ai: ...}`.
- **The daily picture for the reports (`lib/daily-art.js`, `lib/daily-photo.js`, `functions/api/daily-art.js`, `tools/daily-art.test.mjs`, `tools/daily-photo.test.mjs`, 2026-10-26):** owner: "we can have pictures that update daily too", then "no like actual pictures of the games". Pages call `dailyArt(kind, key)` (kind sports/news/finance, key a league, a section or a wrap, from closed lists) for an `<img src>`, `dailyArtImg` for its 16:9 box (no layout shift), and `dailyArtInfo` + `mountCredit` for the line under it. The illustration is painted once per kind, key and day for everyone from a prompt made only of our own words (no names, teams or logos), inside its own budget of 30 a day (Workers AI first, then Pollinations, counted in the shared total with the Art Forge and /image/, ceiling 70 a day); spent or resting, a deterministic SVG. `photo=1&q=<teams, venue, event>&date=` (sports) looks for a real photo: Wikimedia Commons, then Openverse (optional `OPENVERSE_CLIENT_ID`/`OPENVERSE_CLIENT_SECRET`), then Flickr (off until `FLICKR_API_KEY`), then TheSportsDB (off until `THESPORTSDB_KEY`), accepting only CC0, CC BY and CC BY-SA with author, licence link and source page shown ("Photo: <author>, CC BY 4.0, via Wikimedia Commons", when it was taken and whether it is from this game, a report link), kept per query and day for everyone; `video=<id>` adds a YouTube embed only when its oEmbed says an official channel owns it. Owner key: `?refresh=1`, `?diag=1` (what each source answered and why candidates were refused), `DELETE ?photo=<id>`. Playbook: `docs/playbooks/daily-art.md`.

- **Daily cap (`lib/daily-cap.js`, table `void_daily_cap(day, key, n)` made on first use, test `tools/daily-cap.test.mjs`, 2026-10-26):** one global counter for anything that spends the free allowance: `cap(env, key, limit)` is one atomic D1 upsert (`ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE SET n = n + 1 WHERE n < limit RETURNING n`) per UTC day and key, shared by every edge location; no D1 or an error is refused (fail closed). `capAll(env, [[key, limit], ...])` takes a place under each, hours first. Not KV (its writes are over the free allowance). Used by `/api/answer` mode `report`; the sports and finance reports use the same helper.
- **Verse reports on /news/ (`lib/news-report.js`, `/api/answer` mode `report`, `tools/news-report.test.mjs`, 2026-10-26, owner: "Verse can be the reporter"):** a lazily loaded tab: a plain summary per multi-source story, a daily brief across sections and what changed on followed topics since 06:00. Step 1 a deterministic draft from the feeds' headlines and summaries (sources named, figures that differ flagged), step 2 the free answer model polishes it, step 3 `problem()` throws the polish away unless every name, number and date is in the source text (no quote, no first person, no reporter): the draft is shown instead. Labelled "Written by Verse from <sources> · time". One generation per distinct source text, cached on the edge 3 hours for everyone; REPORT_PER_HOUR 10 and REPORT_PER_DAY 80 per edge location, then the draft.
