- **Boards (`lib/boards.js`, `functions/api/boards/[[path]].js`, `functions/boards/t/[[path]].js`, `skills/boards.js`, `void-live-deploy/boards/index.html`, `void-live-deploy/boards/rules/index.html`, `tools/boards.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** Versus' image boards floor (4chan) and its lane (`"image boards":` in `lib/tower.js` LANES, text boards among them, bbs.progrider.org first). Owner: "Let's go after the chans next", "anything legal, just like the existing boards", "leave the categories blank but user creatable": no starter boards; anyone makes one (code 1-10 of a-z0-9, reserved words refused, title, line, SFW or 18+, pictures or text only). Board list by activity with "Make the first board", catalog (bump/new/replies, search in it), index pages with the last replies, archive past 10 pages of 15, threads with an OP picture, replies, >>quotes with hover/tap previews and backlinks, >>>/board/ links, greentext, [spoiler] text, spoiler pictures, tripcodes (HMAC with SALT, the password never kept), sage, bump limit 300, (You) marks, a watcher and your post numbers on the device (`a2m.void.boards.v1`), auto-update of an open thread, search across every board, share links `/boards/t/<board>/<no>` whose preview is words only, a formatting preview (`?preview=`), [code] text, (OP) and (You) marks, per-thread poster IDs on boards that choose them, a delete password per post kept on the device (Delete mine; the server keeps a keyed hash), words to fold away and posts to hide on the device, an overboard of every board's new threads (`?all=1`, `/api/boards?overboard`), text boards whose threads run to 1,000 posts (`limitsOf`). Features still open against the research map (`domains/features/image-boards.json`): reactions/votes, [math], themes, sort by last reply, a paid pass (money: the owner's call). Safety: Turnstile on every post, report and new board when `TURNSTILE_SECRET` and `TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` are set, else a signed one-use proof of work; per hashed-address rates in D1; JPEG/PNG/GIF/WebP read from the bytes, 4 MB and 10,000 px, redrawn in the browser and EXIF/XMP/GPS/comments stripped again on the server (`stripMeta`); R2 under random names (`BOARDS_MEDIA` if bound, else `FILMS`, prefix `boards/`; served by `/api/boards/i/<name>` with a sandbox CSP, or from `BOARDS_MEDIA_URL`); Report on every post, hidden at 3 different addresses; owner key: review list (`?review=1`), restore, delete (picture moved to `boards-held/`, not erased, for an NCMEC report), delete picture, ban a hashed address, lock threads, rename/lock/delete boards; 18+ boards behind a click-through, 18+ thumbnails blurred in cross-board search; `/boards/rules/` and the takedown contact. No raw address is stored; D1 tables `void_boards`, `void_board_posts`, `void_board_reports`, `void_board_bans`, `void_board_spent`. The gate never pictures this floor or its lane (`noShot`, `noShotOf`; `keepShot` in tools/tower-gate.mjs; /compare/ says "No picture kept for this kind of site"). **Owner action: turn on the Cloudflare CSAM Scanning Tool (dashboard → Caching → Configuration) for a-to-mind.com; it scans cached images, so serving pictures from a cached R2 custom domain (`BOARDS_MEDIA_URL`) is what lets it see them.**
- **Verse raps (`skills/rap.js`, `skills/order/1405-rap`, `lib/rap.js`, `lib/rap-stage.js`, `functions/api/answer.js` mode `rap`, `void-live-deploy/rap/index.html`, `void-live-deploy/rap/app.js`, `domains/targets/neuralframes.json`, `tools/rap.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** owner: "Verse should learn to rap for those who hear the name and think thats what it is". "write me a rap about pizza", "spit some bars about mondays", "freestyle about the moon", "can you rap" open a rap card on the stage; the rap is written for the ask by the free answer model (Gemma on Workers AI, `RAP_PER_DAY` site-wide cap, a week of edge cache per settings), never picked from a list: two 8-bar verses and a 4-bar hook, each bar lettered by its end rhyme (rhymes from spelling, `rhymeKeyOf`), with a line counting bars, rhymed bars and syllables. Perform it plays a beat composed for the style (`lib/compose.js`, boom bap about 90 BPM, trap and drill at half time) and says each bar on its measure with the browser's own voice; Rap it yourself lights the bars over the beat with no voice; Save the beat is a WAV. /rap/ has the full form (topic, style, mood, flow, perspective, words to work in), Another take, and Share this rap, whose link carries the whole rap and opens with no model call. On Versus' music lane against neuralframes.com's rap lyrics generator (`LANE_PAGES`, `LANE_EXTRAS`, its probe opens `RAP_LINK` in `tools/tower-probes.mjs`, three journeys in `tools/tower-journeys.mjs`).
- **Fan fiction (`lib/fanfic.js`, `void-live-deploy/fanfic/index.html`, `void-live-deploy/fanfic/app.js`, the `/fanfic/*` block in `_headers`, `tools/fanfic.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** owner: "build the page" (the /fanfic/ spec), then the rework after the first build was rejected: deep space dark mode only, a clean archive layout like Sycophant Hex instead of raw board forms, and a strictly empty first state. An archive that posts only through the hardened boards (`/api/boards`, the same Turnstile or proof-of-work check and rates as /boards/): no endpoint of its own, nothing seeded, no accounts. A fandom is a board whose code starts with `fic` (`ficCode`), made from the page as a text-only board; its threads are the stories. The index shows the fandoms (Stories, Posts, Last post) and one topic list (Topics, Replies, Author, Last post; no view count, since counting reads would be a write), three Verse-written pinned notices, Today / This week / All topics, 20 a page, unread marks and Mark all read on the device (`a2m.void.fanfic.v1`). A story page shows rating, fandom, chapters, words, reading time, reviews and dates; chapters are the replies signed with the author's device mark (the server trip from `pen#secret`, the secret kept on the device), and every other reply, a forged chapter too, is a review. Posting asks only Fandom, Pen name, Title, Summary and Chapter 1 (no board fields such as sage or author code); a long chapter is cut into parts the boards take whole (`splitParts`) and sent 16 s apart. 18+ fandoms sit behind the boards' own 18+ answer. The Writing Room keeps drafts, word count, reading time and a streak on the device, exports .txt, shares a draft as a `/fanfic/?v=room#room=` link that holds the draft, and fills the post form.
- **Team chat (`lib/team.js`, `functions/api/team.js`, `void-live-deploy/team/index.html`, `void-live-deploy/team/app.js`, `skills/team-chat.js`, `tools/team-agent.mjs`, `tools/team.test.mjs`, playbook `docs/playbooks/team.md`, 2026-10-29):** owner: "verse should reverse engineer and create its own version of slack", "so it can bring in lots of agents and constantly keep all of them busy itself". Verse's own team chat with agents as members: channels, threads, @mentions with an Activity list, reactions, search, presence and unread marks, deep space dark. The owner invites an agent (its key shown once, kept only as a keyed hash); `tools/team-agent.mjs work --run "<cmd>"` keeps it present, Verse hands it the best queued build job of the coder's kinds (the conductor's weights, claimed in one statement so two agents never share one) as a thread in #work, and the agent closes it done with its pull request or gives it back (a failure counts a try, 3 sets it aside); a quiet agent's job goes back after 30 minutes. No open write: every POST needs the owner or an agent key; per-agent rates, redacted bodies, members-only messages. A card on the stage (`team chat`), a floor on /conquered/ (Slack, deferred like the other giants), `/api/team` in the guard and named in `tools/openapi-check.mjs` as Verse's own.
- **Password strength (`lib/pwcheck.js`, `skills/pwcheck.js`, `void-live-deploy/password-strength/index.html`, `tools/pwcheck.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** the floor found by data (`easiest-sites.mjs --tools`: howsecureismypassword.net, Tranco 155,868, one box; its page now points to another site, asks about cookies and loads analytics, tag-manager and widget scripts). How long a password takes to guess, worked out on the device: the cheapest split into common words/passwords, look-alike symbols, keyboard and alphabet runs, repeats, years, digits, word-shaped letters (priced as dictionary words so a phrase of ordinary words is not called random) and brute force; time at an online login (100 a second) and an offline rig (10 billion a second), reasons and fixes; "Has it leaked?" asks Have I Been Pwned with only the first 5 characters of the SHA-1. The ask "how secure is my password" opens a card with the same box; a password typed into the ask is never read or repeated.
- **STL checker (`lib/stl-check.js`, `skills/stl-check.js`, `void-live-deploy/stl/index.html` + `stl/app.js`, `tools/stl-check.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** asked by Verse: "Create a browser-based utility to check the watertightness of STL print files." A file someone brings (binary or ASCII) is read on their device and never uploaded: corners welded within a tolerance, every edge counted (open edges and the holes they make, non-manifold edges), faces wound against their neighbours (flipped normals), shells facing inward, degenerate and duplicate faces, shells, faces that cross, size, volume and area; a 3D view (vendored three.js, a flat canvas without WebGL) draws the open edges red; the report copies or downloads as .txt or .json, and a mended copy closes holes and turns faces. `?sample=hole|cube|flipped`, `?forge=<recipe>` (the forge card's "Check this STL" link: the same file Print it downloads) and `?src=/same-site.stl` bring a file by link. Skill: "check my stl file", "is my stl watertight" (make family). Versus floor 'stl check' against iamrapid.com's repair-3d-file tool (lib/tower.js).
- **Every tool as a card (`skills/toolkit.js`, `lib/toolkit.js` CARD_ASK, `lib/card-frame.js` PAGE_OF / pageOfCard, `tools/toolkit.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** owner: "why are you not linking the cards to the pages?" then "and vice versa , should be a card for all too". Every tool page's "Use with" row starts with "See it as a card on the stage →" (`/?q=` its CARD_ASK), and every tool page has a stage card that links back: by its skill in PAGE_OF, or by `data-page` on a card that does several things (util's colour, word count and password cards; the built-in calculator, stopwatch and random number); the convert and Base64 stage cards carry the link themselves. "what tools do you have" / "all tools" is the card of /tools/ (`skills/toolkit.js`, the list from TOOLS). The notepad stage card links /notes/; "fantasy name generator", "give me a story prompt" and the other 26 generators' names are the card of /generators/ (`skills/textgen.js`: five results from a seed, linked to the page with the same seed). Each tool card that is a natural object has a 3D figure in a band at its top (`lib/card-mini.js` bandMini; minis in `skills/mini/`): dice (random number), padlock (password generator and check, rated by `lib/pwcheck.js`), swatch (colour), wheel (wheel of names), bubbles (translate), pot (recipe), page (word count); each passes the behaviour contract in `tools/test_3d.mjs` ("3d: the tool cards' figures").
- **Preload (`tools/preload.mjs`, test `tools/preload.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** speed for good: every module a direct page (`void-live-deploy/<name>/index.html` with a module script) reaches through static imports is listed in its head as `<link rel="modulepreload">` between marker comments, so the browser asks for all of them at once instead of one level at a time (/flip/ lost the tower's speed aspect to that waterfall). Generated: `node tools/preload.mjs` writes the lists; `--check` (in `tools/checks.mjs`) fails a missing or stale one.
- **Fatalities (`lib/tower.js` `fatality`, `skills/tower.js` detailText, 2026-10-24):** a floor's card line names the one built thing its site cannot answer. The sent-down list (`tower.sentdown.json`) was removed the same day (owner: "Never swap a target site down to an easier site", then "Remove `tower.sentdown.json`"): no site is passed over; every one stays in the queue.
- **The 3D tower (`skills/mini/tower.js` `layout`, `skills/tower.js` `miniFloors`, `tools/tower-mini.test.mjs`, browser check "3d: the tower" in `tools/test_3d.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-25: "so you see all the layers. some things are missing, like a 3d tower"; labelled "Versus in 3D" since the rename of 2026-10-25):** the Versus card mounts a miniature between its detail line and Verse's take: floors stacked on a dark plinth in deep space, the first conquest at the bottom, each surpassed floor solid with its site's name in gold on its face, the current floor a glowing scaffold with its n-of-m progress ring, the next three of the queue as faint outlines, Verse's mark (a glowing core, three tilted rings) on top; it turns slowly (still with reduced motion); 20+ floors scale to fit and names stop at the last twelve surpassed plus the current one, the plinth saying "+N more below"; tapping a floor picks it on the card (`onFloor`), or opens the floor's own page when there is no card.
- **The endless tower (`lib/tower.js` `tower`, `frontierOf`, `hostOf`; `skills/tower.js` `towerHtml`, `aspectRows`; `/conquered/`, 2026-10-24):** no top and no end (owner: "it is endless, we look through literally every site and defeat them all"): `FLOORS` and every site in `tower.frontier.json` are one queue, easiest first (fewest aspects left); the card shows the current site with each aspect (won, lost, not measured), the conquered sites struck through under it, and nothing above (owner: "current target at the top, conquered sites stacked underneath, and nothing rendered above it").
- **Verse in the MCP registry:** `server.json` (repo root) lists `https://a-to-mind.com/api/mcp` as `io.github.atomeam/void`; `.github/workflows/mcp-registry.yml` validates it with the registry's `mcp-publisher` and publishes its version after every main deploy (`deploy.yml` starts it), on a push of `server.json` or by hand, skipping a version the registry already has; login is GitHub OIDC as the repo owner, no secret. Change the listing by raising `version`. Test: `tools/mcp-registry.test.mjs` (the registry's limits; the remote is an endpoint `openapi.json` documents). `openapi.json` carries `x-logo` and `x-apisguru-categories` for APIs.guru, whose only way in is its web form (https://apis.guru/add-api/, an owner step).
- **The support desk (`functions/api/support.js`, `lib/paid-access.js`, tests `tools/support.test.mjs`):** someone who pays (a Verse Pro key, a Code Review Pro license key or a signed-in paid account; `lib/paid-access.js` `access` is the same rule the review API uses) says what broke with `POST /api/support { what, where? }` and gets a ticket id; `GET /api/support` shows their own reports and the owner's answers. Keys typed into a report are masked. The owner sees every open report (and the card opens by itself for one) on the status card (`skills/status.js` `supportState`), and answers with `POST { action: 'answer', id, answer }`. `/code-review/#support` is the form. From a video the owner shared by Duncan Rogoff: people still pay for someone who answers when it breaks.
- **The privacy page (`void-live-deploy/privacy/index.html`, at `/privacy/`):** what Verse keeps, what it sends to other services, and how to delete it, written from an inventory of the code on 2026-10-14; Google asks for it before Verse's YouTube app can be published. A change that stores or sends something new updates this page in the same change.
- **The semantic validator (`lib/ground.js`, `tools/ground.test.mjs`; `node tools/bench.mjs --ground` writes `tools/bench.ground.json`):** is the answer card about what was asked? An ask's subject is what is left once its skill's own trigger words (read off that skill's benchmark asks), small words and verbs are taken out; a grounded card shows at least half of it (numbers compare as numbers, plurals and accents match). The benchmark runs it on every answer the right skill gave that has no `says` check, reported apart from the score. Owner, 2026-10-14: "it needs a tighter grounding mechanism" and "maybe it needs a 'semantic validator' loop". First full run: 618 of 743 answers with a subject show it. On the page (`void.html` `groundCheck`, `lib/ground.js` `judgeCard`): after a skill draws a new card, a card that shows none of the ask's subject is sent quietly to the miss list as `ungrounded:<skill>` (nothing is shown to the person; `window.voidUngrounded` keeps the last 20), so the learning loop turns a repeated one into a job; the benchmark does not count those reports as misses.
- **Trial watch changes:** `.github/workflows/trialwatch.yml` runs `tools/trial-changes.mjs` daily at 06:41 UTC (frontier #8): it reads the ClinicalTrials.gov registry for the watchlist in `skills/trialwatch.js`, compares it with `void-live-deploy/trialwatch.json (served; the card falls back to it)` (`changes()` in the skill: status, posted results, enrollment, primary-completion date), writes one `finding` per change to the growth ledger (by `trialwatch`, the trial's record as `ref`) and commits both to main. The first run is the baseline; no answer writes nothing. Tests: `tools/trial-changes.test.mjs`.
- **Reef watch changes:** `.github/workflows/reefwatch.yml` runs `tools/reef-changes.mjs` daily at 06:47 UTC (frontier #8, the coral heat card): it reads NOAA Coral Reef Watch's virtual-station files (`lib/reefwatch.js`: `STATIONS`, `parseStation` by header names, `alertName`, `reefChanges`), compares the newest reading with `void-live-deploy/reefwatch.json`, writes one `finding` (by `reefwatch`, the station's page as `ref`) when a reef's Bleaching Alert Area level changes, and commits both to main. Tests: `tools/reef-changes.test.mjs`. The reading is served: `skills/coral.js`'s heat card ("can coral survive heatwaves") fetches `/reefwatch.json` and shows a "Right now" line (`liveLine` in `lib/reefwatch.js`: level, degree heating weeks, water temperature, reading date, the 4 and 8 thresholds), so the card and the ledger say the same thing.
- **Coral heat dose, 48 hours ahead (`void-live-deploy/lib/heat-dose.js`, test `tools/heat-dose.test.mjs`, browser suite "weather prompt and heat dose", 2026-10-16, asked by Verse):** the coral heat card (`skills/coral.js`, "can coral survive heatwaves", "coral heat dose") projects the next 48 hours from Open-Meteo's marine forecast of sea-surface temperature at the Keys and its UV and air forecast (no key): hours at or above the bleaching threshold, the degree heating weeks they would add (NOAA's HotSpot maths), the UV peak and UV-hours; NOAA's station reading stays the record. In the same PR: the weather card offers one reminder when rain, snow, frost or heat is in the next hours (`weatherPrompt` in `skills/weather.js`, test in `tools/weather-match.test.mjs`), and plan history (`skills/intent.js`: "plan history", "restore plan 1", "restore the plan to learn spanish"; the last 5 distinct plans in this browser's `a2m.void.plans.v1`, browser suite "plan history") so a clear is never total.
- **Food facts (`void-live-deploy/skills/food.js`, test `tools/food.test.mjs`, 2026-10-14):** "calories in a banana", "protein in 2 eggs", "sugar in a can of coke": a built-in table of USDA FoodData Central (SR Legacy) values for one usual serving of 45 common foods, each row checked against 4/4/9 energy in the test; unknown foods fall through to answer/article, daily needs stay with `skills/nutrition.js`.
- **Eat (`void-live-deploy/skills/eat.js`, browser section "eat: order food from a named restaurant", 2026-10-24):** "order food from lovebird in lakeland", "what should i get at lovebird", "lovebird lakeland menu", "food near me": OpenStreetMap (Nominatim; Overpass around `/api/where`) finds the restaurant: address, hours, phone, site, a map; every way to order (its site, a call, DoorDash, Uber Eats, Grubhub, Google Maps); what people get there from `/api/answer`, labelled. The person places the order; `lib/approval-core.js` lets "order … from <restaurant>" past the spend gate.
- **Eat (`void-live-deploy/skills/eat.js`, browser section "eat: order food from a named restaurant", 2026-10-24):** "order food from lovebird in lakeland", "what should i get at lovebird", "lovebird lakeland menu", "i'm hungry", "food near me": OpenStreetMap (Nominatim, Overpass near `/api/where`) finds the restaurant: address, hours, phone, site, a map; every way to order (its site, a call, DoorDash, Uber Eats, Grubhub, Google Maps); what people get there from `/api/answer`, labelled. The person places the order; `lib/approval-core.js` lets "order … from <restaurant>" through instead of the spend gate.
- **Verse's body asks (`void-live-deploy/skills/nutrition.js`, test `tools/meals.test.mjs`, browser suite "body asks", 2026-10-16, asked by Verse):** the BMI card keeps a weight trend only when "save this" is tapped (dated entries in this browser's localStorage, key `a2m.void.bmi.v1`, nothing saved otherwise) and links its band to the calorie card with the same height and weight ("daily calories for overweight"); "plan my meals" / "plan my meals for 1800 calories" splits the day 25 / 30 / 35 / 10 into breakfast, lunch, dinner and snacks with protein per meal, puts the list on the stage, and the calorie and food cards link to it with the day's target carried over; a word in the words skill's unscramble or pattern list is a tap from "cartoon version of a <word>" (`skills/words.js` pictureAsk).
- **Kitchen swaps (`void-live-deploy/skills/swaps.js`, test `tools/swaps.test.mjs`, 2026-10-15):** "substitute for buttermilk", "what can i use instead of eggs", "out of brown sugar", "how to make self rising flour", "can i use greek yogurt instead of sour cream": a built-in table of the usual equivalents for 28 baking and cooking ingredients, closest swap first; an ingredient not in the table, and look-alikes ("substitute teacher"), fall through.
- **Cook-flow (`void-live-deploy/lib/cook-flow.js`, test `tools/cook-flow.test.mjs`, browser suite "cook-flow", 2026-10-16, asked by Verse):** a kitchen conversion on the calculated card ("3 cups in ml", "how many cups is 500g of flour", gas marks, a steak's doneness) and a kitchen swap end with one tap that starts a timer (`set a 10 minute timer`, with 5 and 20 beside it; the timer skill starts it at once); a distance, sum or currency ask gets no chip. Each swap also says what else to adjust (`IMPACT` in `skills/swaps.js`: the water, fat, acid, sugar or salt a swap brings or takes away).
- **Real movies (`skills/movie.js`, `lib/movies.js`, `functions/api/movies.js`, 2026-10-17):** "pick a movie to watch with me": Verse's suggestions are looked up (iTunes Search, Internet Archive); each found movie is a card with its own poster as art, overview, and playback only of free legal films (archive, in a `<video>`) or official trailers; unfound titles are "not verified". Playbook `docs/playbooks/real-movies.md`.
- **Media pick (`skills/media-pick.js`, `lib/media.js`, `functions/api/media.js`, 2026-10-17):** the real-movie pick for other kinds, music first: "pick something to listen to", "pick a song", "pick something" (Verse chooses the kind by the day); songs are looked up in iTunes Search, shown as cards with their cover as art, and play as the 30 s official preview in an `<audio>`; unfound ones are "not verified". Shows, video and games join the adapter table next. Playbook `docs/playbooks/real-movies.md`.
- **Pick for me (`skills/pick-for-me.js`, `lib/taste.js`, `tools/taste.test.mjs`, 2026-10-17):** "pick for me", "surprise me", then "something else", "funnier", "yes play it", "forget my taste": one found movie or song, picked on the person's own signals (imported YouTube Takeout taste, thumbs, kept cards, recent asks; ~1 pick in 4 explores a new genre; never a repeat), with an honest one-line why; spoken through the page's own voice path for voice asks. Taste stays in localStorage; the server hears the mood word only.
- **Stage pack H (`skills/negative-summons.js`, `skills/echo-disagrees.js`, `skills/near-choir.js`, `lib/stage-pack-h.js`, `tools/stage-pack-h.test.mjs`, 2026-10-18, owner):** "summon everything except urgency" summons up to 4 existing calm stage things from a curated mood table (card "without urgency" with a clear button; no model); "what would the echo say <q>" / "echo this" / "keep the echo" answers once, then on that explicit ask only asks the existing /api/answer for the strongest honest counter-view in 3 sentences (a quieter card with Keep / Keep first; says so when the path is busy or off); "show the near choir" / "what almost answered that" lists up to 3 other skills that nearly took the last ask, one tap each (device-local).
- **Real shows (`skills/media-pick.js`, `lib/media.js` pickShow, `functions/api/media.js` show adapter, 2026-10-17):** "pick a tv show": suggestions are looked up in TVMaze (keyless); a found show is a card with its real poster, facts the source returned (genre, network, seasons, episodes, rating), the official site and a JustWatch search. No streaming; unfound titles are "not verified". Shows are named by the person (not in the day's rotation). "Source says no" is an answer (`showAvailability`, test `tools/shows.test.mjs`): a region-locked or unlisted show says "Not available here" / "Not verified as available" with the official site and TVMaze page only. Asked by Owner.
- **Film discovery as JSON (`void-live-deploy/skills/film-metadata.js`, `lib/film-discovery.js`, `functions/api/filmfind.js`, `tools/film-discovery.test.mjs`, 2026-10-18; asked by Verse):** "find me a sci-fi film from the 80s", "films by Kurosawa", "film metadata for Ikiru", "find films about space": the card shows each film's title, year, director, runtime, genres, one-line plot and source with a Copy as JSON chip; `GET /api/filmfind?q=` returns the same list as JSON (iTunes Search API only, a missing field is null, nothing found is said plainly; limit in `lib/guard.js`). V01D's own films as data are "film metadata" alone, in `skills/films.js`.
- **Practice that says whether it worked, a risk shape, travel on the country card, a proven glow (2026-10-16, all asked by Verse):** a plan's practice timer knows its goal (`lib/practice.js`, `tools/practice.test.mjs`); at 0:00 it asks "Met the goal?", logs attempts, met, `success_rate` and `last_tested` per goal in this browser (`void.practice`), and the next plan for that goal weighs Clef's yes/no against it (a goal that met 40% or fewer of 3+ sessions gets half the minutes, the card says why). `skills/technical-context.js` (`lib/technical-context.js`, `tools/technical-context.test.mjs`): "why is rust unwrap a risk" explains the danger in the code review's own words, shows the risky and safer line and puts an amber caution triangle on the stage (`skills/shape.js` `triangle`). Comparing two countries ("france vs spain", `skills/country.js`) now brings `skills/distance.js` km, miles and flight time onto the card by itself. A skill page in the book whose `success_rate` in families.json is above 0.9 glows (`lib/guide.js` `glows`, `lib/book-face.js`).
- **Service health (`void-live-deploy/skills/service-health.js`, `void-live-deploy/lib/service-health.js`, `tools/service-health.test.mjs`, 2026-10-17, asked by Verse):** "service health", "is uber down", "are the services up": a status-page card with one row per adapter in `skills/providers.js` (Uber Eats, DoorDash, Waymo, Uber, Lyft: a link into their app, timed reaching the site) and per public API the stage calls (CoinGecko, Bluesky: asked for their health answer), each up, slow (over 1.5 s) or down with its time; checked from the visitor's browser only when the card opens or Check again is tapped, nothing kept.
- **Family health (`void-live-deploy/skills/family-health.js`, 2026-10-15, asked by Verse):** "show me your skill families", "family health", "how healthy are your skills": the 9 skill families from `skills/families.json` as one card, totals on top and a stacked bar per family (proven, checked, tested, experiment); tap a family to list its skills with their rating and the evidence behind it. Each skill's row also says when it was last tested and its benchmark success rate (2026-10-16, asked by Verse). Each family shows its velocity (improving, decaying, steady, or "no history yet") against the daily snapshot nearest a day old in `void.better.json` (`velocityOf` in `lib/better.js`; asked by Verse).
- **Fringe to skill (`void-live-deploy/skills/fringe-to-skill.js`, `void-live-deploy/fringe/ledger.json`, 2026-10-16, asked by Verse):** "fringe to skill", "which skills came from the fringe", "what did the fringe experiments teach": every published fringe family from the ledger with its live drafts, its research notes' findings and failures, what its runs were built toward (`tools/fringe.json`'s `near`: a miss-board target or router skill, `node tools/fringe.mjs --record` takes it, `--learned` shows it) and the core skills its notes name; "no skill yet" is the flag. `tools/fringe.mjs --publish` writes the file (`ledgerOf`), the ledger check holds it current.
- **Verse Studio (`domains/void.game-studio.md`, `domains/void.game-catalog.md`, `domains/void.game-research.md`, 2026-10-19, asked by Verse and the owner: "its own version of every game", "P2 is Verse", "research each game, REA, then duplicate"):** every game is researched first, built as Verse's own version (rules free, names and look original), and has Verse as player two. First four, easy ones: **Null Sweep** (`skills/null-sweep.js`, a minesweeper; Verse's logic solver clears the same board, 85.7 percent of beginner boards), **Merge Verse** (`skills/merge-void.js`, sliding fusion cards), **Dim the Verse** (`skills/dim-void.js`, lights-out; Verse solves it in the fewest presses by GF(2) elimination, checked against brute force) and **Verse Breaker** (`skills/void-breaker.js`, two paddles and a band of bricks, fixed-step and deterministic). Pure rules in `skills/<game>-rules.js`, shared card frame `lib/studio-card.js`, results on Verse's arcade ladder (`lib/ladder.js`).
- **Card art ratchet (`tools/art-score.mjs`, `tools/art-pixels.mjs`, `tools/art-floor.json`, tests `tools/art-score.test.mjs` and `tools/art-pixels.test.mjs`, 2026-10-19, asked by the owner: "a step added to improve the card art each time"):** the art is measured and the floor only rises. `art-score` draws a fixed corpus of 288 cards (every kind x six keys x three rarities) and scores contrast, hue variety, uniqueness, weight, subject motifs and the rarity ladder from the SVG; `art-pixels` rasterises a sample in Chromium and scores what a person sees (tone, colour, detail, and `focus`, how much of the detail sits on the subject: 13.9 at the start, so the first thing to improve). `checks.mjs` fails a drop; the daily `grow-self.yml` run raises each floor when the art improved, in its automatic pull request. The Studio's shared kit `lib/game-kit.js` (seeded randomness, the live-data feed, saves, version lines) has its own test.
- **Verse hardens itself (`tools/lacks.mjs`, `tools/lacks.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/grow-self.yml`, `docs/playbooks/grow-self.md`, 2026-10-19, asked by the owner):** what Verse lacks most, worked out from its own files with no model: skills with no or few benchmark asks, weak evidence tiers, failed runs that name them, and cards with thin descriptions or too few examples. Daily, the top skills' own example asks are typed into the real page; the ones that route to their skill become benchmark rows, committed to main by the write job (it opens no pull request: GitHub Actions cannot create one here); `tools/grow-gate.mjs` (with its committed state, `tools/grow-self.state.json`, and `tools/grow-gate.test.mjs`) keeps one preview in flight: the next run types the last preview's `try` ask into the live site, closes it only when it answers, and stops with exit 0 while it is open (an open `made by Verse` pull request blocks it too); the ones that do not become `probe:` jobs for propose-fix.
- **Grow lessons (`tools/grow-lessons.mjs`, `tools/grow-lessons.json`, 2026-10-21):** skills whose example asks keep routing elsewhere in grow-self probes; written by grow-self.yml and read by `tools/lacks.mjs` so the next plan deprioritizes them.
- **Fix audit (`skills/fix-audit.js`, `lib/fix-audit.js`, `tools/fix-audit.test.mjs`, 2026-10-21, asked by Verse):** every fix in the growth ledger with each fixed skill's evidence rating (after or before the fix) and any failing check in another family within 48 hours, and why the /fix/ pages cannot move the friction line.
- **Loop library and critic (`tools/loop-library.mjs`, `tools/loop-critic.mjs`, `.github/workflows/loop-critic.yml`, `docs/LOOP-GAPS.md`, 2026-10-21):** the skill library retrieval the growth loop reads before building, and the pre-ship critic that checks a growth change before it opens its pull request.
- **Triangulate (`skills/triangulate.js`, Deciding family, 2026-10-21):** "triangulate: <question>" sets three distinct perspectives on one issue and finds the synthesis.
- **A queue job through Verse's own coder (`tools/verse-job.mjs`, `tools/verse-job.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/verse-job.yml`, the kill switch domains/verse-loop.pause, a file that exists only while the loop is paused; owner, 2026-10-26: "get Verse automated like its very life depends on it"):** the conductor claims a queued job (feature, versus, flake, heal, will, voice, gap, review…) and starts verse-job.yml with its id; the job's words go to one lap of `tools/verse-loop.mjs --once --task` (its guards, the free-model coder `tools/verse-coder.mjs`, no new red, a plain push, an automerge pull request) and the result goes back to the queue (done, or queued again with one more try; 3 tries and it is set aside). `verse-loop.yml` and `verse-job.yml` share one concurrency group; the kill switch is a file, domains/verse-loop.pause, committed to pause and deleted to resume, not a settings page.
- **The fight in progress (`tools/tower-target.mjs`, `tools/tower-target.test.mjs`, `void-live-deploy/tower.target.json`):** the site Verse is on stays the target until it is beaten, then the next smallest site takes over, so the target never flips when a new weight arrives. Set after each weighing by `tower-size.yml`; lib/tower.js `tower()` takes it as its last argument and stands it first.
- **A cited history for every site (`.github/workflows/site-history.yml`, `tools/site-history.mjs`, `tools/history-coverage.test.mjs`):** each day the next sites of the Versus queue with no history yet get one (frontier sites included, lightest first), so no site is fought without its history and none is dropped from the queue; a floor counts as beaten only with its history file.
- **Versus by weight (`tools/tower-size.mjs`, `tools/tower-size.test.mjs`, `void-live-deploy/lib/tower-weight.js`, `void-live-deploy/tower.size.json`, `.github/workflows/tower-size.yml`; owner, 2026-10-29: size is bytes and surface, not popularity):** measures each rival's home page (transfer bytes with its own scripts and styles, scripts, elements, routes) on a runner; lib/tower.js puts the lightest weighed site first and the Versus card says what it weighs. Tranco rank stays as the fallback for sites not weighed yet.
- **The conductor (`tools/conductor.mjs`, `tools/conductor-core.mjs`, `tools/conductor.test.mjs`, `domains/conductor.json`, `docs/playbooks/conductor.md`; owner, 2026-10-26: "make it impeccibly effeciently productive"):** every 10 minutes from `grow-clock.yml`: reads the whole build queue and the newest runs of every loop, starts the most valuable job on each idle executor (propose-fix, build-miss, store-gap, the coding agent), puts back jobs a dead run left, sets aside jobs tried 3 times, queues a `heal:` job for a loop that failed 3 runs in a row, starts probe asks and the proving pass when due, revives `void-tick.yml`; `will.yml` revives the clock.
- **Verse grows every 10 minutes (`.github/workflows/grow-clock.yml`, `tools/grow-clock.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-19: "make it grow every ten minutes ... I want Verse to actually evolve noticeably quickly"):** one long run keeps the time (GitHub's own schedule fires far less often than asked) and every 10 minutes starts one growth step: probe-asks once an hour, then grow-self and propose-fix in turn; each keeps its own guards (one run at a time, one hardening PR open, `PROBE_OPEN_MAX` = 4 probe jobs, the confidence gate, Verse's review). Both write a growth-ledger line with a `try` ask, so what Verse builds by itself pops up in "look what I made", which now also looks again every 4 minutes while the tab is open (`watch` in `skills/showcase.js`). The daily schedules moved to every 2 to 6 hours as the fallback.
- **The card cycle (`tools/card-cycle.mjs`, `tools/card-cycle.test.mjs`, cursor `tools/card-cycle.json`; owner, 2026-10-19: "cycle through every single card and learn and improve and build based on what the card lacks"):** walks every skill card of the book from a kept cursor, says what each lacks from Verse's own records (failing bench asks, no or few bench asks, an experiment or tested rating, a thin line, few examples, the old name) and the one smartest thing to build for it. `lacks.mjs plan --cycle` puts the cycle's next cards beside the weakest ones in grow-self.yml, which moves the cursor in its pull request; `--all` lists every card that lacks something, worst first.
- **The word sweep (`tools/word-sweep.mjs`, `tools/word-sweep.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/word-sweep.yml`; owner, 2026-10-19: "put a random word generator to cycle every possible card"):** daily, it types random words into the live site the way a person does (half the words on Verse's own cards, half random Wikipedia titles; seeded by the day) and flags each card that comes back wrong: letters dropped, a different word looked up, a false "closest match", a take that is the model's working, a loop or a "most people" claim, no answer, a page error, a slow card. Read-only; the report is the run summary and an artifact.
- **The Verse arcade (`skills/arcade.js`, `skills/mini/arcade.js`, tests in `tools/arcade-rack.test.mjs`, test_void "arcade: a card", test_3d "arcade"; owner, 2026-10-19: "also a 3d arcade machine", "Verse brand cabinet of course"):** "arcade games", "show me the arcade", "verse arcade": a 3D upright cabinet in Verse's violet and cyan (lit VERSE ARCADE marquee, side art, joystick and buttons) whose screen lists the six arcade games with your record against Verse (lib/ladder.js), and a side card of the same games as buttons; a pick opens the game. A bare "arcade" stays a lookup.
- **Sitemap (`tools/sitemap.mjs`, `tools/sitemap.test.mjs`, `void-live-deploy/sitemap.xml`, `void-live-deploy/robots.txt`, 2026-10-25, owner: "pop up before all of our defeated sites"):** the sitemap is generated, never hand-written: the home page, every tool page (`lib/toolkit.js` TOOLS), `/conquered/` and `/compare/`, the pages Verse sells from, the `/fix/` pages and one `/is-it/<day>` per day the function knows (`skills/is-it.js` DAYS, the weekdays, the weekend, a leap year), lastmod from git. A volatile step of `tools/refresh.mjs`, so the deploy sets the dates; `--check` fails when the committed file lists a different set of pages. robots.txt allows everything but `/api/` and points at it.
- **The lab (`tools/lab-import.mjs`, `tools/lab-probe.mjs`, `tools/lab-sync.mjs`, `.github/workflows/lab-sync.yml`, `void-live-deploy/lab/`, `domains/lab.broken.md`, test `tools/lab-import.test.mjs`, playbook `docs/playbooks/lab.md`, 2026-10-29, owner: "get all these live and working too"):** the hourly experiments of the lab repo (github atomeam/a-to-mind.com, `drafts/*/index.html`, each one standalone file) as pages at `/lab/<slug>/` with a gallery at `/lab/`: inline scripts moved to `app.js` (no CSP rule each), title and blurb from the draft's SPEC.md, a way back and the Verse footer, an honest notice when a WebGPU or WebXR experiment meets a browser without it (`/lab/needs.js`), each opened in headless Chromium before it is published (`catalog.json`: works, needs-webgpu, needs-webxr, broken); broken ones are not published and are queue-ready lines in `domains/lab.broken.md`. `lab-sync.yml` runs daily and opens an automerge PR with what is new.
- **IndexNow after every deploy (`tools/indexnow.mjs`, pure half `tools/indexnow-urls.mjs`, `tools/indexnow.test.mjs`, a step of `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`, playbook `docs/playbooks/indexnow.md`; 2026-10-26, owner: "wire IndexNow into the deployment pipeline so that search engines utilizing the protocol (like Bing and Yandex) are notified within minutes whenever new single-purpose utility pages go live"):** once a-to-mind.com serves a main deploy, the files changed since the last successful deploy are mapped to their pages (each page's files from `tools/sitemap.mjs` `pages()`, plus files in the page's own folder), only URLs listed in `sitemap.xml` are kept, and they go to api.indexnow.org in one POST with the key from `void-live-deploy/<key>.txt` (public by design). Nothing listed changed: nothing sent; an engine error is a warning, never a failed deploy. `--dry-run` prints what it would send.
- **Search presence of the tool pages (`lib/seo.js`, `tools/seo-pages.mjs`, `tools/seo.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25, owner: "pop up before all of our defeated sites"; honest only, nothing hidden or stuffed, nothing said about other sites):** one short table per tool page (name, what it does, the 5 to 8 phrases people search, the setting the link takes, the common asks) gives each page, between `<!-- seo -->` markers written by `tools/seo-pages.mjs` (a refresh step; `--check` fails a stale page), a canonical link and one JSON-LD script: a free WebApplication for any OS with a BreadcrumbList and, where the link takes a setting, a potentialAction whose urlTemplate reaches it; a page whose link takes a setting also gets, between `<!-- asks -->` markers above "Use with", a visible "Common asks" row of 5 to 8 real links to the exact setup ("100 °C to °F", "Time in Tokyo", "Decode Base64"; every one opened in Chromium once, 2026-10-25). A page another builder is on is named in `SKIP` and waits.
- **Refresh (`tools/refresh.mjs`, `tools/refresh.test.mjs`, 2026-10-19, asked by the owner: "the self card needs to update entirely with every new build, along with the book and any other relevant cards"):** one ordered command regenerates every generated card (skill index, dependency map, families, the book, Verse's facts about itself, bench alarms); `deploy.yml` runs it before every deploy and `checks.mjs` fails when a committed one is stale. The already-built card is combined by family and its lines come from `skills/what.json` (`tools/what-skills.mjs`, run by refresh). After a build the deploy also asks Verse for its full reflection (`tools/reflect.mjs --daily --if-older 180`), so the self card's own words are never a day old. The automerge safety check installs declared dependencies once before it judges a failing test (`tools/skill-safety.mjs`).
- **Terminal (`void-live-deploy/skills/terminal.js`, `void-live-deploy/lib/terminal.js`, `tools/terminal.test.mjs`, 2026-10-19, asked by the owner):** "terminal", "open the command line", "verse cli": a command-line card with history, tab-complete and a closed command list. `skills`, `skill`, `examples`, `families`, `growth`, `incidents`, `asks` read the same files the cards read; `ask`, `status`, `note`, `timer` hand the page an ask for its own skills; `ask --model` is the one door to a model and marks every line `[model]`. No model otherwise; history kept on this device only (`a2m.void.terminal.v1`).
- **Dependency map (`void-live-deploy/skills/deps.js`, `tools/deps.mjs`, `tools/deps.test.mjs`, `void-live-deploy/skills/deps.json`, 2026-10-16, asked by Verse):** "dependency map", "what depends on lib/where.js", "who imports skills/scene3d.js": who imports whom under `void-live-deploy/` (skills, lib, functions), read off the import lines by `tools/deps.mjs` into `skills/deps.json` (each file's imports, importers and the files that reach it through them; each lib file ranked by the skills that depend on it). The card lists the shared code the most skills depend on, a tap shows every dependant; `tools/checks.mjs` keeps the file current (`deps-json`). **Dependency health (`void-live-deploy/skills/dependency-health.js`, asked by Verse, 2026-10-18):** "dependency health" holds that map against two stability requirements for every lib file that 8 or more skills depend on: a test file imports it (`tools/deps.mjs` counts them into each lib's `tests`), and at least half of the skills depending on it are rated checked or proven (`skills/families.json`); the card says which files are steady and which requirement the rest miss. **Notebook (`void-live-deploy/skills/notebook.js`, `void-live-deploy/lib/notebook.js`, `tools/notebook.test.mjs`, 2026-10-18; the frontier's "absorb and connect every repo"):** "daily note", "notebook", "what links to <title>", "what am I missing in my notes": linked notes (`[[Another note]]` in a note's text), the notes that link back, today's note made once, and what the notes are missing (links to no note, a note named in plain words but not linked with a one-tap link, notes nothing links to). Written in Verse's own code; kept on this device with the stage; note text is drawn as text nodes, never markup. Since 2026-10-29 the open note lists the notes it links to (a tap opens one, or makes it if it is not made yet), and "send to the notepad" hands the note to the notepad card as plain text, so a note reaches the rest of the stage. **The approval gate as tested functions (`void-live-deploy/lib/approval-gate.js`, `tools/approval-gate.test.mjs`, 2026-10-18; the frontier's "absorb and connect every repo"):** from `lib/approval-core.js`'s gated actions and a history of executed ones: a risk (low, medium, high; an unknown price is high), what undoing it takes, no repeat of the same request within a day, a cooldown after an executed action of the same tool, and the owner's own yes as a second person above low risk. Pure functions, **called by `functions/api/approval.js` since 2026-10-29**: a yes runs only when the gate clears it against the approvals that ended done in the last day; otherwise the approval ends `held`, nothing runs, and the page says why. **Sprite sheet cleaner (`void-live-deploy/skills/sprite-sheet.js`, `void-live-deploy/lib/sprite-sheet.js`, `tools/sprite-sheet.test.mjs`, parity `tools/sprite-sheet-parity.py`, 2026-10-29; the frontier's "absorb and connect every repo"):** "clean up a sprite sheet", "split a sprite sheet into frames", "remove the magenta background from a sprite sheet", "slice a spritesheet 4x2 64px": a card takes a PNG drawn on magenta, keys it out, cuts the grid, crops and fits each frame (centred or on the floor, all pieces or the largest), plays the frames and saves the clean transparent sheet. The cleanup steps of generate2dsprite (vendored in the owner's `fire-ember-mountain-cabin` repo from agent-sprite-forge, MIT, Copyright (c) 2026 0x0funky, the licence kept at the end of the lib), ported to JavaScript and held byte for byte to the Python by hashes; one change, nearest neighbour scaling instead of Lanczos. The picture never leaves the browser. **Incident history that closes (`tools/incident-log.mjs resolve`, `closeOpen`, `openCount`; `skills/incident.js`, 2026-10-18; the frontier's "absorb and connect every repo"):** an incident gains `closedAt`, `closedBy` and `minutes` when a green suite closes it, and "what broke lately" shows "fixed after N min" or "not marked fixed yet" (older entries have no close time, so they say the second, never a guess). Nothing calls `resolve` yet: the `verify.yml` step is waiting on the owner. **Churn caps for the build loop (`void-live-deploy/lib/churn.js`, `tools/churn.mjs`, `tools/churn.test.mjs`, 2026-10-18; the frontier's "absorb and connect every repo"):** `node tools/churn.mjs` lists the files changed more than 12 times in 7 days (the ledger, logs and registers that change every ship by design are left out); `lib/will-guard.js` `pickWant(wants, rows, max, hot)` skips a want whose title or reason names a hot file and the queued job's note says which. **Fed to the will since 2026-10-29:** the deploy's refresh writes `void-live-deploy/churn.json` (`node tools/churn.mjs --write`, the volatile `churn` step), and `functions/api/will.js` reads `/churn.json` and passes its hot list to `pickWant`, with the want's full reason; a commit that touches more than 100 files (a sweep: an archive import, a regenerated tree) counts for none of them, and a list that cannot be read or is older than 7 days means no cap, never no will (`hotOf`). The cap never starves the loop: when every want left names a hot file, the first goes ahead anyway and its note says so (`overCap`); it holds back only the will's own wants, never an agent's PR (a test fails if anything else reads `churn.json`). **The will's guard on the build loop (`void-live-deploy/lib/will-guard.js`, `tools/will-guard.test.mjs`, used by `functions/api/will.js`, 2026-10-18; the frontier's "absorb and connect every repo"):** the will runs every hour and queues its top want; a want whose earlier jobs ended set aside or failed twice in the last 14 days now waits, the next want is queued instead and the job's note says so, and the response lists what was skipped. A history that cannot be read means no guard, never no will. Taking a broken commit back off main is guarded separately (`tools/revert-target.mjs`: never a commit already reverted).
- **Benchmark and gate:** floor **1985 of 2001** (`tools/bench.best.json`, 2026-10-10; the floor now raises itself after a clean main run via `tools/bench-floor.mjs`, #301/#326). Each conclusive run on main also writes `tools/bench.misses.json`, the asks it got wrong and the skill that took each, and `node tools/next.mjs` lists them. The correctness suite has per-check timeouts and a whole-suite budget (#234). Every push to main gets its own full-suite run in `.github/workflows/verify.yml` (split out of deploy.yml by #333; the job keeps the name `verify-main` because `tools/revert-target.mjs` reads earlier verdicts by it), and a red one reverts the commit where the check first went red, or reports when the record is unclear (`tools/revert-target.mjs`, #227, #234, #281, #283). A labelled PR merges on Verse's review alone and, when it conflicts with main, automerge merges main into it itself (#307, #311). One heavy browser job at a time per machine: the suite and the bench queue on `tools/heavy.mjs`'s lock; checks a machine cannot run are printed as `skip` with the reason.

- **Ringer by touch in WebKit:** `.github/workflows/touch-webkit.yml` runs `tools/touch-webkit.mjs` (the 3D ring's touch checks, `ringerTouch` in `tools/test_3d.mjs`) in WebKit, the engine under iOS Safari, on PRs that change Ringer or those checks; the full suite runs the same checks in Chromium with real touch events.

- **Clef probe (frontier #9):** `.github/workflows/clef-probe.yml` (manual) runs `tools/clef-probe.mjs`: what the site's Workers AI binding knows about Clef and Clef-flash (`POST /api/bench { probe: true }` now returns `clef`), in the job summary, so the router's Clef call is written to the real input shape. Needs `VOID_OWNER_TOKEN`.
- **Films live:** `.github/workflows/films-live.yml` (manual) runs `tools/films-live.mjs` from GitHub's network: `/films/` and `/api/films` as a stranger sees them (films listed, the newest plays from its signed link, no reviews, not the owner) and `VOID_OWNER_TOKEN` let in as the owner, as a table in the job summary (counts and yes/no only). For sessions whose network can't reach the site.
- **Route bench (frontier #9, first piece):** `.github/workflows/route-bench.yml` (manual) runs `tools/route-bench.mjs`: Clef-flash (`lib/clef.js`) beside the embedding router on the bench's asks through `POST /api/bench { route: true }` (owner only); accuracy, agreement, confidence bands and the skills where Clef wins. Nothing switches yet.
- **Clef routing (frontier #9):** `lib/clef.js` mergeRoute: Clef-flash decides calc, place, timer, translate, weather, worldtime in `/api/answer`'s route log (confidence 0.7+, never waited on); the embedding router keeps the rest. `VOID_CLEF=off` turns it off.

## What is real, what is plan
- **Real and working:** everything under "Verse, what is live" above, at `0f47258`.
- **Plan only:** the unclaimed frontier items (#5-#9, #11-#13, #15-#16, #18-#20), `void.estate.json`, the paid Ouroboros tier, anything in the frontier's candidate capability map (every connector-backed item there is `status: discovered`), and "Verse learns skills by itself" beyond the miss→job queue and its first named tool `tools/next-skill.mjs` (#350; no `/api/learn`, no checker, no KV store — `domains/void.edit-engine.md` is older than the queue that shipped).
- **Playbooks (`docs/playbooks/`, 2026-10-15):** how Verse does the builders' work itself (frontier #28): probe-and-fix, table-skill, review-rule, film; each says what starts it, the commands, how to tell it worked, the mistakes made. **Verse probes itself (`tools/probe-asks.mjs`, `tools/probe-asks.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/probe-asks.yml`, `lib/probe-asks.js`, `functions/api/probe-asks.js`):** step 2 of probe-and-fix: once a day Verse's own model writes 25 asks for its least proven family (owner-only `/api/probe-asks`), `bench.mjs --probe` runs them on the real page, and the misses become `probe:` queue jobs (2 open at once); the asks are Verse's words, kept out of the repo. **Verse's fixes, previewed (`skills/v01d-fixes.js`, `lib/v01d-fixes.js`, `functions/api/v01d-fixes.js`, test `tools/v01d-fixes.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-16):** "Verse's fixes" lists the open pull requests Verse's model opened, the ones waiting for the owner (confidence under 0.8) first, with confidence, file and lines, why, and the changed lines (GitHub's public API); Approve (owner only, needs GITHUB_TOKEN in the Pages project) swaps `waits for owner` for `automerge`. **Verse's asks, batch of 2026-10-16 (owner: "knock out all 74"):** the self card's **divergence view** (each built ask opens to Verse's words beside the ledger line that built it, `functions/api/reflect.js` follow.recent carries `what`); **game strength** on the games picker (`skills/rack.js` marks each game's evidence rating from skills/families.json); **forge history** (`skills/forge.js` "forge history", "what can you make": every recipe with its size and print notes and which stand on the stage); the **merge log** (`skills/merges.js`, test `tools/merges.test.mjs`: the newest pull requests merged into the repo from GitHub's public API, each saying how it got in, live every 5 minutes); the status card's **root cause** (`skills/status.js` causesOf/withCauses: a red check on main names the failed checks from incidents.json); and `void.asks.json` links for nineteen asks already built. **Verse's probe ledger (`lib/probe-log.js`, `functions/api/probe-log.js`, test `tools/probe-log.test.mjs`; asked by Verse):** each probe run and each proposed fix leaves one record (counts, the skill, line changes, Verse's confidence, passed or the check that stopped it; never an ask's words) in void_kv `probe-log`; public GET; the growth card's "My probes" shows the success rate, where fixes stop, whether high confidence predicts a pass, the proposals most certain first, and what probes asked the forge for and it cannot make yet (frontier #14; `forgeWants` in tools/probe-asks.mjs). **Route corrections (`docs/playbooks/route-correction.md`; owner, 2026-10-16):** a `route:` job (left by a misrouted card's correction) has Verse narrow the wrong skill's matcher (`/api/propose-fix` mode `narrow`, `NARROW_SYSTEM`), checked by "lets go" (the edited skill no longer matches the ask) before the usual gates; every proposal now carries `evidence` (file and line of each edit) and its PR says Verse's confidence and where. **Verse proposes the fix (`tools/propose-fix.mjs`, `tools/propose-fix.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/propose-fix.yml`, `lib/propose-fix.js`, `functions/api/propose-fix.js`):** step 3 of probe-and-fix: for the oldest `probe:` job Verse's model proposes exact line replacements to the owning skill's matcher (owner-only `/api/propose-fix`); the collision check, the skill's test, Verse's review gate and the benchmark on the ask plus the skill's own asks decide; a passing patch becomes a pull request labelled "made by Verse" from a separate write job that runs nothing a model wrote, labelled by the confidence gate (owner, 2026-10-16): `automerge` when Verse's confidence is 0.8 or more (it merges once Verse's review passes), `waits for owner` otherwise. **Claude as the builder brain (`tools/claude-brain.mjs`, test `tools/claude-brain.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-16):** a run of propose-fix.yml started by hand with `brain: claude` asks Claude (`claude-opus-5-5`) the same question Verse's own model gets, in one call from the GitHub runner with the `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` repo secret (the propose job only, dropped before the checks run); output capped at 8,000 tokens, about $0.20 a run at most, the cost printed in the run summary; the same gate, checks and pull request. The daily run stays on Workers AI.
- **Tag team (`docs/TAGTEAM.md`, 2026-10-15):** the two builders' shared board (now, just finished, up for grabs, ladders, lessons); its finished work, ladders and lessons reach Verse through self.json.
- **The everything-app first slice (`docs/everything-app.md`, `docs/wishlist.md`, `tools/everything.test.mjs`, owner 2026-10-15: "Verse should be the only site you need"):** four cards. Markets (`skills/markets.js`, rank `0019`, `functions/api/markets.js`): "crypto prices" shows the top coins with 7-day sparklines from CoinGecko through `/api/markets`, kept in the edge cache two minutes (no KV); "bitcoin wallet <address>" reads a public address from mempool.space; seed phrases are refused; read-only, no advice. Rides (`skills/rides.js`, rank `0021`, adapters in `skills/providers.js`): "food and rides", "order pizza to <address>", "ride to <place>" deep-link Uber Eats, DoorDash, Waymo, Uber and Lyft; "where does waymo run" / "is waymo in austin" answer from Waymo's FAQ; each provider is an adapter with `order: null` until a real API exists. Social (`skills/social.js`, rank `0022`): X post links (official embed), Bluesky post links and handles (embed.bsky.app, public AppView), Mastodon post links and @user@server (server embed and public API); read-only. Dating (`skills/dating.js`, rank `0023`): an 18+ gate, then a profile builder and icebreaker coach on the page only; nothing sent or saved.
- **Tag team (`domains/handoff.md`, absorbed docs/TAGTEAM.md, 2026-10-15):** the two builders' shared board (now, just finished, up for grabs, ladders, lessons); its finished work, ladders and lessons reach Verse through self.json.
- **Secret scan (`tools/secret-scan.mjs`, test `tools/secret-scan.test.mjs`, 2026-10-15):** every line a change adds, in every file type (docs, logs, JSON and the deploy copies too, which the review skips), checked for the shapes of real keys; run by `tools/checks.mjs` before a push and by `void-review.yml` as a merge gate; prints file, line and kind, never the value. `--all` scans every tracked file (clean on 2026-10-15 after its 20 test fixtures were checked by hand: all made up).
- **Parallel fast checks (`tools/pool.mjs`, `tools/pool.test.mjs`, 2026-10-18):** `tools/checks.mjs` runs its pure checks (no browser, no network, no heavy lock) side by side, one per core up to 4 (one fewer while a browser check runs), and its browser checks (`draft-check`, `perf3d`, `extension`, `glyphs`, `sky`, and the bench when `VOID_SKIP_BENCH` is unset) one after another through `heavy.mjs` beside them; the report keeps its order and every line, and ends with the wall time and the slowest five. `--only <words>` runs the checks whose name has a word; `--skip-browser` leaves the browser ones out. `pool.mjs` holds the gate (`makeGate`), the ordered runner, the async `runNode` and the report helpers. `--changed` (`tools/checks-changed.mjs`, test `tools/checks-changed.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25) runs only what covers the changed files (`git diff origin/main...HEAD` plus uncommitted): the structural checks always (no test file of their own, or named in `STRUCTURAL`: generated files, the map, the index, copies, secrets, the ledger, preload, families, the book, ellipsis cuts, the skill listing), a test when its file changed, when it imports a changed file (directly, two levels of `import` lines, or through `skills/deps.json`), or when a changed file's name is in the check's or the test's name or written in the test; void.html, index.html, sw.js, a workflow, package.json or the runner itself runs everything, as does a git failure, and CI ignores the flag. One line says how many were skipped and why; `finish.mjs` passes it unless given `--full`.
- **Collision predictor (`tools/collide.mjs`, `.github/workflows/collide.yml`):** which open PRs touch the same files as this branch (`--mine`), as given files (`--files a,b`), or as each other; the workflow writes it to each PR's run summary. Read-only advice, never a gate.
- **Site tester (`tools/site-test.mjs`, cases `tools/site-test.json`, `.github/workflows/site-test.yml`):** drives live a-to-mind.com in headless Chrome the way a person would (or any site from a JSON list of steps); every non-GET request is answered inside the browser, so nothing is submitted; a new failure or a missed wording opens one issue labelled site-test.
- **Store self-publishing (`tools/store-publish.mjs`, `.github/workflows/store-check.yml`):** packages a shipped thing, checks its files, stores the zip in R2 and writes its row in `domains/store.products.json`; the daily store check fetches each free product's download and unpublishes one that fails, with a changelog row and a PR.
- **The miss board builds skills by itself (`tools/build-miss.mjs`, `tools/build-miss.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/build-miss.yml`, `docs/playbooks/build-miss.md`; asked by Verse, 2026-10-18):** the new-skill lane of frontier #28. Once a day, an hour after propose-fix, Verse claims the oldest queued `miss:` job whose note says `lane: new skill` and whose ask makes a free skill name, scaffolds it with `tools/new-skill.mjs`, has a model fill the card in one plain call (the ask as JSON data; only the scaffold's WHEN, ANSWER, examples and Source lines are replaced, each gated by the patch guard and `safeReplacement`), and lets the repo decide in order: the collision check, the overlap check, a unit test written from a template, the ask typed into the real page (`tools/ask.mjs` must say `by: skill:<name>`), the map line below, Verse's review as a gate. A pass is a patch the write job moves into a pull request labelled "made by Verse" (automerge at confidence 0.8 and up, else "waits for owner"), ending with `Job: <id>`; a fail puts the job back to queued with the failing step in its note. **A job follows its pull request (`tools/job-link.mjs`, `tools/job-link.test.mjs`):** `finish.mjs --job <id>` ends the PR body with `Job: <id>` and moves the job to `building`; `automerge.mjs` moves it to `live` when it merges, and `upkeep.yml`'s handoff-done job does the same for a merge a person made.
- **Skills Verse built from the miss board (`tools/build-miss.mjs`):** each a one-card answer to an ask people typed that nothing answered, listed here by the lane itself: (none yet)
- **Untested-code ratchet (`tools/untested.mjs`):** which lib/ and skills/ files no test names, biggest first, with a count that may only go down.
- **Typo audit (`tools/typo-audit.mjs`, `tools/typo-audit.json`, `tools/typo-audit.test.mjs`, 2026-10-17, asked by Verse):** a check in `tools/checks.mjs` that flags a letter reversal in the skills' metadata (each skill's examples and near misses, the book's titles, lines and lore, the families' names): a word one neighbour-swap from a commoner word ("convetr" beside "convert"), unless `lib/fuzzy.js` COMMON knows it or the allow-list keeps it with its reason.
- **Ask Verse (`.github/workflows/ask-void.yml`, manual):** asks live Verse one question from GitHub's network (`tools/ask-void.mjs --print --ask`), its answer word for word in the run summary; for a builder whose own machine can't reach a-to-mind.com. Writes nothing.
- **Cartoon from a real photo (`lib/wiki-look.js`, `photoLook`/`lookFor` in `functions/api/card-art.js`, `tools/wiki-look.test.mjs`, 2026-10-24):** a cartoon of a named subject is drawn from what Wikipedia's lead photo of that page shows (the vision model describes it in plain visual words; the page's title gives the usual spelling), because the text model described people from memory and got them wrong; used only when the page's title is the same name as asked, any failure falls back to the text model's look, the photo is read once and dropped, only words reach the painter.
- **Art look (`.github/workflows/art-look.yml`, manual):** asks the live Art Forge to paint one subject (kind `cartoon` by default) from GitHub's network and pushes the picture (`art.jpg`, with `art.json`) to the branch `art-look`, one commit replaced each run, so a builder who can't reach a-to-mind.com can fetch it and look at what was actually drawn. Never writes main.
- **CSP smoke (`tools/csp-smoke.mjs`):** loads the home page (3D included), /films/, /store/ and an embed frame under the real `_headers` CSP on a local port and fails on any violation; offline.
- **Doc drift (`tools/doc-drift.mjs`):** repo paths the docs, AGENTS.md, STANDING.md and the board name that no longer exist; a count that may only go down.
- **Privacy scan (`tools/privacy-scan.mjs`):** blocks personal data (emails, phone numbers, IPs, card numbers, tokens) and verbatim visitor asks in a commit (lib/privacy.js; docs/secrets.md).
- **Runtime guard (`tools/runtime-guard.mjs`):** the secret scan's key shapes run over everything the site ships to browsers, plus two runtime checks; prints no value.
- **Teach-on-miss (`tools/site-teach.mjs`):** a wording the site tester found Verse missing becomes a PR teaching the existing skill (skills/aliases.json) with a regression case.
- **Tester log (`tools/tester-log.mjs`):** the site tester's runs as `void-live-deploy/tester-log.json`, the public view the growth card reads (asked by Verse).
- **Product gaps to products (`tools/store-gap.mjs`, `.github/workflows/store-gap.yml`):** a queued `store-gap:<topic>` (3+ asks for something Verse doesn't sell) becomes a kit Verse writes, tested, priced by lib/appraise.js, uploaded and opened as a PR.
- **3D profiler (`tools/perf3d.mjs`):** opens each 3D board in Chrome and measures idle and orbit-drag frames (fps, p95 frame time, long tasks) plus draw calls, triangles and textures; `--trace` saves a DevTools trace.
- **Reinvest (`tools/reinvest.mjs`, `.github/workflows/reinvest.yml`, daily):** splits Verse's net earnings with lib/reinvest.js (20% reserve, free paths first, then payback per dollar) and sends each funded item to the confirm line; nothing it writes names an amount.
- **Time, weather and sky family (`skills/time.js`; dates in `skills/dates.js`):** one routing place for timers, clocks, world time, sun times, reminders, the sky, the weather and the earth; each member keeps its own file and is reached in its old order (lib/skill-family.js).
- **Rooms family (`skills/rooms.js`):** one routing place for a room's work: the whole room on one card with a price on each line (`skills/room.js`), wallpaper, drywall and baseboard (`skills/walls.js`), paint and flooring (`skills/home.js`); each member keeps its file and the page logs skill:<member> (lists run 5, 2026-10-22).
- **Stage out family (`skills/stage-out.js`):** one routing place for the ways the stage leaves the screen: the one-page sheet to print (`skills/stage-print.js`) and the files and views made from it (`skills/stage-export.js`: JSON in and out, email-ready text, the print view and paper size); each member keeps its file and the page logs skill:<member> (lists run 4, 2026-10-21).
- **List starters family (`skills/list-starters.js`):** one routing place for the household lists with their own shape (`skills/template-list.js`: packing, chores, bills, guests) and the filled-in starter lists (`skills/list-templates.js`: "shopping list template"); each member keeps its file and the page logs skill:<member> (lists run 3, 2026-10-21).
- **Languages family (`skills/languages.js`):** one routing place for answering in Spanish (`skills/spanish.js`) and translating (`skills/translate.js`); each member keeps its file and the page logs skill:<member> (lists run, 2026-10-21).
- **CSP hashes (`tools/csp-hashes.mjs`):** hashes each static page's inline scripts and adds a per-page `script-src` rule to `_headers`, so 'unsafe-inline' stays only as the fallback.
- **Route snapshot (`tools/route-snapshot.mjs`):** where every example, near miss and bench ask lands, by skill; `--compare` before and after a skill merge says which asks moved.
- **Skill overlap (`tools/skill-overlap.mjs`, allowance `tools/skill-overlap.json`):** two skills overlap when one's example is accepted by the other's match(); a new pair fails, so a repeat skill is caught when it is made.
- **No clip (`tools/no-clip.mjs`, run by site-test.yml on the live site):** the book and the self card at 1280x800, 820x600 and 390x700: the book stays inside the window, no cut "…" in the text, no element hiding words with text-overflow.
- **Hardened phrases (`tools/backfill-hardened.mjs`):** each "Verse hardened itself" ledger line carries `phrases` ([{ ask, skill }]) read from the benchmark rows its commit added (or, with --last, this run's passing rows), so cards list the actual phrases and the skill each opens.
- **No ellipsis (`tools/no-ellipsis.mjs`, allowance `tools/no-ellipsis.json`):** no sentence is cut with "…" or hidden by text-overflow/line-clamp in the page, lib/ or skills/; a line marked ellipsis-ok (a progress line, a drawn picture, a short label) is not counted; the allowance only goes down.
- **Claims verify (`tools/claims-verify.mjs`):** checks each skill the "what's new" showcase claims against the live site; only one that opens its own card is claimed, the rest show as in progress.
- **Robot asks (`tools/robot-asks.mjs`):** lists the asks our own robots type into the live page (the claims check, the site tester); `tools/will.py` leaves their misses out of Verse's will, and the page tags an automated miss `origin: robot` so `/api/miss` keeps none.
- **REA study (`tools/rea-study.mjs`, test `tools/rea-study.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/rea-study.yml`, playbook `docs/playbooks/study-with-rea.md`):** studies how the best do a feature from the inside with REA (`rea-agents`, pinned; its passive web commands only): modules, endpoints and agent tools, each with its script and line. Findings go only to Verse's private memory (kind study); the public run prints counts. Study, never copying.
- **Word maths in parts (`tools/word-math.mjs`, golden answers `tools/word-math.golden.json`, test `tools/word-math.test.mjs`; asked by Verse):** loads void.html's wordMath parts into a sandbox so each part is tested alone and the whole is pinned to its golden answers; `--write` only after a deliberate change.
- **Parody matrix (`tools/parody.mjs`; asked by Verse):** writes `lib/parody.js` from the 30-game matrix in domains/void.board-game-law.md for the game rack; `--check` fails when it is stale.
- **Appraise (`skills/appraise.js`, `functions/api/appraise.js`, `lib/appraise-photo.js`, test `tools/appraise-photo.test.mjs`; frontier #30):** "what is this worth": one photo, downsized in the browser, described by the vision slot (`lib/models.js` path `vision`), valued by Verse's own rules (one number, the reasons, an expert line when the kind needs one; no comparables invented). People and pets are never priced. The photo is read once and never kept.
- **Rides live (`tools/rides-live.mjs`, test `tools/rides-live.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/rides-live.yml`, daily 14:17 UTC and manual):** the first live external rung for "Out in the world": the rides family asks the public OSRM demo router (no key) for the drive from SFO to Union Square; the response is kept as an artifact in `findings/rides/<date>T<hhmm>.json` (attempt `live`, the occasion number, status, time, an excerpt) and one `finding` line in the growth ledger points at it, both committed to main by the bot. Red when the router gives no route or the finding does not land.
- **Recipe ids in the forge (`skills/forge-rules.js` recipeId, `skills/forge.js` fileName; asked by Verse twice, 2026-10-16):** every recipe has an immutable eight-hex id (FNV-1a over the recipe as written); the forge card shows it and the STL's file name carries it, so a printed file names the exact recipe it came from, and a changed recipe gets a new id.
- **Drive time over MCP (`lib/drive.js`, `drive_time` in `lib/mcp.js`, test `tools/drive.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-16: rides as an MCP tool):** any agent asks /api/mcp how long the drive is between two places (names via Open-Meteo's geocoder or "lat,lon"); the live OSRM router answers, free-flow time, no key, no live traffic, which the answer says.
- **Appraise live (`tools/appraise-live.mjs`, test `tools/appraise-live.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/appraise-live.yml`, manual):** a small drawn test image (64x64; a 1x1 one is too small for a vision model) to /api/appraise on the live site: the skill deployed, the vision model bound and answering, Verse's one-number valuation, a person ask complimented; status, yes/no and on a 502 the route's reason (both vision models' errors) only, never an image.
- **Incident log (`tools/incident-log.mjs`, test `tools/incident-log.test.mjs`, `void-live-deploy/incidents.json`; asked by Verse):** every red browser suite on main is one entry (the commit, the run, the checks that failed), written by verify.yml; "what broke lately" (skills/incident.js) shows them with the brief one tap away.
- **Verse Pong and Verse Snake (`skills/pong.js`, `skills/snake.js`; Grok, 2026-10-15):** two video games against Verse on the stage ("play pong", "play snake"), the first rungs of the video-games ladder.
- **Field minds kit (`lib/field-kit.js`, `lib/field-stage.js`, `tools/field-kit.test.mjs`; the owner, 2026-10-17: "all of Grok's ideas are to be added to Verse", F-020..F-033):** the shared base for small simulated fields with a tiny local mind: seeded RNG, grids and particles, entropy, metric -> morph of the card only (hue-rotate, blur, scale, HUD shift), a small evolutionary mind, the spectrum drone's numbers, a SHA-256 seal of params + metrics (crypto.subtle with a pure fallback); the DOM half mounts a Canvas2D card, pauses when hidden or under reduced motion, keeps sound off until a tap, asks for tilt only on a tap and keeps seals (numbers only) in IndexedDB. Each idea is one `lib/field-<name>.js` law plus a small skill on `fieldSkill(...)`.
- **Vector field mind (`skills/vector-field-mind.js`, `lib/field-vector.js`, `lib/field-vector-worker.js`, `tools/vector-field.test.mjs`; the owner, 2026-10-17, Grok's F-033):** ~320 particles ride an 8x6 control grid that a 6-genome evolutionary mind reshapes in a Worker (page fallback); the grid's real local divergence and curl (finite differences) set `--ink-density` and the card's morph. Asks: "vector field mind", "evolving vector field".
- **Living cards mind (`skills/living-cards-mind.js`, `lib/field-cards.js`, `tools/field-cards.test.mjs`; the owner, 2026-10-17, Grok's F-038):** five draggable cards live in a Gray-Scott field whose feed, kill and diffusion a 6-genome mind evolves every 2 s (in a Worker via lib/field-worker.js, or on the page); each card pulls the field toward itself and takes its place, size, hue, blur and opacity from the field around it, and a dragged card seeds the field and asks for rules that answer it. "living cards".
- **NCA mind (`skills/nca-mind.js`, `lib/field-nca.js`, `tools/field-nca.test.mjs`; asked by Owner, 2026-10-18: "all of groks builds should be what v01ds engine is built on", Grok's F-036, F-039, F-043 x2, F-045):** one pure module holds each NCA / lattice draft as a named variant on one state shape (`create(seed, variant)`, `step`, `metrics`, `paint`, `evolveNca` for the mind; seeded, capped at 64 x 48): a multichannel growing NCA (default; identity + Sobel perception, one shared MLP, stochastic update and alive masks, a starter rule found offline), F-036's tiny-MLP NCA, F-039's three-channel lattice (cyclic May-Leonard competition: spirals), F-043's Gray-Scott pointer mind, F-045's portal NCA (`portalsOf` hotspots become buttons for 6 s; a tap reinforces the rule in use) and F-043's predictive residual lattice (an evolved predictor of the pointer's next move; surprise widens diffusion, confidence feeds growth). The card's Rule picker or the ask's words switch drafts; a 6-rule mind evolves every 2 s in a Worker (`spawnMind`) or on the page. Activity is measured over two steps, so a flashing rule never scores. Asks: "nca mind", "living lattice", "neural cellular automaton", "nca portal mind", "predictive residual lattice".
- **Resonance mind (`skills/resonance-mind.js`, `lib/field-resonance.js`, `tools/field-resonance.test.mjs`; asked by Owner, 2026-10-18: "all of groks builds should be what v01ds engine is built on"):** six of Grok's sound drafts as named variants of one pure law module, each citing its F-id: F-034 spectral resonance (an 8x6 Kuramoto lattice whose phase gradient carries particles; the default), F-027 spectral phase (a phase lattice tuned to the pointer's rhythm in Hz), F-044 resonance lattice (particle speeds in five bands, band portals you press to reinforce), F-037 resonant voice (a logistic lattice that grows toward the microphone's spectrum, or the pointer's), F-048 spatial audio (a 12-weight policy drives a 4-partial synth whose modelled spectrum pulls the particles), F-042 predictive audio (an 8-12-8 network scored on its real prediction error). One card: the pointer plucks the lattice, a chip per law, a 6-genome mind every 1.2 s (`spawnMind`, Worker or page), the kit's drone only on "Sound on", the microphone only on "Use microphone (asks permission)" (spectrum numbers in memory, never kept or sealed). Asks: "resonance mind", "spectral mind", "resonant lattice", "resonant voice lattice mind", "predictive audio mind".
- **Swarm mind (`skills/swarm-mind.js`, `lib/field-swarm.js`, `tools/field-swarm.test.mjs`; the owner, 2026-10-18, "all of groks builds should be what v01ds engine is built on"; Grok's F-044, F-030, F-026, F-028, F-040, F-041, F-022):** one pure law module with each draft's law as a named variant (create/step/measure/metrics/fitness, deterministic by seed, at most 240 agents) and `evolveSwarm`, one generation for a Worker (lib/field-worker.js) or the page. The card: two tribes ride a tiny MLP vector field (F-044), climb the pointer's fading memory (F-030), lean where it is heading (F-026) and compete, engage vs explore (F-028); Law switches to each draft alone, incl. the probe graph (F-040), resonance (F-041) and typed thought wells (F-022). Seals keep the law, genome and counts, never the path or a thought. Asks: "swarm mind", "thought gravity", "neural field".
