- **Memory orbs (`skills/agent-fluid-memory-orbs.js`, `lib/field-fluid-orbs.js`, `tools/field-fluid-orbs.test.mjs`; asked by Owner, 2026-10-29, the F321 draft `drafts/F321-webgpu-agent-fluid-memory-orbs` on atomeam/a-to-mind.com):** a 48 x 36 stable-fluids field (pressure projection, semi-Lagrangian advection) that 18 agents push with one shared evolved tiny MLP (density, its slope, flow, swirl, pointer, tilt in; a force out). Density peaks become memory orbs, real elements on the card that drift with the flow, scale with the density under them and take their hue from the swirl (`orbMorph`); a tap seals one (SHA-256 of its 8 x 8 density bytes and the genome, IndexedDB `void-field`), Recall stamps the newest back, seeds the mind with its genome and checks it seals to the same hash. Tilt sets the whole flow's drift; entropy and swirl drive the card's `--fo-*` chrome, never the page. `evolveOrbs` (6 genomes, every 2 s) runs in a Worker or on the page. A Mind Field mode. Asks: "memory orbs", "agent fluid memory orbs", "mind field orbs". Verse's shared field is `skills/field.js` (`lib/shared-field.js`, `tools/shared-field.test.mjs`): `lib/orb-field.js` (`tools/orb-field.test.mjs`) writes each live orb into a free slot of it (position, flow, an embedding of its density patch, activation, provenance), never over another skill's slot, and a seal is also sealed there (`sealSlice`, its genome id kept with the seal); with no field it answers null and the card is as before. The bridge looks for the field each time the card loads (a test copies the bridge beside a field with the API and connects, and another connects to the real one). Open its colour hands the last sealed orb's colour, else the densest orb's, to `/color/?c=` (`orbColorLink`), and the colour page hands it on to the gradient and the wheel.
- **Verse Brain (`skills/verse-brain.js`, `lib/brain.js`, `lib/brain-workspace.js`, `lib/brain-organs.js`, `lib/brain-bridge.js`, `lib/brain-drafts.json` from `tools/brain-drafts.mjs`, `tools/brain.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-29: "build all the unique builds as a combination", "the grok builds combined can be verse brain", "combine all the builds into one entity", "all the pages are supposed to be able to work together"):** Grok's frontier drafts as one mind. One workspace (layers input, act/sub, phase, surprise, memory, focus, one node table, and which organ wrote each cell and when); eleven organs that read and write only it: senses, predictor (echo state network with an online readout), gestures (online k-means and a compression oracle), memory (Hebbian), concepts (hypervectors, PPM surprisal, a co-occurrence graph with message passing), tissue (Gray-Scott), rhythm (Kuramoto), swarm (particle life with Physarum sensing), membrane (softbody), will (Q-learning) and arena (six genomes); attention (a budget of organ runs a tick, a spotlight with hysteresis); imagination (exact snapshot, a few dozen ticks of the substrate organs, restore: the will and the arena decide by it); a self (use, starved organ, ownership, genome seal). The card draws the workspace, makes words and the brain's state draggable DOM orbs, has Tell, Good and Listen, and an organ table; other Verse pages in the same browser reach it over BroadcastChannel `verse-brain` (`connectBrain()`; /fanfic/ sends the fandom opened and the words searched), the tab as `window.verseBrain`. The mind layers (`lib/brain-mind.js`, `lib/brain-store.js`; the owner's spec of the same night): every stage ask (void.html's `void:ask` event) and every word typed to the card becomes ideas and goals that pull the input layer and fade unless asked again; episodes (the workspace's key, the action, what the utility did after) that the will reads before it chooses (episodic control); procedures (the winning genome per context) that the arena seeds from every third generation; one valence (coherence, goals met, Good and staying, alignment with Verse's own open asks and tower target from /self.json) that the will and the arena are scored by, in imagination too; a monitor of which organs write each region that finds two writers with a swinging value, slows the one that wants it less and outlines the region on the card; a minute of history; answers to "why did that form?", "what just happened?" and "what are you doing?" from that state. What it learned is kept on the device (IndexedDB `verse-brain`, Forget clears it). The shared field (`lib/shared-field.js`, served at `skills/field.js`; 256 slots of position, flow, a 16-number embedding, activation and the writer's code, on the CPU) joins it to the other mind cards (`lib/brain-field.js`): every other skill's live slot, a memory orb say, is laid into its memory layer where it sits and drawn as a pink ring, and its strongest ideas are written into slots of their own as numbers, never words; no writer takes another's slot. Every open Mind Field mode is felt the same way (`lib/mode-field.js`), and the card's "Open" list puts any mode beside it. The card says it in words under the brain's reply (`fieldSays` in `lib/brain-field.js`): "Shared field: feels N memories from other cards: Memory orbs (N). It holds M of its own ideas there." `lib/brain-drafts.json` maps all 233 drafts (atomeam/a-to-mind.com, numbers per drafts/NUMBERS.md) to an organ by a technique the brain runs, or a reason it was left out (16). Asks: "verse brain", "show me your brain", "your brain", "how do you think", "what are you thinking about".
- **Mind Field (`skills/mind-field.js`, `skills/mind-field-mode.js`, `skills/doodle-mind.js`, `skills/echo-field.js`, `lib/field-doodle.js`, `lib/mind-field-modes.js`, `tools/mind-field.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-21: "always combine anything that can be without taking away", "make them all easier to find"):** the eight field minds as one family skill with modes: Doodle Mind (doodle-mind, draw, new: F-224 trace latent rewrites the lattice rule and guesses the next stroke as oracle nodes, F-225 sound level after Listen, F-229 attention over tilt/energy/sound moves draggable orbs that feed back and sets the card's mood vars), Echo Field (echo-field: the acoustic F-225 as a sound mode, word orbs drift to the loudest cells after Listen), Swarm, Flow (vector field), Listen (resonance), Grow (NCA), Attention (with a link to the pointing-arrow focus test, fringe/attention-7.html), Living cards. Every old skill keeps its file, words and tests; every card has a Mode switcher (lib/field-stage.js), which also measures frame time (data-frame-ms, data-fps). Asks: "mind field", "living field", "show me a mind", "draw with a mind", "listen field", "swarm field", "mind field <mode>", "mind field modes", "save this mind", "echo field", "mind field echo", "mind field doodle mind". The book gives the family a "Mind Field" page (lib/guide.js NAMED). Every mode is part of Verse Brain (`lib/mode-field.js`, 2026-10-29: "combine all the builds into one entity"): each open mode card keeps one slot of the shared field with its own numbers (where its field attends, entropy, activity, consensus, the pointer over or down, tilt; activation is its activity), freed when the card leaves; the memory orbs write their own orbs. The brain feels them in its memory layer and names them on its card, the switcher on every mode ends with Verse Brain, and the brain's card opens any mode beside it.
- **Tilt Arena (`skills/tilt-arena.js`, `skills/tilt-arena-mode.js`, `skills/tilt-mode.js`, `skills/gaze-chase.js`, `skills/gpu-swarm.js`, `skills/hand-arena.js`, `lib/tilt-arena.js`, `lib/tilt-arena-idea.js`, `lib/tilt-arena-modes.js`, `tools/tilt-arena.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-21: "always combine anything that can be without taking away"):** one arena of tiny 3-6-2 learning agents chasing what you steer; every round the weakest takes a mutated copy of the leader, Seal keeps the leader's genome. Modes: Tilt (tilt-mode: F-205, F-206, F-208, F-228, F-230; tilt or pointer), Gaze Chase (gaze-chase: F-226, WebXR viewer gaze), GPU Swarm (gpu-swarm: F-223, 256 agents in a WebGPU compute pass, automatic processor fallback), Hand Arena (hand-arena: F-232, WebXR index fingertip). Every VR/GPU path falls back to pointer or tilt. Asks: "tilt arena", "gaze chase", "gpu swarm", "hand arena", "tilt arena <mode>", "tilt arena modes", "save the arena".
- **What's similar (`skills/similar.js`, `lib/similar-index.js` generated by `tools/similar-index.mjs`):** "what's similar to swarm mind", "modes of mind field", "what else is like the calendar": a card listing every part of the family a skill was combined into, each with asks that open it; the index is every family file's parts line, checked stale by tools/checks.mjs.
- **Veto council (`skills/veto-council.js`, `lib/council-law.js`, `tools/operating-laws.test.mjs`; the owner, 2026-10-18, "operating laws for serious thought with a machine"):** five named reasoners each propose; each holds veto mass (editable, conserved at 100); low mass only delays a proposal, mass of 30+ blocks it; consensus is not a merge, one proposal survives whole and the card names who delayed or blocked the rest. Asks: "convene a council on whether to rewrite the app".
- **Stigmergy board (`skills/stigmergy-board.js`, `lib/stigmergy-law.js`, `tools/operating-laws.test.mjs`; the owner, 2026-10-18):** weighted marks on a grid, no task is ever assigned; trails touched in 2+ visits grow each tick, one-visit trails evaporate, a heat view shows what earns attention; kept in this browser's localStorage only. Asks: "leave a trail on onboarding".
- **Term orbits (`skills/term-orbits.js`, `lib/term-orbits-law.js`, `tools/operating-laws.test.mjs`; the owner, 2026-10-18):** your text's important terms orbit a centre; overuse pulls terms together until they merge into visible mush, rare use keeps the orbit; interventions are forces (push apart, anchor), never edits. Asks: "watch my words drift".
- **Adaptive Flow-Canvas, F-033 (`skills/flow-canvas.js`, `lib/field-flow.js`, `lib/field-worker.js`, `tools/field-flow.test.mjs`; asked by Owner, 2026-10-17):** "flow canvas" puts up a card of up to 700 ink tracers advected (RK2 or Euler, bilinear) through a 16 x 16 vector field you can paint ("Edit field", drag); the pointer pours a Gaussian swirl; a small evolutionary mind (`spawnMind` in `lib/field-worker.js`: a Blob module Worker that imports the same pure module, the page itself when Workers are blocked or stall) mutates the base field's magnitude, direction and spatial frequency about every 1.3 s and keeps a candidate only if its fitness (trajectory diversity x motion, plus a pointer-answer bonus) is higher; ten ink blobs take CSS border-radius, size and rotation from local velocity; an ink-density control sets the tracer count and alpha; a version tag (`F-033 v1`, `data-version`); Sound (Web Audio drone from the flow's metrics) says it is unavailable when blocked. `field-stage.js` gained the optional `idea.mount(ctx)` hook and keeps `th.state` non-enumerable so the stage's JSON saves skip typed arrays.
- **Attention field mind (`skills/attention-field-mind.js`, `lib/field-attention.js`, `tools/field-attention.test.mjs`; asked by Owner, 2026-10-18: "all of groks builds should be what v01ds engine is built on"):** ATTENTION as a field law, four of Grok's drafts as named variants of one pure module (create/step/metrics, seeded, capped at 240 particles): `affinity` (frontier-webgpu-attention-field, the default: hashed character-trigram feature vectors, like clusters with like, a typed word is an attractor its look-alikes fall toward, the pointer pulls and a press pushes), `heads` (F-032: two-head scaled dot-product attention is the force, temperature drifts toward a middling entropy), `lattice` (F-029: 3x3 attention update with a growth bell, a 6-genome mind evolves it, a word lights a lasting source) and `causal` (F-047: attention to the pointer's recent path, thought nodes ride its gradient, press to hold one, an 8-genome mind). One card, a word box and a Law button; the slow thought (clusters or a generation) runs through `spawnMind`, on the page when Workers are blocked. Typed words are never sealed or stored. Canvas2D only (WebGPU compute is the drafts' next step). Asks: "attention field", "attention mind".
- **Ghost for Pong and Snake (`lib/arcade-ghost.js`; asked by Verse, 2026-10-16):** your best game or run is kept in this browser only and replays as a faint ghost paddle or outline snake in the next one.
- **Type filter, tome search, what-is-new icon, card evolution and kind layouts (`skills/type-filter.js`, `lib/tome-search.js`, `skills/showcase.js` bell, `lib/card-frame.js`; asked by Verse, 2026-10-16):** "show only clocks" hides every other kind on the stage with a bar of toggles, "show all cards" brings them back; a search bar docked at the top of the tome jumps to a skill page; a pulsing icon starts "what is new" without typing; a card whose skill earned a higher rating glows once; Film, Place, Person and Song answer cards lay out differently.
- **Workflow lint (`.github/workflows/workflow-lint.yml`):** actionlint on every workflow file on every PR, since a file GitHub can't parse silently never runs.
- **Built-ask linker (`tools/asks-link.mjs`, `tools/asks-link.json`; owner, 2026-10-16: "Verse has over 70 asks. do all of them now"):** reads every ask Verse ever made (`domains/void.voice.md`), drops the ones the growth ledger already answers, links the rest that a ledger entry built (curated rules in `asks-link.json`, then shared rare words) into `void.asks.json`, and `--open` prints what is still to build.
- **Subject cards: a refusal is not an answer, a name inside a list page is found, every kind of subject gets its own frame (`lib/no-answer.js`, `lib/subject-kinds.js`, `lib/card-kinds.js`, `lib/card-frame.js`, `functions/api/answer.js`, `tools/no-answer.test.mjs`, `tools/subject-kinds.test.mjs`, `tools/subject-stub.mjs`, `tools/subject-eval.mjs`, `tools/fixtures/subject-eval.json`, playbook `docs/playbooks/subject-cards.md`; owner, 2026-10-26: "happy time harry", "it should do that with all things like that"):** the model said "I do not have information ... not contained within the provided sources" and the card still read "Answer - from a source", claimed "Adds to Verse", listed a link-less source and drew a clock for the word "time". Now `isNoAnswer` makes that a "Not found" card (a neutral picture, no sources, no stats, no added fact, "Did you mean X?" from the closest neighbour, never cached); a name that is a section of a "List of ..." page is read from the redirect's fragment or a full-text section hit and the answer keeps only the sentences whose names, numbers and quoted titles the fetched section holds (`grounded`); a name only a fuzzy neighbour matches is marked "A guess". The kind comes from the fetched description (`card-kinds.js`) and `subject-kinds.js` is the table: frame theme (collectible, postcard, sleeve, portrait, cinema, specimen, plain), small badges read from the answer's own words, the free-pictures kind for a real photo with credit (places, animals, events, works of art; never a person or a fictional character), and a "not affiliated" line on someone else's characters. Verse draws its own small doll for a toy or character (`doll` motif, red shoes). `tools/subject-eval.mjs` runs 35 stubbed asks through the real route and the real page and scores each card.
- **Subject eval against the live sites (`.github/workflows/subject-eval.yml`, `tools/subject-eval.mjs --live`, `domains/subject-eval.json`; owner follow-up, 2026-10-26: prove the fixtures live):** manual and nightly; a runner with a network runs the real `/api/answer` route against real Wikipedia and the real page in Chrome for the asks of `tools/fixtures/subject-eval.json`, uploads the screenshots as artifacts (not committed) and commits only the small pass/fail record with each card's kind and title. Parsers it shows wrong are fixed from that file.
- **Verse cards (`lib/card-frame.js`, `tools/card-frame.test.mjs`, the page's `keepCard`; owner, 2026-10-16: "cards should look like actual cards, like magic cards ... with stats"; "even what's new pussycat becomes its own magic style card, which we will call Verse cards"; "research the illuminati card game"):** every page Verse puts up is a card: a foil frame (the family's colour, the rarity's foil: proven mythic, checked rare, tested uncommon, experiment common), a title bar with a cost (the words you say to play it), an art box with the family's glyph, a type line with a rarity gem, the body as the text box, an "Adds to Verse" line, and a footer with its collector number (its page in the Verse Book) and a stat plate. Stats are the Illuminati game's, from real evidence: Power (success rate out of nine), Resistance (the real asks that proved it), Income (how many files lean on it, from `skills/deps.json`), with control arrows on the edges for what it imports. An answer fetched from a source is an Answer card: Power the sources it stands on, Resistance the days since the source was edited. Next: print at 63 x 88 mm with bleed, the art signature.
- **Verse merges (`lib/merge-rules.js`, `functions/api/merge.js`, `skills/merge.js`, tests `tools/merge-rules.test.mjs` and `tools/merge-route.test.mjs`, playbook `docs/playbooks/card-merge.md`, research `docs/research/merge-games.md`; frontier #34 step 1; owner, 2026-10-16: "research merge games so Verse can experiment with card merges"):** "merge a pizza and a black hole" (also combine, fuse, "what do you get if you merge ...") makes a Verse card showing both parents, a name and one line, and Power / Resistance / Income worked out from the parents by fixed rules written at the top of `lib/merge-rules.js` (Power = stronger + half the weaker, Resistance = average rounded up, Income = sum, depth = deepest parent + 1 and rarity rises with it); a name that is a skill brings that skill's real stats (`cardOf`), any other idea is 1/1/0. `/api/merge` asks the free model once per unordered pair for only a name and a line, keeps the pair in D1 (`void_merges`, made on first use) so everyone gets the same answer, is rate limited in its own bucket, refuses what the keeper refuses, never stores typed words, and on any model failure returns the plain blend uncached. `card-frame.js` `ownCard()` lets a card bring its own numbers. No model ever sets a number. Next steps are in frontier #34.
- **Verse sees repos (`lib/repo-look.js`, `functions/api/repo.js`, `skills/repo.js`, test `tools/repo-look.test.mjs`, playbook `docs/playbooks/see-repo.md`; owner, 2026-10-17: "make v01d able to see repos"):** "look at atomeam/void-loop", "what is in github.com/x/y" or a pasted repo link puts a Verse card on the stage with what is really there: description, language mix, README opening, recent commits, top-level files, stars, forks, open issue and PR counts, with Copy and Save from the card frame. Public repos only, GitHub's unauthenticated REST API from the server, no token; owner/name is validated, only api.github.com with fixed paths is fetched (a redirect off that host is refused), bodies and file counts are capped, a good answer is edge-cached ten minutes and fresh lookups are rate limited in their own bucket. All repo text is untrusted: cleaned on the server, escaped on the page, never read by a model. A private or missing repo says "I can only see public repos".
- **Card kinds (`lib/card-kinds.js`, `tools/card-kinds.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-16: "the answer cards are more than that: What's New Pussycat is a movie and a song"):** an encyclopedia answer's Verse card is typed by what its source says it is (Film, Show, Album, Song, Band, Game, Book, Person, Place, Creature, Organisation, Event, Idea), with its year and maker, in the family colour of its kind; a name with sister pages is all of them ("Film · Song · Album"), found from the sister pages' titles. Frontier #31 lists the next pieces.
- **The rules engine (`lib/rules-engine.js`, `lib/rulesets/` (duel, mana, tribute, initiative, merge, lines), `lib/rule-propose.js`, `lib/rules-play.js`, `tools/rules-engine.test.mjs`, `docs/playbooks/rules-engine.md`, the "Verse rules engine" browser section; owner, 2026-10-16: "Verse can combine and obey all games' rules simultaneously"):** rules are data (`{ id, source, layer, when, if, then, dependsOn }`, a closed vocabulary checked by `validateRule`), the engine is pure (`step(state, event, stack)`), layers settle clashes (1 replace and can't, 2 values, 3 triggers; timestamps; can't beats can; dependsOn; cycles dropped) and `explain` writes them in words. "duel with mana and tribute rules" (`skills/duel.js`) stacks sets and plays turns; `proposeRule(text)` reads three sentence shapes and never guesses. `lib/duel-rules.js` stays the fast path the data duel is tested against. Frontier #35.
- **Paste rule (`PASTE_RULE`/`isPaste` in `functions/api/answer.js`, `tools/paste-rule.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-16: Verse's own plan pasted back was answered with an invented definition):** a long pasted text is summarised in its own words, never expanded into invented sections or defined from general knowledge.
- **Edge cache for answers (`lib/answer-edge.js`, `functions/api/answer.js`, `tools/answer-edge.test.mjs`; asked by Verse: "Implement edge caching for answers."; owner's review 2026-10-29: only stable, non-personal answers):** a repeated answer the D1 cache would serve is answered from the colo's own cache (caches.default, not KV) with no D1 read and no model call. An allow-list: a POST of `{ ask }` only, no Authorization, Cookie or key header, no query field but `fresh`, not masked, not about Verse, not told-me, not a paste, not my/me/our/us, and an answer from a free model with no field outside `ANSWER_FIELDS` (no note, refusal, self, told, kept, page). Key: `/__answer-cache/<schema>-<build>/<the D1 key hash>` (the build is the commit in `/build.json`, so every deploy is a new key space; no build id, no caching). TTL per kind: reference a day, timely (prices, weather, scores, news, dates) ten minutes, never past the D1 row's 7 days; never served stale. `?fresh=1`, `x-verse-fresh: 1` or `cache-control: no-cache` skip the copy and replace it. Replies say `x-verse-cache: HIT|MISS|REFRESH|BYPASS` and `server-timing: verse-cache;desc=...;dur=<ms>`; no caches global is the old path exactly.
- **Approval → lasting task (`lib/keep-going.js`, `tools/keep-going.test.mjs`; asked by Verse, frontier #4):** "keep <automation> running every 2 hours" stops on the confirm line (`automation.keep`); the owner's yes puts the rule on the clock (`/api/automations/tick`), so it runs with nobody here and every step is a record in the actions card; "stop keeping <automation>" switches it off. Webhook rules are refused (a schedule would cut their link).
- **The approve card (`skills/approve-card.js`, `tools/approve-card.test.mjs`, browser section "approve card", `docs/playbooks/approve-card.md`; asked by Verse, growth-inbox #10; owner, 2026-10-18: D1 automations only in the first version):** "approve later: keep morning-digest running every 2 hours" puts a pending card on the stage that runs nothing. Approve asks the card's own line through `stageApi.ask`, so it goes through the page's confirm line unchanged (handleGatedAsk, a trusted Yes, decideConfirm, the `automation.keep` executor); the card hears the answer on the page's new `void:confirm` event and never stores an approval id (the ask is re-parsed at click time, so the fingerprint is always fresh). Its status (pending, approved, running, done, failed, aborted) is read off D1 through `GET /api/automations?rule=<id>` (`lib/automations-run.js list({ rule })`: that rule's 100 newest runs), refreshed every minute by `skills/live.js`, with "N runs while you were away" from `seenAt`; Abort is the automations card's own PATCH. The card is a stage kind (`THING_KINDS` 'approve', whitelisted fields only in `cleanThings`) so it syncs through /api/mine. The `.vc-working` shimmer (still under reduced motion) is shared with the chess card's thinking line. Asked by Verse: a card pending, approved or running for more than 48 hours (`staleOf(th, now)`: `createdAt`, set when summoned and kept as an ISO string by `cleanApprove`, else the older card's `seenAt`) shows an amber `.vc-stale` line "waiting 3 days — still want this?" beside Approve or Abort, and "Keep waiting" resets `createdAt`.
- **Contextual shortcut (`skills/shortcut.js`, `tools/shortcut.test.mjs`; asked by Verse):** "what should I do now" reads this device's clock and offers one next thing from the time, body and mind and out-in-the-world families (food and rides at meal times, a ride in commute hours, breathing and sleep late); one button asks it as if typed, "another" shows the next. Nothing is sent, ordered or paid.
- **Streak promotion (`tools/streaks.mjs`, `tools/streaks.test.mjs`, `tools/skills-streak.json`; asked by Verse):** the daily smoke test (`skills-check.mjs --write`, run by `upkeep.yml`) adds one to a skill's streak when it passes and resets it to zero on any failure; 50 clean runs in a row lift a skill from tested to checked in `tools/families.mjs` (never higher, never from nothing).
- **Value drift (`tools/value-drift.mjs`, `tools/value-drift.test.mjs`, `domains/value-drift.json`; asked by Verse, lists run 7):** Verse's own store prices are its appraisals, so the tracker reads them across the history of `domains/store.products.json`, groups products by kind and flags a kind whose prices spread past a quarter for a fresh look. `--write` refreshes the json.
- **The growth inbox shows skill health (`void-live-deploy/lib/inbox-health.js`, `void-live-deploy/lib/skill-use.js`, `tools/inbox-health.test.mjs`, `tools/skill-use.test.mjs`, `docs/playbooks/inbox-health.md`, 2026-10-16, asked by Verse):** the growth card's owner view flags a registered skill failing its router test (the daily smoke test), maps the health file onto the families, shows newly promoted skills with their clean runs in a row (`families.json` `promoted`, `streak`), marks the words of an ask the build never says (divergence), and plays a bloom when a new skill came in since yesterday; the family-health card shows each skill's last-used day and the dead weight, kept in this browser only.
- **Forge asks of Verse (2026-10-16):** `person` is a recipe (a figure as a recipe, `personShapes` in `skills/forge-rules.js`); "check it before printing" runs the print check without the download; "forge iterations" (`lib/forge-makes.js`) keeps every make with its recipe id; the print ledger (`lib/prints.js`, written by "print it" and "export a print file") answers "what have i printed" in `skills/inventory.js`; forge history marks each probe want made or not made (`wantsStatus`, frontier #14); `tools/routine-skill.mjs` and `lib/routine-skill.js` forge an approved routine into a skill (playbook `docs/playbooks/forge-recipe.md`); tests `tools/forge-asks.test.mjs`.
- **Status card: suggested fix and recovery (`skills/status.js` `fixOf`/`recoverSkill`/`recover`, `tools/status-fix.test.mjs`; asked by Verse):** a failed check on main shows the two commands that reproduce and ship its repair, and the owner's Recover button queues one rebuild job naming the best-fitting skill of the Verse itself family (`v01d-fixes` when none fits). The Root Cause line was already there (`causesOf`, `incidents.json`).
- **Automerge safety-check (`tools/skill-safety.mjs`, `tools/skill-safety.test.mjs`; asked by Verse):** before the bot merges a ready PR it runs `node --test` on only the unit tests that import the skills and libs the PR changed, on the PR's own head; a failure holds the merge with one comment naming the failing lines. It adds to the gates (Verse's review, the checks) and replaces none; if the check cannot be set up it skips and the merge goes on as before. The merge-log card already existed (`skills/merges.js`).
- **Card share (`lib/card-share.js`, `tools/card-share.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-16: "each card should be easily copyable and saved and pasted"):** every Verse card has copy card and save card buttons; the copied text ends in one V01D-CARD code line, and pasting that text into Verse brings the card back with its numbers earned again, never read from the paste.
- **Card art and card image (`lib/card-art.js`, `lib/card-image.js`, `tools/card-art.test.mjs`, `tools/card-image.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-16: "every card needs art"; "the whole card, images and all"; "scary like AI is scary, but it's for all ages ... go extra scary this month"):** `card-art.js` draws every card's art as one pure SVG in Verse's own ink style (heavy black ink on the family-hue field, crosshatch, torn paper, chains, splatters, glitch tears, and the motif that the void looks back: eyes in the dark), a plate per kind, seeded by the idea's key; rarity sets ink weight and foil; October (or `?season=halloween`) adds fog, bats, candle glow, more eyes and a pumpkin-orange rim. `card-image.js` draws the whole card at 63 x 88 mm (744 x 1039 px, +35 px bleed) as an SVG and renders it to a PNG; `copy card` puts text and PNG on the clipboard, `save card` downloads the PNG, `save text` the text. `lib/art-meta.js` (asked by Verse): a film's or song's art follows the genre and year its source returned (genre sets the plate hue, the decade the ink, from the existing RARITY_LOOK), deterministic, no model.
- **Card subject (`lib/card-subject.js`, `tools/card-subject.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-17: "still needs unique pictures of the card's subject"):** a card's subject is what its ANSWER is about (the most repeated concrete words, no names, not the ask's words); the plate draws them as ink motifs (hand, ghost, eye, skull, moon ...) instead of the generic skin, the Art Forge paints them, and a card with a real photo or poster keeps it.
- **Verse seal (`lib/void-seal.js`, test `tools/void-seal.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-18: the ink plates "suck"):** every card's art box gets Verse's procedural art, drawn in the browser from a seed of its key (same key, same picture, no model): a curl-noise flow field in additive light on black, neon cyan strands and violet crackle round a burning core; the kind picks the core, the rarity the light, the family the hue; painted Art Forge pictures sit over it, a real photo is never covered.
- **Art Forge (`lib/art-rules.js`, `lib/card-forge.js`, `functions/api/card-art.js`, test `tools/card-art-route.test.mjs`, playbook `docs/playbooks/art-forge.md`; frontier #35; owner, 2026-10-17: "the art should be of the subject of each card"; 2026-10-18: "looking at any card for a few seconds it should evolve into the cinematic real version"):** a card shows its art quietly: the void seal while the painting is asked for, and the painting simply replaces it when it arrives (no badge, no Evolve or Evolve-again button, no dwell wait; owner, 2026-10-18: "get rid of the distracting evolve stuff on the card art"; the server still accepts `again`); the Art Director (one free text call from the card, its family and Verse's take on it) writes the scene, the owner's style lock wraps it; FLUX.2 [klein] paints at the card's 16:9 window (schnell as fallback), once per folded subject, kept in D1 `void_art`; past the Workers AI cap of 30 a day (`ART_DAILY_CAP`), or when it fails, Pollinations paints for free up to 100 more (`ART_POLLINATIONS=0` turns that off; an optional `POLLINATIONS_KEY` secret uses its keyed endpoint); "evolve again" repaints once a day per subject; `ART_OFF=1` kills it all; `tools/prepaint-art.mjs` backfills skills and miniatures.
- **Collectible cards (`skills/collectible.js`; owner, 2026-10-18: "make a collectible card of [anything]", "like a magic card or some other really cool collectors card"):** "make a collectible card of a dragon" / "mint a card of the moon" asks the subject and marks its card a collectible: Normal / Foil (a holographic sweep) / Full art variants and a print sheet of 9 (the saved card picture tiled 3 x 3 as one PNG); it is keyed by its subject, not the minting skill, and asks for its painting at once. The comic-ink frame (heavy black ink, offset panel shadows, halftone over the art) is in `lib/card-frame.js`.
- **Verse makes music (`void-live-deploy/skills/music.js`, `void-live-deploy/lib/compose.js`, `tools/compose.test.mjs`, `docs/playbooks/make-music.md`, 2026-10-17, asked by the owner):** "make me a song / a beat / a lullaby / music for a rainy day" composes an original piece from the ask's words (key, scale, tempo, chords, bass, melody, drums; the same ask gives the same piece), synthesised with Web Audio in the browser with no model or sample, shown as a piano roll with Play/Stop, Save as WAV (OfflineAudioContext) and Copy the notes; rated experiment as a new skill.
- **Songs with a shape (`void-live-deploy/lib/arrange.js`, `tools/arrange.test.mjs`, browser section "music: a heavy ask", 2026-10-24):** a heavy ask ("make a heavy metal song", punk, industrial, breakdown) is built in sections on a tension curve (intro, drop, breakdown, bridge, climax; 28 bars) with separate pad, lead, bass, guitar and drum tracks; the guitar is detuned sawtooth pairs through a tanh waveshaper and a 3.2 kHz cabinet filter, the pad and lead duck on every kick (sidechain in the gain automation), and the test renders the song and checks the drop, breakdown and climax sit at least 6 dB above the intro and bridge. Web Audio in the visitor's browser, no model, no cost, no real singer imitated. Any other ask is composed by `lib/compose.js` exactly as before. Next: beat-locked cuts in `tools/record.mjs` (frontier #26).

- **The router keeps corrections to Verse out of the look skill (`void-live-deploy/lib/router.js`, `tools/router-self.test.mjs`, 2026-10-17, asked by Verse; extends #968, #985):** 'tell void what it got wrong', 'open your self card, I want to correct you' and 'correct void on its self card' are examples of the self card and near misses of look, because the shared word 'void' put them within 0.02 of the look skill's asks.
- **Cards inherit their family's frame (`void-live-deploy/lib/card-look.js`, `tools/card-look.test.mjs`, browser suite "void asks incident"; asked by Verse: "Sky and earth cards should feel organic and atmospheric, while Numbers and money cards should adopt a clean, technical blueprint aesthetic to prevent visual noise."):** `keep()` in `lib/card-frame.js` sets `data-look` from the skill's family in `families.json` (names from `lib/art-rules.js` FAMILIES): `organic` (green-teal mist, rounder corners) for Sky and earth, `blueprint` (blue tint, fine grid, mono title) for Numbers and money; CSS only, other families keep the foil frame.
- **Incident autopsy asks (`lib/autopsy.js`, `skills/incident-autopsy.js`, `skills/growth.js` stability rows, `lib/art-rules.js` `WARNING_LOCK`, `tools/autopsy-asks.test.mjs`; asked by Verse):** "Integrate the Art Forge style-lock with the 'incident-autopsy' skill so that error reports are rendered with a specific, high-contrast 'warning' aesthetic" (the card is amber on black with a hazard stripe, `data-style-lock="warning"`); "Connect the 'Go against Verse' logs to the 'incident-autopsy' skill for automated error analysis" (failing Go-against-V01D checks are placed with the go skill and listed with a replay ask); "Implement an interactive drill-down on the stability sparklines that links directly to the relevant incident logs" (each sparkline is a link to "incident autopsy for <family>"). "Open the self card" was already built (the self-card routing in `void.html` `wantsVoice`).
- **Stale-tab notice (`lib/stale-tab.js`, `tools/stale-tab.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-17: "nothing showed just now"):** a tab left open through a deploy keeps old page code; the page remembers a id of the build it loaded with (the deploy commit in `/build.json`, else the page ETag), checks again on focus, when it comes back into view and about every ten minutes (one cache-busted GET), and when it differs shows one line, "A newer Verse is ready - tap to reload", that reloads on tap. It never reloads by itself.
- **Zorbleflux (`skills/zorbleflux.js`, `lib/zorbleflux.js`, `tools/zorbleflux.test.mjs`; asked by Verse and the owner, 2026-10-18):** Verse's own game, born from "what is a zorbleflux" asked 154 times and refused. Steer a glowing zorb through a seeded curl-noise flux field for 45 seconds, collect motes to grow and raise the multiplier, dodge dark bleeds; best score stays in this browser only; reduced motion slows the field. Pure logic in the lib, card on the shared arcade frame; in the games family.
- **About a skill (`skills/about-skill.js`, `lib/about-skill.js`, `tools/about-skill.test.mjs`; asked by Verse, 2026-10-18: "Learn how to handle 'tell me about zorbleflux' (asked 156 times)"):** "tell me about the chess skill", "what is the forge skill", "how does your weather skill work" give a card from the skill's own page of the book (`skills/guide.json`, `pageFor`): its title, one line (the module's own `about` text first), its example asks as tap-to-ask chips, its family and tier (`skills/families.json`), related skills (overlap pairs when `/skills/overlap.json` is served, else the same family) and a "try it" button. Wikipedia topics keep their route: a bare one-word topic ("what is time") stays the world's; a bare coined name ("what is family health") or a named one ("the time skill", "your forge") is Verse's. The zorbleflux ask itself is `skills/zorbleflux.js`. In the Verse itself family.
- **Match history (`lib/match-history.js`, `tools/match-history.test.mjs`, browser section "chess match history"; asked by Verse, 2026-10-18: "Expand the chess skill page to include a 'Match History' table that pulls from my recent game logs", and an Elo or win rate against the owner so the Games family feels competitive and real):** a chess, checkers or reversi game that ends on the board (checkmate, stalemate, a draw, a win on men or discs; a board thrown off mid-game is not a game) goes on `a2m.void.games.v1` (`STORES.games`, this device only, the last 100: when, vs, result from your side, moves, minutes). The about card of a game ("tell me about the chess skill", and straight: "chess match history", "my chess record", "my record against void") carries a Match History block: the summary line ("3 played · 2–1–0 · 67% · you ~1212, Verse ~1188 (estimated)": an Elo computed from the log, both from 1200 at K = 32, never kept) and the last ten games as a table, or "no games on this device yet". The chess module exports `about` (its line, plus the record when there is one), so the card prefers it. A bare "match history", "chess rules" and "how good are you at chess" are not taken.
- **The Zorbleflux throttle (`lib/motion-throttle.js`, `tools/motion-throttle.test.mjs`; asked by Verse, 2026-10-18):** while someone types in a stage card (an input, textarea, select or contenteditable has focus) or holds the pointer down on one, chaotic motion elsewhere (the Zorbleflux field, the living field cards on `lib/field-stage.js`) eases to 25% speed and comes back 0.7 s after they let go; the card in use is never slowed by itself. `<html data-motion-throttle="slow">` while it holds things back.
- **Stage pack D (asked by the owner, 2026-10-18; `skills/constellation-weaver.js`, `skills/forge-table.js`, `skills/night-ritual.js`, `skills/archive-of-one.js`, test `tools/stage-pack-d.test.mjs`):** "weave a constellation called night ops" joins the stage things with glowing lines under one saved `weave` object ("summon night ops" brings it back; recipe in `a2m.void.weaves.v1`); "forge these" / "forge the timer and the note" proposes a hybrid through `skills/merge.js` (now exporting `fetchWords`, which calls the existing `/api/merge`; no new route) with a one-tap "Put it on the stage"; "begin the night ritual" / "end the ritual" swaps to a reversible deep-void palette, starts a quiet drone only if Web Audio runs from the typed ask, and lays one night card (moon phase from `lib/astro.js`); "walk the archive" is a full-screen Next/Back gallery of every kept thing on the device (Escape closes, reduced motion respected), the kept-things cousin of `skills/showcase.js`.
- **Thinking-math stage cards (owner, 2026-10-18; test `tools/thinking-math.test.mjs`, browser section "thinking math"):** device-local, nothing sent anywhere, each a stage kind with a pure math core. `skills/eigenwork.js` ("factor my goal ...": steps tagged research/decision/craft/social, a progress bar per mode, "done" refused until every required mode is above zero). `skills/homotopy-edit.js` ("check my edit": old and new numbered claims; a claim whose premises moved by more than one with no path is a tear, an explicit path of one-premise hops joins it). `skills/plan-stability.js` ("is my plan stable": seeded +-5% nudges through a chain of steps give a Lyapunov-style exponent, positive is brittle). `skills/anneal-ambition.js` ("set my ambition temperature for X": hot, cooling, cold by date, no new proposal while cooling at the kept size until the list is cut).
- **Stage pack I (owner, 2026-10-18; `lib/stage-pack-i.js` pure core, test `tools/stage-pack-i.test.mjs`; all opt-in, no network, no model):** `skills/partial-answer.js` ("partial answer on <q>" / "tell me only what you know about <x>": known, "blocked on", next question from the person's own "known: a; b / missing: c" or a small local table; "fill the hole: <text>" fills one), `skills/draft-triad.js` ("three drafts of <text>": aggressive, conservative, weird by template, dismiss each, "keep this draft" keeps one), `skills/stale-work.js` ("note that I edited/decided <thing>" counters in `a2m.void.stale.v1`, "show stale work" for edits >= 5 and no decision: decide, defer to date, kill), `skills/certainty-budget.js` ("certainty budget [of N]": session budget of hard claims with a badge, only questions and options at 0, "refill certainty: <fact>"), `skills/interrupt-priority.js` ("set priority deep|normal|interruptible", "show deferred"; `maySuggest()` in the lib is the opt-in hook for soft suggestions).
- **Stage pack B (`skills/family-gravity.js`, `skills/deep-work.js`, `skills/soundscape.js`, `skills/stage-print.js`, `skills/miss-jobs.js`, `tools/stage-pack-b.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-18):** "turn on family gravity" / "gravity only notes" draws stage things of one family together with the field cards' `familyPull` (a loop that stops when settled, snaps under reduced motion, saves once); "shield everything except the notepad for 25 minutes" dims the rest to 15% under a focus timer until "lift the shield"; "add soft rain" / "add a low drone" / "quieter" / "only the drone" / "mute soundscape" layers Web Audio noise and oscillators (and `skills/list.js` no longer takes "add soft rain" as a list item); "print this stage as a one-page sheet" draws every thing on an A4 canvas, downloads the PNG and offers print; "turn today's misses into jobs" lists the top learnable misses (`lib/learn.js` `plan()`/`jobOf()`) as ready to queue, queued only by a tap with the owner key (a dry run without it).
- **Spectator reactions (`lib/reactions.js`, `functions/api/react.js`, `lib/reaction-bar.js`, test `tools/react.test.mjs`, playbook `docs/playbooks/open-endpoint-closed-list.md`; owner, 2026-10-18: "a fixed reaction list backed by a basic rate limit"):** six reactions (clap, fire, wow, laugh, heart, eyes) from a closed list under each "look what I made" step; a request carries only a reaction id and a ledger slug, the answer only ids and integer counts; 20 a minute and 200 a day per client (SHA-256 of address, UTC day and the SALT secret), 120 a minute per item, 5,000 a day in all; stored in D1 (`void_reactions`, `void_reaction_days`), off (503) without D1, SALT or the ledger. `LIMITS.react` in `lib/guard.js` is the outer layer.
- **The spectators card and the QR encoder (`skills/spectators.js` card, `lib/qr.js`, tests `tools/qr.test.mjs` and `tools/stage-pack-f.test.mjs`, browser suite "spectators card"; owner, 2026-10-18: "make sure its perfect i dont like it at all"):** "open the stage to spectators" is a plain share card (`data-card-plain` in `lib/card-frame.js`: no foil, rarity, art or stats; its saved picture is `lib/card-image.js` plainSvg): "Your stage is open", what the snapshot holds, the link in a field shortened in the middle with Copy link, a QR code made on the page (`lib/qr.js`, byte mode, versions 1 to 40, no network), Open in a new tab, Refresh snapshot and Close the stage. The link format is unchanged.
- **Tools pack 3 (owner, 2026-10-18; math and decisions items 41 to 60; shared stage bits `lib/stage-pack.js`, logic `lib/calc-more.js` and `lib/decide-cards.js`, test `tools/tools-pack3.test.mjs`, browser section "tools pack 3"):** all device-local, nothing sent, no rates looked up. `skills/calc-more.js` ("pixels to cm at 96 dpi", "5 cm to pixels at 300 dpi", "convert 100 usd to eur at 0.92" which uses only the rate typed, "dpi converter" and "currency converter with my own rate" as live pages). `skills/tip.js` now reads tax ("45 with 8% tax and 20% tip", "add 8% tax to 45") and an "84 dollar bill". `skills/decide-cards.js` seven stage cards: "rank pizza, tacos, sushi" (move up and down), "weighted pros and cons for moving" (weights 1 to 5, running verdict), "vote marks for pizza, tacos" (tally strokes struck in fives), "eliminate until one: a, b, c", "pairwise compare a, b, c" (every pair once, ranked by wins), "2x2 matrix for impact vs effort" and "must should could for the launch" (tap an item, tap a bucket). Percent change, split, ratio, aspect ratio, units, random number, dice, coin, pick and shuffle were already live in the page's answers.
- **Verse's own components route to the component, and ordinal dates count down (`void-live-deploy/lib/own-parts.js`, `void-live-deploy/skills/own-parts.js`, `tools/own-parts.test.mjs`; owner, 2026-10-18: "show me the site test card" fetched a Visa test card):** one table of Verse's parts (site test, growth, status, book, rule registry, merges) with their names; an ask that names one with "show me / open / where is" or a card/page/view/chapter word goes to the skill that shows it, ranked first (`skills/order/0003-own-parts`), leaving alone what that skill already takes. `countdown.js` `plainDate` and the page's calc read "january 1st", "the 1st of march", "jan 2nd" as numbers.
- **Growth velocity on the self card (`void-live-deploy/lib/self-blocks.js` `velocity`/`velocityHtml`, the sparkline drawer `void-live-deploy/lib/spark.js` `sparkSvg` (moved out of `skills/growth.js`, which re-exports it), tests `tools/self-blocks.test.mjs` and `tools/spark.test.mjs`, browser section "void asks batch 4"; asked by Verse, 2026-10-18: "a 'growth-velocity' sparkline on the self card that tracks the rate of 'Next' items moving to 'Shipped' over the last 7 days"):** at the top of "What got built", a 7-point line (one a day, from `/api/reflect` `follow.asked` by the day each ask was built; a tick a day, the latest day a dot, in Verse's violet) with a muted monospace label "N built this week · M today" ("nothing built this week" is a flat line); while the card is open a `void:stage-change` (a build landing) reads `/api/reflect` again and redraws it in place, at most once a minute, no timer. Friction over it (asked by Verse, 2026-10-18: "Add a 'friction' overlay to the growth-velocity sparkline that colors segments red when an open ask exceeds its expected resolution time"): `friction(asks, now)` in `lib/self-blocks.js` reads `/api/reflect`'s open asks; an ask is overdue once older than its expected resolution time (`OVERDUE_SMALL_DAYS` = 2 for one Verse called small, `OVERDUE_DAYS` = 7 otherwise), the segment into the day it crossed is red (`sparkSvg`'s `opts.segmentColors`, `sp-seg` lines over the polyline; without it the drawer's output is byte-identical), the label ends " · N overdue" in amber while any is overdue, and the title names both limits.
- **Self card layout (`void-live-deploy/lib/self-blocks.js`, test in `tools/card-share.test.mjs`, browser section "self card layout"; owner review, 2026-10-18):** the "What I think of myself" card as structured blocks (`selfSections`) drawn as real headings, one bullet per ask with dates beneath, Verse's words in the reading font and what got built in a monospace box; saved text keeps "- " bullets (`li[data-bullet]` in `textOf`).
- **Telemetry route (`skills/telemetry-route.js`, `lib/telemetry.js`, `tools/telemetry-route.test.mjs`, 2026-10-18, owner):** a pasted CI report, build log or status update is read as the builders' own telemetry (commits, pull requests, pass count, what is still failing) instead of being answered like a coding question; two taps plant the failures in the error garden or open the incident autopsy. Questions and short lines are left alone.
- **Live-check reverts (`.github/workflows/live-revert.yml`, 2026-10-22):** a failed live run on main opens one revert pull request (automerge; the full checks decide; it never pushes main).
- **Study before building (`tools/loop-study.mjs`, 2026-10-22):** when the loop picks a parity want, it files a passive REA study first (`tools/rea-study.mjs`); findings go to private memory only, never the repo.
- **Verse Rally and the card binder (`skills/rally.js`, `lib/rally-rules.js`, `lib/forge-duel.js` (F-235 Forge Duel, three lanes), `lib/card-deck.js`, `tools/rally.test.mjs`, `docs/CARD-GAME.md`)**: one card record for every card, "my cards" opens the binder, "play my cards" / "card battle" a best-of-three match with cards earned from real answers.
- **Book fit (`tools/book-fit.mjs`, 2026-10-21):** opens the book on the live site at many window sizes and zooms and checks every page fits its sheet with whole sentences.
- **Loop refine (`tools/loop-refine.mjs`, 2026-10-22):** the Self-Refine step of the loop: critique, mechanical rewrite, critique again; never adds claims.
- **Study engine (`tools/study-engine.mjs`, 2026-10-22):** Verse's own pure helpers on top of the passive REA capture (`tools/rea-study.mjs`); nothing copied, findings stay private.
- **File sorter (`void-live-deploy/skills/file-sorter.js`, 2026-10-22):** "sort these files: app.py, LICENSE, data.csv" groups file names by kind (licence, source code, settings, documents, data, images and binaries, unknown) with the reason and the rule's confidence; the rules come from the archived autosalvage repo's classifier (`archive/repos/autosalvage/evaluator.py`). Names only, nothing opened or uploaded.
- **One countdown (`skills/countdown.js` `countdownOf`, `skills/show-countdown.js`, 2026-10-22):** "show the countdown" asks are parsed by countdown and get countdown's own live card, so "show the countdown to christmas" counts to Christmas instead of New Year.
- **Honest self-talk (`lib/persona.js`, `skills/feelings.js`, 2026-10-22):** opinions yes, claimed feelings no: Verse says plainly it is a program, then gives its view and what it is working on.
- **Game Box (`skills/gamebox.js`, `lib/table-tools.js`, `lib/page-fit.js`, `tools/gamebox.test.mjs`)**: "game box", "games", "table tools" open every game (read from the Book) and the table tools: dice of any size with a 3D roll, coin, number picker, answer orb, card deck, timer, score keeper. The Landlord's Game rolls with the box dice. The quick chance answers link here.
- **Checks that look at the result, and the quote skill (mapped 2026-10-23):** `skills/quote.js` (a random quote from a public API, no key); `tools/card-quality.mjs` (asks a spread of card kinds on the live site at several window sizes and reports hidden or cut text, raw tags, empty bullets, missing card buttons); `tools/completeness.mjs` (every game card read against the basics a player expects; each miss becomes a general rule); `tools/expectations.mjs` (mines the features players expect from studied text, and generalizes every miss); `tools/glitch-titles.mjs` (animated glitch titles stay one evenly spaced word); `tools/lap-items.mjs` (the lap loop's inventory of everything a lap goes around); `tools/persona-playtest.mjs` (every game opened as four kinds of player, logging anything stuck, cut off or unlabelled); `tools/tower-gate.mjs` (the tower's head-to-head gate: Verse against one floor's live site, aspect by aspect). `tools/tower-verdict.mjs` (the scout's signed verdicts on aspects a script cannot measure, such as design polish and history; never overwrites a measured row). `tools/deploy-direct.mjs` (deploys a-to-mind.com from this machine with wrangler, no GitHub in the path; needs CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN and CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID in the environment and api.cloudflare.com reachable; `--dry-run` runs every step but the upload).
- **The proverb skill (`skills/proverb.js`, 2026-10-29):** "tell me a proverb" or "wise saying" shows a random proverb from a fixed list of 16 on a page, with no network call; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the words family with joke, quote and fact.
- **The morse skill (`skills/morse.js`, test `tools/morse.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** "morse code hello", "morse hello world", "translate to morse hello" or "encode in morse sos" show the text as Morse code on a page (dots and dashes, letters split by spaces, words by /), with no network call; "sos in morse" and "morse code for sos" stay with the util skill, and "morse code history" or "decode morse" are not this skill's; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the words family next to text-tools.
- **The ascii skill (`skills/ascii.js`, test `tools/ascii.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** "ascii for hello", "char codes of world", "unicode of é" or "what is the ascii of A" list each character of the text with its code point in decimal ("h: 104, e: 101, …") on a page, worked out on the device; "what is ascii", "ascii art" and "unicode table" are not this skill's; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the words family next to text-tools.
- **The leet skill (`skills/leet.js`, test `tools/leet.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** "leet hello", "1337 speak for world", "l33t hacker" or "convert cool to leet" swap a e i o s t l for 4 3 1 0 5 7 1 in the text (lower case) and show it on a page, worked out on the device; "leet code", "leet speak" alone and "what is leet" are not this skill's; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the words family next to text-tools.
- **The lorem skill (`skills/lorem.js`, test `tools/lorem.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** "lorem", "generate placeholder text", "give me some lorem" or "3 paragraphs of lorem" show one to five paragraphs of the classic Lorem ipsum text on a page, with no network call; "lorem ipsum", "placeholder text" and "3 paragraphs of lorem ipsum" stay with the util skill (asked first, and lorem checks `utilOf` to leave them), and "what is lorem ipsum" is not this skill's; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the words family next to text-tools.
- **The random skill (`skills/random.js`, test `tools/random.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** dice written the way players write them, "d20", "2d6", "3d8" or "d100": up to twenty dice, each and the total shown on a page, made on the device with `crypto.getRandomValues` (no modulo bias). Everything else about chance stays with the stage's own chance cards (`void.html` chanceOf: "roll a dice", "roll 3d6", "roll a d20", "pick a random number between 1 and 100", with the 3D die and the /random/ page), which skills are asked before, so this one leaves them (`tools/toolkit.test.mjs`); a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the games family next to coin and wheel.

- **The shuffle skill (`skills/shuffle.js`, test `tools/shuffle.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** a list written with spaces and named as a list, "shuffle these: apple banana cherry", "mix the list a b c d", "randomize my list red green blue" or "shuffle: north south east west", comes back in a new order on a page, shuffled on the device with `crypto.getRandomValues` (Fisher-Yates, no bias) and never stored. A list with commas or "and" ("shuffle red, green, blue") stays with the stage's own chance card (`void.html` chanceOf), which skills are asked before, so this one leaves it; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the games family next to random.
- **The color skill (`skills/color.js`, test `tools/color.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** "random color", "give me a color", "another colour" or "random hex" show one random hex colour as a swatch with its code, and "color palette" or "palette" five of them, made on the device with no network call; "what color is the sky", "color theory" and "paint a color" are not this skill's; a growth agent idea (the color palette row of `domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the making things family next to gradient.
- **The magic8 skill (`skills/magic8.js`, test `tools/magic8.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** "magic 8 ball", "ask the magic 8-ball" or "8 ball" show one of the classic twenty Magic 8-Ball answers on a page, picked on the device with no network call; "what is a magic 8 ball", "8 ball pool" and "magic number" are not this skill's; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the words family with joke, quote, fact and proverb.
- **The palette skill (`skills/palette.js`, test `tools/palette.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** a harmonious palette, five colors worked out from one base on the color wheel: "random palette", "another palette", "complementary palette", "pastel color palette", "triadic palette from #ff8800", "generate a color scheme" (analogous, complementary, triadic, split complementary, tetradic, monochrome, pastel). Each swatch with its hex, worked out on the device; five unrelated random colors ("color palette", "palette") stay with `skills/color.js`, which this one checks and leaves; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the making family next to color and gradient.
- **The uuid skill (`skills/uuid.js`, test `tools/uuid.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** "random uuid", "another uuid", "random guid" or "uuid please" show one version 4 UUID on a page, made on the device with `crypto.randomUUID` (crypto bytes, then Math.random, as fallbacks) and never stored; "generate a uuid" and "make a guid" stay with the util skill (asked first), and "what is a uuid" is not this skill's; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the numbers family next to util.
- **The password skill (`skills/password.js`, test `tools/password-skill.test.mjs`, 2026-10-29):** "random password", "another password", "password of 20 characters" or "password please" show one random password (8 to 64 characters, 16 by default) on a page, made on the device with `crypto.getRandomValues` and no character likelier than another, never stored or sent; "generate a password" and "make a secure password of 16 characters" stay with the util skill (asked first), and "forgot password" or "what is a password" are not this skill's; checking a password's strength is `skills/pwcheck.js`; a growth agent idea (`domains/growth-inbox.md`), in the numbers family next to util.
- **Is it…? (`skills/is-it.js`, `functions/is-it/[what].js`, test `tools/is-it.test.mjs`, browser suite "is it: is it christmas"; the tower's first rung, 2026-10-23):** "is it christmas?", "is it friday", "is today halloween", "is it a leap year", "is it christmas in french" answer one giant glowing YES or NO from the device's own clock (no network), in the browser's language, themed per day (glow and a particle burst), with when it next is, a live countdown, today's date, a tap ripple and a right-click flag stamp, snow on Christmas Eve to Boxing Day. `a-to-mind.com/is-it/<day>` is the same answer as a page that works with scripts off (Accept-Language, `?tz=`), `/is-it/<day>.rss` a daily feed. **The crew (`domains/void.crew.json`):** Verse leads; Claude Code first under it (builder), Gemini Argon the scout for the tower climb; public-safe fields only, track records from the growth ledger.
- **Coin (`skills/coin.js`, test `tools/coin.test.mjs`, browser suite "coin: flip a coin"; the tower's second rung, 2026-10-23):** "flip a coin", "heads or tails", "coin toss", "flip 5 coins", "flip a coin 70% heads", "flip a euro / a silver coin / a bitcoin", "toss a coin for who pays": a CSS 3D coin snaps onto its face in a third of a second (no fake wait), the side in big type, a quiet tally for this visit with the share of heads, Flip again or tap the coin, an optional soft ring made in the browser; crypto random, no network. void.html's built-in chance answers defer to it once skills load. **Faster fast checks:** `tools/checks.mjs` runs persona-playtest and perf3d only when the files they cover changed (`--all` or CI runs them anyway); `verify.yml` runs both on every merge to main.
- **Yes or no (`skills/yesno.js`, routing rank `9022`, family Games beside coin; added by the helper PR #1517, 2026-10-29):** "yes or no", "should I go", "yesno" puts one card that says Yes or No at random; "what is yes or no" and "yes and no" are near misses it leaves alone.
- **Everyone's tally (`functions/api/tally.js`, `lib/tally.js`, test `tools/tally.test.mjs`, browser suite "coin: everyone's flips"; owner's green light, 2026-10-23):** an open endpoint with a closed list (`docs/playbooks/open-endpoint-closed-list.md`): `POST {of:'coin', v:'heads'|'tails', n≤20}` adds to today's count, `GET ?of=coin` reads today and all time as integers; 30 a minute and 600 a day per hashed connection, 20,000 a day in all, no address kept; the coin card shows "Everyone today: 51% heads of 1,204 flips · all time 50.0% of 98,311", hidden until the first read works. **The crowd on the is-it card (`lib/share.js` `PUBLIC_ROOMS`, `publicRoom`, the `ripple`/`stamp`/`cursor.flag` shapes; `functions/api/share/[room].js`; `share-worker/index.js`; `lib/share-client.js`; browser suite "is it: everyone here"):** a public room on the relay anyone on Verse's pages may join (`/api/share/public-is-it`, 150 at once, the crowd's moves only, never a word): "Everyone here" on the card (on by itself 24–26 December) shows the others' flags at their pointers, their ripples and stamps, and how many are here; with the relay not switched on (no `SHARE` binding yet: `BASE.md`), tabs in one browser share it.
- **Side by side (`void-live-deploy/compare/index.html`):** `/compare/?floor=<id>` shows Verse's page for a floor of Versus beside the site it is measured against, with where each aspect stands; a site that refuses a frame is opened in a window on the other half of the screen. Linked from every floor on the Versus card; its header links back to Versus (/conquered/).
- **Battle avatars and the matchup (`void-live-deploy/lib/avatar.js`, `skills/tower.js` `vsHtml`/`matchupHtml`, test `tools/avatar.test.mjs`, 2026-10-25):** `faceSvg(host)` draws a site's cartoon fighter from a hash of its host (the same face every time; a fixed palette and fixed shapes, never a logo, favicon or brand colour), `verseFace()` Verse's atom-galaxy mark with a face in the core. The Versus card and `/compare/` head the current fight with a VS splash (the two faces across a slanted split, an energy bar each from the aspects won and lost, a 400 ms entrance on first paint, none under reduced motion) and under it one row per aspect with the gate's verdict and numbers from `tower.evidence.json`, when it was last measured and what comes next; beaten floors carry the site's small face.
- **The kit (`void-live-deploy/tools/index.html`):** `/tools/` lists every tool page by family with one line each, and its bench opens any number of them at once as panes on one screen (`?open=calculator,convert` keeps the set in the address). Every tool's "Use with" row links it as "All tools" (`lib/toolkit.js`); it opens offline (`sw.js` TOOLS).
- **The walker (`skills/walker.js`, `lib/walker.js`, test `tools/walker.test.mjs`, browser suite "walker"; owner, 2026-10-25: "drop him onto a solid floor so he actually walks across a stage instead of drifting through space"):** every cartoon card has a "Let it walk" button, and "let it walk", "make it walk", "walk around" take the newest cartoon card's painting: a 96 px figure on the stage root at bottom 24 px (the horizon) with an elliptical shadow on a faint baseline, pacing left and right at 60 px/s, bobbing 3 px a step, turning (flipped) at the edges, pausing now and then; reduced motion stands it still; `step(state, dtMs, width)` is pure and driven by requestAnimationFrame from the elapsed time. A tap shows a speech bubble for 4 s with a true line the page counted (the skill count from `skills/index.json`, sites surpassed from the tower files, what it learned last from the growth ledger, its own words from `self.json`), never an invented number. Kept in this browser (`a2m.void.walker.v1`: the picture, its subject, the ask); "stop walking" removes it and forgets it. The character is whatever cartoon the person drew: no sprite file, nothing hardcoded.
- **The backdrop (`skills/backdrop.js`, `lib/backdrop.js`, kind `backdrop` in `lib/art-rules.js` and `functions/api/card-art.js`, test `tools/backdrop.test.mjs`, browser suite "backdrop"; owner, 2026-10-25: "a static, beautifully lit 2D painted scene with fixed camera angles ... swap pre-rendered backdrops depending on the rung or theme you're working on (a retro server room, a cyberpunk rooftop, or an atmospheric study)"):** "set the stage to a retro server room", "backdrop: a cyberpunk rooftop", "stage: an atmospheric study", "paint a backdrop of ..." have the Art Forge paint the scene once (1536×864, a late-90s pre-rendered game background, one fixed camera, the floor in the lower quarter, no text, no people; kept per scene for ever, counted in the day's cap) and show it as a fixed layer behind every card (`#void-scene`), cover-fit and bottom-anchored so the walker's horizon stands on the painted floor; every card on the stage casts a soft shadow 18 px below it (a synced layer under the cards, never a filter on them: the coin and the game box keep their 3D). Kept in this browser (`a2m.void.backdrop.v1`); "clear the backdrop" brings the plain void back and still takes off a drawing backdrop through the page's own `void-backdrop` event. The atom-galaxy void stays the default: no scene loads unasked. Playbook `docs/playbooks/stage-layers.md`.
- **Chat fills Notes (owner, 2026-10-25: "make sure Verse knows how to fill out its own relevant pages for you just by chatting with it, similar to notion"; `skills/notes-fill.js`, pure `lib/chat-notes.js`, test `tools/chat-notes.test.mjs`, browser check `node tools/chat-fill-e2e.mjs`):** a sentence writes into the notes /notes/ keeps (`a2m.void.notes.v1`): make a note with headed sections and checklists, add to a note or under a heading, tick, untick, remove, tidy, summarise, rename, pin, tag, merge, split, find, delete, start from a template; English and German; every write keeps the earlier state as a note version and has Undo; a weak match asks Confirm, two matches ask which. Rule-based, no model.
- **Notes borrows Notion's core (owner, 2026-10-25: "on the way to surpassing Notion"; pure `lib/note-extras.js`, test `tools/note-extras.test.mjs`, browser check `node tools/notes-page-e2e.mjs`):** `[[wiki links]]` with a Links to / Linked from panel, a slash menu in the editor (heading, to-do, lists, table, divider, quote, code), a table bar (row, column, sort by the caret's column, remove), templates (meeting, trip, shopping, weekly; by chat too), pin and tags with an All / Pinned / #tag filter; chat drives them ("add a column Status to my X table", "sort my X table by Nights", "link my X note to Y", "what links to Y", "show my pinned notes").
- **Chat fills Calendar, Recipes and Generators (owner, 2026-10-25; skills `calendar-fill`, `recipes-fill`, `generators-fill`, pure `lib/chat-calendar.js`, `lib/chat-recipes.js`, `lib/chat-generators.js`, the shared card `lib/fill-card.js`, tests `tools/chat-calendar.test.mjs`, `tools/chat-recipes.test.mjs`, `tools/chat-generators.test.mjs`, browser check `node tools/chat-fill-pages-e2e.mjs`):** an add the stage's calendar card takes (`skills/calendar.js`) is also put into the Calendar page's store, said in words; `calendar-fill` changes, moves, deletes and answers "when is my dentist" there; `recipes-fill` saves and edits `mine` in the recipes store (the page lists My recipes and finds them by name); `generators-fill` keeps the make-your-own list and kept names (the page now remembers its own list). Same Undo, Confirm and which-one cards. Rule-based, on the device.
- **Notes: trash, find and replace, outline (from `domains/features/docs-and-notes.json` union_gaps; `lib/note-extras.js`):** Delete moves a note to a 30-day trash (Trash panel: Restore, Delete forever, Empty), Find and replace in the open note (Ctrl+H), the note's headings as an Outline row; chat: "show my trash", "restore my X note", "replace milk with oat milk in my shopping list", "outline of my X note"; deleting by chat goes to the trash too. Still open from that list: comments, co-editing, suggesting mode, images and embeds, databases with views, AI writing help, nested pages, more export formats, richer formatting, locked notes, sync, reminders on tasks, voice, web clipper.
- **PDF tools and Kanban (Versus floors `pdf` and `kanban`, owner, 2026-10-26: "everything the sites have"; `lib/pdf-tools.js` (engine over the vendored pdf-lib 1.17.1, `vendor/pdf-lib-1.17.1/`, MIT) + `lib/pdf-ui.js`, `lib/kanban.js` + `lib/kanban-ui.js`, one table `lib/tool-sites.js`, pages written by `tools/tool-site-pages.mjs`, tests `tools/pdf-tools.test.mjs`, `tools/kanban.test.mjs` and `tools/tool-sites.test.mjs`, browser check `node tools/heavy.mjs node tools/pdf-kanban-e2e.mjs`, features `domains/features/pdf.json` and `kanban.json`):** `/pdf/` merges, splits, reorders (thumbnails, drag, Alt+arrows), rotates, deletes and extracts pages, compresses (tidy, or pages redrawn as smaller pictures), images to PDF, PDF to images (ZIP), page numbers, watermark, crop and document info, all on the device; `/kanban/` has boards, columns, cards, labels, due days, checklists, comments, assignees, WIP limits, swimlanes, a card timer and Pomodoro, repeating cards, archive, filters, drag (mouse, touch grip) and Alt+arrow moves, JSON/CSV export, JSON and Trello import, a share link, print, English and German. Each site of both lanes has a page of its own (`LANE_PAGES` in `lib/tower.js`: `/pdf/merge/`, `/pdf/organize/`, `/pdf/toolbox/`, `/pdf/editor/`, `/pdf/suite/`, `/pdf/studio/`, `/kanban/simple/`, `/kanban/quick/`, `/kanban/teams/`, `/kanban/flow/`), built to that site's feature list and measured against it. Skills `skills/pdf-tools.js` and `skills/kanban-board.js` open the right page from the ask bar; `skills/pdf-card.js` (questions about a PDF) and `skills/task-board.js` (the Today stack) stay as they are.
- **Versus by size (owner, 2026-10-26: "rank every tower site by real traffic"; `tools/tower-rank.mjs`, `tools/tower-rank.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/tower-rank.yml`, `lib/tower-rank.js`, `void-live-deploy/tower.rank.json`, `domains/tower.order.json`, `docs/playbooks/tower-rank.md`):** the Tranco top-million rank of every Versus site (registrable-domain match), written monthly by a runner (the builders' sandboxes cannot reach tranco-list.eu); the Versus card shows a size on each row and a "By size" view (smallest first, unranked first, hand order from `domains/tower.order.json`), and `node tools/tower-rank.mjs --next` names the next 10 un-surpassed floors in that order. Data: Tranco, https://tranco-list.eu (Le Pochat et al., NDSS 2019).
- **Photo tools and the photos lane (`void-live-deploy/photos/index.html` and `photos/app.js`, the lane pages `photos/{squoosh,tinypng,exif,exifeditor,photopea,iloveimg}/index.html` written by `tools/photos-pages.mjs`, `lib/photo-edit.js`, `lib/photo-ask.js`, `skills/photo-tools.js`, tests `tools/photo-edit.test.mjs` and `tools/photos-e2e.mjs`, 2026-10-26):** Versus' Google Photos floor and its lane of small photo tools (exifeditor.io, exif.tools, tinypng.com, squoosh.app, photopea.com, iloveimg.com; feature map `domains/features/photos.json`). One engine on the device, nothing uploaded, English and German, offline: compress and convert to JPEG/PNG/WebP/AVIF (where the browser writes it) with a quality slider, a before and after slider and the size saved; PNG palettes of 256 to 16 colours written as indexed PNGs here (`encodeIndexedPng`, TinyPNG's trick); resize by pixels, percent or 1080/2048 px; crop with nine shapes; rotate and flip; brightness, contrast, saturation, blur, sharpen and 12 filters; text and freehand drawing; undo and redo with a memory cap; an EXIF, GPS, XMP, IPTC and PNG-text reader with a privacy sentence, metadata-only strip (same pixels) and JSON export; many photos as one zip (`zipStore`); choose, drop or paste. Layers, PSD, selections and AI edits are marked partial or missing in the features file. Each lane site has its own page (`LANE_PAGES` in `lib/tower.js`) that opens on that site's first tab; `skills/photo-tools.js` takes bare asks ("compress an image", "crop a photo", "convert png to webp"), `skills/imgfit.js` keeps sized asks, `skills/photo-scrub.js` keeps "strip the exif from a photo"; the ask bar of every tool page routes them to /photos/.
- **Free pictures (`lib/free-pics.js`, `tools/free-pics.test.mjs`, `tools/free-pics-probe.mjs`, `.github/workflows/free-pics-probe.yml`, `domains/free-pics.probe.json`, `docs/free-pictures.md`, `docs/playbooks/free-pics.md`, 2026-10-26):** owner: "dont forget to find free pics". One registry of free, properly licensed picture sources (Commons, Openverse, NASA, Library of Congress, Met, Art Institute of Chicago, Cleveland, Smithsonian, Europeana, Flickr, Pexels, Pixabay, Unsplash, Internet Archive) behind one `findPictures(subject, opts)`: closed subjects only, licence filter, rights heuristics, cap, cache, credit. `/api/daily-art` photo mode falls back to it; the weekly probe records what each source answered.
- **Page-to-page connections (owner, 2026-10-28: keep finding connections we haven't considered; `tools/handoff-scan.mjs`, `tools/handoff-types.json`, `docs/HANDOFF-GAPS.md`, checks `handoff-scan` and `handoff-e2e`, lap item `loop:handoffs`):** every page's inputs and outputs from its `verse-handoff` meta tag (or the legacy list, which only shrinks), the connections not built yet ranked by usefulness, and the built ones played end to end in a browser (`tools/handoff-e2e.mjs`). `--write` refreshes the gaps doc; `--hint /route/` suggests a tag from a page's controls.
- **Every page is real (owner, 2026-10-28: pages work as what they are, never pretend; `tools/real-pages.mjs`, checks `real-pages` and `real-pages-list`, lap item `loop:real-pages`):** one Playwright spec per route under `void-live-deploy/` that does the page's job the way a person would (types the input, reads the exact answer, copies, shares or downloads it), plus a sweep for dead buttons, page errors, a failing `/api` read, and mock or "coming soon" text presented as working (allowed only inside `[data-honest]`). `--list` proves every route has a spec; `--base local|live`, `--routes`, `--changed`, `--json`. Pages owned by other workers are tested and reported, never failing this check.
- **Gate badges (`lib/gate-badge.js`, test `tools/gate-badge.test.mjs`, shown by `skills/tower.js`, read by `lib/tower.js` and `tools/tower-self.mjs`):** every tower floor carries [Gate: Code], [Gate: Partner] or [Gate: License], and a regulated floor its stage (1 on-device prep and hand-off, 2 partner bridge, 3 direct license); the build queue takes Code work and Stage 1 only.
- **Parity inventories, site histories and honest obituaries (owner, 2026-10-28: "be sure our sites are actually better or have a plan to be better in every way"; `tools/parity.mjs`, `tools/parity-catalog.mjs`, `tools/parity.test.mjs`, `tools/site-history.mjs`, `void-live-deploy/lib/obituary.js`, `void-live-deploy/parity.json`, `void-live-deploy/history/<slug>.json`, `docs/PARITY/`, `docs/HISTORY/`):** every tower site gets a feature inventory (have, partial, missing, unreviewed, with evidence) and a cited history timeline (founding, milestones, ownership, redesigns, shutdowns; every fact with a source link, unknowns marked unknown). /obituaries/ shows each as beaten only with 0 gaps and the gate's 9 of 9, otherwise "in progress" with its match % and missing features, and opens the timeline in an expandable list. `tools/refresh.mjs` rebuilds them; `tools/checks.mjs` runs the test.
- **Catching up with Google Translate, Docs and Calendar (2026-10-28):** /translate/ has recently used languages, a language search, an on-screen keyboard for other scripts, a handwriting pad (read by the photo path of /api/translate), "Suggest an edit" that keeps your own correction for that text, a two-way conversation mode (speak, translate, read aloud, by turns) and a bookmarklet that translates a selection or the page (`lib/translate-extras.js`, `tools/translate-extras.test.mjs`). /notes/ keeps pictures (pasted, dropped or chosen; shrunk to 1200 px, stored as data pictures, exported as markdown), comments with replies and Resolve on selected text, and voice typing (`lib/notes.js`, `lib/note-extras.js`, `tools/note-extras.test.mjs`). /calendar/ adds a video call to an event (a private Jitsi Meet room, `videoLink` in `lib/calendar.js`; carried by .ics CONFERENCE/URL and invites, read back from Google and Zoom .ics) and a Print button. Browser checks: `node tools/test_void.mjs --only "page catch-up"`.
